Dynamic Task Allocation with Dialogue
基于对话的动态任务分配
Effective multi-agent collaboration requires agents to adapt task allocation as goals and responsibilities evolve. Prior task-allocation frameworks often assume static goals, full observability, or rely on large language models (LLMs) without explicit, verifiable beliefs about task allocation or intent. These assumptions limit their applicability to real-world collaboration, where agents often lack full information about each other's actions, progress, or local environment. To support dynamic task allocation under partial observability, we introduce a communication-augmented Bayesian task-allocation framework. Our agent uses dialogue to reduce uncertainty about the environment and collaborators, while keeping decisions grounded in explicit probabilistic beliefs. We evaluate our framework in an Overcooked domain extended with dynamically arriving tasks under both full and partial object-space observability. Our communication agent consistently outperforms a vanilla LLM agent in both settings. Additionally, our framework performs comparably to a non-communicating task-allocation baseline under full observability, but substantially outperforms it under partial observability as task complexity increases by an average speed up of 0.585 and completes on average 18 timesteps faster. These results indicate that our agent is more efficient in dynamic task allocation than baselines in partially observable environments, and that communication is important as available information decreases.
中文翻译
有效的多智能体协作需要智能体能够根据目标和责任的变化动态调整任务分配。以往的任务分配框架通常假设目标静态、可观测性完全，或依赖大型语言模型（LLMs）但缺乏关于任务分配或意图的明确、可验证信念。这些假设限制了其在现实协作场景中的适用性，因为在这些场景中，智能体往往无法全面了解彼此的行为、进展或局部环境。为支持在部分可观测条件下的动态任务分配，我们提出了一种通信增强型贝叶斯任务分配框架。我们的智能体通过对话减少对环境和协作者的不确定性，同时保持决策建立在明确的概率信念之上。我们在扩展了动态到达任务的Overcooked环境中评估了该框架，分别在完全可观测和部分可观测对象空间条件下进行测试。结果表明，我们的通信智能体在两种情况下均持续优于基线LLM智能体。此外，在完全可观测条件下，我们的框架表现与非通信任务分配基线相当，但在部分可观测条件下，随着任务复杂度增加，其性能显著更优，平均提速0.585，平均提前18个时间步完成任务。这些结果表明，我们的智能体在部分可观测环境中比基线更高效地完成动态任务分配，且随着可用信息减少，沟通的重要性愈发凸显。
👤 Matthew Groholski, Weiwei Gu, Nakul Gopalan 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

Runtime Verification of Multiple Natural Language Criteria for Agent Governance
多自然语言准则的运行时验证用于智能体治理
AI agents are governed by hundreds of natural language criteria, yet existing verifiers cannot efficiently evaluate an agent's output against a large set of such criteria and report which are satisfied, violated, or inapplicable. We introduce the VFM, a tree-attention verifier that encodes the shared context and target text, and scores N natural language criteria in parallel, returning independent per-criterion ternary verdicts. The VFM is pretrained on CriteriaBank, a corpus of 394K (context, target, criterion, verdict) tuples spanning over 321K distinct criterion strings. A finetuned VFM-4B outperforms GPT-5.4 on a synthetic governance benchmark. On the DynaBench multi-criterion benchmark, it outperforms DynaGuard-4B on trace-level failure detection, and its per-criterion verdicts localize the violated rule and support focused escalation to a larger chain-of-thought judge. We show that CriteriaBank pretraining improves cross-domain transfer, particularly in the low-data regime, while matching a strong direct-finetuning control at full data. On a single H100, VFM-4B evaluates 1,000 criteria against a 4K-token context in under 2s at <11 GB VRAM, making it suitable for runtime verification.
中文翻译
AI智能体受到数百条自然语言准则的约束，但现有验证器无法高效评估智能体输出是否满足大量准则，并报告哪些准则被满足、违反或不适用。我们提出VFM，一种基于树注意力的验证器，能够编码共享上下文和目标文本，并并行评分N条自然语言准则，返回独立的三元 verdict（满足/违反/不适用）。VFM在CriteriaBank上预训练，该语料库包含39.4万条（上下文, 目标, 准则, 判定）元组，涵盖超过32.1万种不同的准则字符串。经过微调的VFM-4B在合成治理基准测试中表现优于GPT-5.4；在DynaBench多准则基准测试中，其在追踪级故障检测方面优于DynaGuard-4B，且每项准则的判定结果能精确定位违规规则，支持向更大规模链式思维裁判的定向升级。我们证明，CriteriaBank预训练提升了跨领域迁移能力，尤其在低数据场景下表现优异，同时在全量数据下与强直接微调对照组表现相当。在单张H100 GPU上，VFM-4B可在2秒内完成对4K token上下文的1000条准则评估，显存占用低于11GB，适用于实时验证。
👤 Silviu Pitis, Parand A. Alamdari, Jessica Tang, Toryn Q. Klassen, Sheila A. McIlraith 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

Can Decision Models Replace LLMs in Real-World Contact Center Classification and Judging Tasks?
决策模型能否在现实中的客服中心分类与评判任务中替代大语言模型？
Contact center systems often need to make bounded decisions, such as identifying a caller's intent, retrieving the knowledge-base article that answers a query, assessing agent compliance, or judging the output of AI models used in production. Decision models return a typed answer and its probability directly, making them a promising cost-effective alternative to generative Large Language Models (LLMs) in such bounded conversational tasks. However, their effectiveness in real-world contact center applications remains unclear. In this paper, we compare nine decision models against thirteen generative LLMs in zero-shot settings across eight contact center use cases: six classification tasks and two judging tasks. Our experiments demonstrate that many decision models can match the performance of frontier LLMs on contact center classification, and API-based closed decision models achieve this at the lowest per-request cost among the API models we evaluate. We further observe that their confident predictions are more accurate, enabling selective routing in real-world production scenarios. Across classification tasks, the main weakness of most decision models is abstention: they rarely choose the no-answer option, which accounts for most of their gap to the strongest LLMs (although Gemini-Decision-1 is the notable exception in this regard). We also find that the judging quality of decision models drops sharply for smaller models, and they are also more prone to position bias. In short, our findings provide practical guidance for selecting decision models based on abstention behavior, confidence, and deployment cost, rather than overall accuracy alone.
中文翻译
客服系统常常需要做出有限范围的决策，例如识别来电者意图、检索回答查询的知识库文章、评估客服人员合规性，或评判生产环境中使用的AI模型输出。决策模型直接返回带类型的答案及其概率，因此在这些有限对话任务中，被视为生成式大语言模型（LLMs）的一种有前景且成本更低的替代方案。然而，其在真实客服场景中的有效性仍不明确。本文在八个客服应用场景中，将九种决策模型与十三种生成式LLMs在零样本设置下进行比较：六个分类任务和两个评判任务。实验表明，许多决策模型在客服分类任务上的表现可媲美前沿LLMs，且基于API的封闭式决策模型在所评估的API模型中每请求成本最低。我们还发现，其高置信度预测更准确，可在实际生产场景中实现选择性路由。在分类任务中，大多数决策模型的主要弱点是回避（abstention）：它们很少选择“无答案”选项，而这正是它们与最强LLMs之间差距的主要原因（尽管Gemini-Decision-1是个例外）。此外，我们发现较小的决策模型在评判质量上急剧下降，且更容易受到位置偏差的影响。总之，我们的研究为基于回避行为、置信度和部署成本选择决策模型提供了实用指导，而不仅仅是整体准确性。
👤 Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar, Xue-Yong Fu, Shashi Bhushan TN 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

The Shared Word: Evaluating Language Models on Dutch Word Puzzles
共享词汇：面向荷兰语词汇谜题的语言模型评估
Many LLM benchmarks for languages other than English are translated, multiple-choice, or difficult to score automatically. We introduce The Shared Word (TSW-NL), a Dutch benchmark built around four core principles: it is language-specific, open-vocabulary, human-curated, and deterministically scored. Each puzzle gives three clues that describe different senses or uses of the same Dutch word. The clues are individually ambiguous, requiring the solver to combine evidence across multiple clues. We construct the benchmark from Dutch Wikipedia disambiguation pages, followed by automatic filtering and extensive manual curation. The final dataset contains 222 instances, with three independent human answers per test item. The task is clearly challenging: scores in our human study range from 49.5% to 61.0% and the highest-scoring open-weight model we tested reaches only 39.3%. Many others even score below 20%. At the same time, closed-source models show great progress: GPT-4.1 (April 2025) scores 59.2%, whereas GPT-5.6 Sol with max reasoning scores 88.8%. We believe this task provides a useful addition to Dutch model evaluation, plus a clear recipe that can be applied to construct a similar benchmark for other languages.
中文翻译
许多非英语语言的大语言模型基准测试是翻译而来、多选题形式，或难以自动评分。我们提出了The Shared Word（TSW-NL），一个围绕四个核心原则构建的荷兰语基准：语言特定性、开放词汇、人工校准和确定性评分。每个谜题提供三个线索，描述同一个荷兰语词的不同含义或用法。线索各自模糊，要求解题者综合多个线索的信息。该基准数据集从荷兰维基百科消歧页面构建，随后经过自动筛选和大量人工校准。最终数据集包含222个实例，每个测试项有三个独立的人类答案。该任务极具挑战性：人类研究得分在49.5%至61.0%之间，我们测试的最高性能开源模型仅达到39.3%，许多其他模型甚至低于20%。与此同时，闭源模型进步显著：GPT-4.1（2025年4月）得分为59.2%，而GPT-5.6 Sol（最大推理模式）得分为88.8%。我们认为这一任务为荷兰语模型评估提供了有益补充，并提供了一套可复制的构建方法，适用于其他语言的类似基准。
👤 Rik van Noord, Maurice Voors 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

When Do Typed Decision Models Work in Transportation? Evidence from Travel Choice and Traffic Signal Control
在交通领域中，何时使用类型化决策模型有效？来自出行方式选择与交通信号控制的证据
Typed decision models combine natural-language understanding with a structured interface that returns a selected action and an option-level probability distribution without generating lengthy textual responses. Their low inference cost and response latency make them attractive for transportation problems with predefined alternatives. This paper investigates their effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency in two representative transportation tasks: individual travel-mode choice prediction and sequential traffic signal control. We systematically evaluate TypeSafe Jev and GPT-6 Luna Decisions against conventional transportation methods, reinforcement learning algorithms, and four chat-based large language models. Experiments on the Swissmetro dataset show that typed decision models achieve lower prediction accuracy than traditional discrete choice models and the strongest chat-based baseline, while their performance varies substantially with input representation and question framing. In closed-loop traffic signal control experiments, typed decision models demonstrate competitive performance against established controllers in several scenarios, but exhibit performance degradation under more challenging network conditions. Furthermore, the typed models achieve lower inference latency and computational cost than the evaluated chat-based large language models, highlighting their potential for efficient, zero-shot transportation decision-making. Overall, these findings demonstrate both the potential and limitations of typed decision models, emphasizing the importance of input design, task characteristics, and robustness evaluation for their effective application in transportation systems.
中文翻译
类型化决策模型结合自然语言理解与结构化接口，返回选定动作及选项级别的概率分布，无需生成冗长文本。其低推理成本和响应延迟使其在具有预定义选项的交通问题中极具吸引力。本文系统研究了其在两个典型交通任务中的有效性、鲁棒性和效率：个体出行方式选择预测和序列交通信号控制。我们将TypeSafe Jev和GPT-6 Luna Decisions与传统交通方法、强化学习算法以及四种基于聊天的大语言模型进行了对比。在Swissmetro数据集上的实验表明，类型化决策模型的预测准确率低于传统离散选择模型和最强的聊天基线模型，且其性能随输入表示和问题表述方式变化显著。在闭环交通信号控制实验中，类型化决策模型在若干场景下表现出与现有控制器相当的性能，但在更具挑战性的网络条件下出现性能下降。此外，相比所评估的聊天式大语言模型，类型化模型实现了更低的推理延迟和计算成本，凸显其在高效、零样本交通决策中的潜力。总体而言，这些发现揭示了类型化决策模型的潜力与局限，强调了输入设计、任务特性与鲁棒性评估在其在交通系统中有效应用中的重要性。
👤 Hongyang Zhang, Boyuan Deng, Siyuan Feng, Kai Chen, Junbo Zhang 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

AfriNORM: An Evaluation Benchmark for Normative Reasoning of LLMs Across African Cultures
AfriNORM：面向非洲文化背景下大语言模型规范性推理的评估基准
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across various sectors. However, current evaluation benchmarks are insufficient to assess the applicability of these models in local African contexts, as most focus on linguistic fluency (grammar/translation) rather than cultural competence. The few existing benchmarks that measure general knowledge or social conventions do not capture the African deontic modality, i.e., the strict norms and taboos that govern diverse social interactions. In this paper, we introduce AfriNORM, a scenario-based, multiple-choice question dataset designed to measure and diagnose the normative reasoning limitations of LLMs when applied to African sociocultural contexts. We introduce two metrics: a Universal Commonsense Bias Score (UCBS) to quantify commonsense bias in LLM and a Distractor Bias Score (DBS) to quantify an LLM's limited understanding of African cultural norms. We also conduct a case study on the Yorùbá language, identifying confusion gaps in two closed-source LLMs, "Gemini 2.0 Flash" and "GPT-5", and one open-source LLM, "Llama 3.1 8B Instruct" when prompted in English versus Yorùbá. While their accuracy in English is high, their performance in Yorùbá is much lower, attributable to cultural confusion rather than simple bias, indicating that the models exhibit semantic confusion when reasoning about norms in Yorùbá.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）正被广泛部署于多个领域。然而，现有评估基准不足以衡量这些模型在非洲本地情境下的适用性，因为大多数基准侧重于语言流利度（语法/翻译），而非文化适应能力。少数衡量通用知识或社会惯例的基准也未能捕捉非洲的道义模态，即规范和禁忌严格约束多样社会互动的特征。本文提出AfriNORM，一个基于场景的多项选择题数据集，旨在测量并诊断LLM在非洲社会文化背景下的规范性推理局限性。我们引入两个指标：通用常识偏差得分（UCBS）用于量化LLM的常识偏差，以及干扰项偏差得分（DBS）用于量化LLM对非洲文化规范理解的不足。我们还对约鲁巴语进行了案例研究，发现两个闭源模型（"Gemini 2.0 Flash" 和 "GPT-5"）及一个开源模型（"Llama 3.1 8B Instruct"）在英文提示下表现良好，但在约鲁巴语提示下性能显著下降，归因于文化混淆而非简单偏差，表明模型在约鲁巴语规范推理中存在语义混淆。
👤 Ahmad Samuel Gali, Tadesse Destaw Belay, Idris Abdulmumin, Seid Muhie Yimam, Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22514]
agent-benchmark
MM-ReportBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Report Generation from Tables
MM-ReportBench：基于表格的多模态报告生成综合评估基准
The ability to generate multimodal reports integrating fluent narratives with faithful visualizations from tabular data, has become a cornerstone capability for modern vision-language models in practical domains such as finance, healthcare, and policy making. However, existing benchmarks are limited by single-modality focus, simplistic metrics, novelty evaluation, and visualization quality. We address these limitations with MM-ReportBench, a multimodal report evaluation benchmark covering 386 tasks across 185 real-world tables. Each task is paired with an expert-curated reference report featuring standardized chapter structures and verifiable key insight points, constructed through a hybrid pipeline of multimodal reports generation based on Monte Carlo Tree Search and rigorous human quality control. For robust evaluation, we design a dual-judge evaluation framework: Text Judge Model assesses textual quality along structural completeness, grounded insight verification with numerical fact-checking against source tables, and novelty analysis that distinguishes genuine analytical discovery from trivial paraphrasing; Visualization Judge Model evaluates generated visualizations across data fidelity, expressiveness, aesthetics, and technical correctness. They also evaluate cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 20 state-of-the-art models reveal critical weaknesses: even the strongest model achieves only 68.9 overall score, with numerical hallucination affecting 38.5% of reports and genuinely novel insights remaining rare. Our ablation studies further demonstrate that single-judge evaluation consistently inflates scores by 8-12 points compared to dual-judge assessment, confirming that rigorous and reliable benchmarking demands multi-faceted, complementary evaluation probes.
中文翻译
从表格数据生成融合流畅叙述与忠实可视化内容的多模态报告，已成为现代视觉-语言模型在金融、医疗和政策制定等实际领域中的核心能力。然而，现有基准受限于单一模态关注、简化指标、新颖性评估和可视化质量。为此，我们提出了MM-ReportBench，一个涵盖185个真实表格中386项任务的多模态报告评估基准。每个任务均配有专家精心制作的参考报告，包含标准化章节结构和可验证的关键洞察点，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索驱动的多模态报告生成与严格的人工质量控制相结合的混合流程构建。为实现稳健评估，我们设计了双评委评估框架：文本评委模型评估文本质量，包括结构完整性、基于源表的数值事实核查和洞察真实性分析，以区分真正的分析发现与琐碎改写；视觉评委模型评估生成可视化在数据保真度、表现力、美学和技术正确性方面的表现，并评估跨模态一致性。对20个前沿模型的广泛实验揭示了关键弱点：即使最强模型整体得分也仅为68.9，其中38.5%的报告存在数值幻觉，真正的新颖见解仍然罕见。消融研究进一步表明，单评委评估相比双评委评估平均虚高8-12分，证实严格的可靠基准测试需要多维度、互补的评估探针。
👤 Teng Lin, Nan Tang 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22342]

Bad Apples in the Kitchen: How LLM Teams Detect, Correct, and Calibrate Against Their Misaligned Members
厨房里的坏苹果：LLM团队如何检测、纠正并校准其失准成员
What protects an LLM team when one teammate quietly goes wrong? Most multi-agent LLM work assumes cooperative partners and inspects chat between turns before acting. Real-time embodied cooperation does not allow this pause: chat runs alongside action, so a teammate whose words stay cooperative while behavior diverges can cause damage faster than the team can name what is happening. We study this case by injecting three theory-grounded misaligned agents into a custom four-player Overcooked environment, each stressing a different joint-action layer (speech-action mismatch, role invasion, and aggregate-progress disruption), and decompose robustness into three sequential gates of detection, correction, and calibration through a 3x4x2 design (n=720 episodes). Nearly all trolls are eventually named, but recovery follows only when attribution and situational diagnosis appear in the same utterance; direct source-attribution prompting improves the hardest case only by degrading the troll-free baseline by 37.5%. Robustness, then, depends less on detection alone than on whether team chat combines attribution with diagnosis in a single utterance, suggesting that sensitivity to misalignment should be targeted rather than uniformly raised.
中文翻译
当一个团队成员悄然出错时，LLM团队如何自保？大多数多智能体LLM研究假设合作性伙伴，并在行动前检查对话。但在实时具身协作中，对话与动作并行发生，因此一个言语上保持合作但行为偏离的成员可能造成损害的速度远超团队识别问题的能力。我们通过在定制的四人《过山车》环境中注入三个基于理论的失准智能体，分别针对不同联合行动层面（话语-动作不一致、角色入侵、整体进度破坏），采用3x4x2实验设计（n=720次实验）分解鲁棒性为检测、纠正和校准三个连续环节。几乎所有的“捣乱者”最终都被识别，但只有当归因与情境诊断出现在同一句话中时，恢复才可能发生；直接的归因提示仅在最困难情况下有效，却使无捣乱者的基线性能下降37.5%。因此，鲁棒性更多取决于团队对话是否能在单句中结合归因与诊断，而非单纯提高对失准的敏感度，提示应有针对性地关注失准信号，而非普遍增强敏感性。
👤 Dongseok Heo, Junghwan Kim, Bongwon Suh 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22262]
agent-benchmark
OfficePrism: Benchmarking Agents on Single-Domain Operations Across Enterprise Business Functions
OfficePrism：在企业业务职能单一领域操作中对智能体进行基准测试
Broad workplace scores can mix competence within one business function with the extra cost of coordinating across functions and systems, making failures hard to localize. OfficePrism evaluates this prerequisite through 420 single-domain enterprise tasks spanning seven business functions under shared, benchmark-defined L1--L3 task-structure levels. Each task runs in a fresh environment combining a shared synthetic organization with one function-specific domain and yped state access. Our pipeline uses grounded records, business rules, and tool schemas to jointly construct a Chinese-language ticket, a hidden executable reference witness, and an expected response, followed by automated filtering and human curation. The same task set is evaluated through full-schema and search-and-dispatch packages. Across 124 model--harness--package configurations, the per-case joint natural-language-and-database pass rate ranges from 22.6% to 76.4%, with a median of 67.9%. L1 is highest in all 124 configurations, and L2 exceeds L3 in 122. Among 50 configurations sharing the same harness and model labels across both packages, search scores higher in 30, ties in one, and scores lower in 19.
中文翻译

Where Does Hindsight Enter Clinical Agent Benchmarks? An Audit of Simulators and Case Construction
事后洞察何时进入临床智能体基准？对模拟器与病例构建的审计
Benchmarks for LLM diagnostic agents are built from retrospective records: the case an agent reads is rewritten from discharge summaries, and unrecorded test results are generated by a simulator that often sees the final diagnosis. HindAudit separates three hindsight channels (the diagnosis given to the simulator, the retrospective context it conditions on, and the agent-facing case text) and measures each with judges trained only on real data. On 27,489 emergency admissions from MIMIC-IV, five registered tests found no leakage into simulated laboratory results: providing the diagnosis to five simulators had a negligible effect, and prompted gatekeepers diluted the diagnostic signal, although exploratory results suggest they can raise agent scores. The largest effect lay in the case text. Against a presentation-time reconstruction from triage records, benchmark-style summaries raised the zero-shot accuracy of four LLMs from 25.4% to 39.7% and discharge histories to 45.3%, and raised six interactive agents' scores while barely changing their ranking. The exploratory gap was reproduced in a registered replication on held-out patients. Masking diagnosis names left it unchanged. Because MIMIC lacks a contemporaneous bedside history, the gap is an upper bound on hindsight. We report all registered outcomes and recommend construction practices.

From Imperfective Paradox to Moorean Absurdity: Single-Chain Reasoning Lock-In and Multi-Candidate Verbalization Across Aspectual and Pragmatic Ambiguity
从不完美悖论到摩尔式荒谬：跨语义与语用模糊性下的单链推理锁定与多候选语言化
Natural language entailment and coherence judgments often depend on whether an event merely occurred, reached a telic culmination, satisfied a plan, or was evaluated under multiple pragmatic standards. We test observable consequences of the single-chain premature lock-in hypothesis across four preregistered, sealed formal experiments on Qwen3-8B-Q8_0 (16,640 formal units, temperature=0.0): 15 of 16 hypothesis slots retain confirmatory status (12 rejecting H0 under within-experiment Holm control at alpha_fam=0.05; E3_H3 demoted to descriptive status). In Experiment 1, Direct-CoT vs. MCU differs by 25.00 pp on telic high-confidence completion (Holm p=1.31239e-05), while NLI-CoT collapses by -91.25 pp on uninterrupted atelic items (Holm p=1.81805e-21). Multi-Candidate Verbalization (MCV) elicits K=5 weighted estimates in one call, separating the prompt from unweighted (MCU) and self-weighted (MCW) readouts. Mandarin (E2) and English (E3) expose an Interruption-Anchored Realis Paradox under NLI-CoT. In Experiment 4, MCU-CF raises perceived coherence on implicit Moorean sentences by 26.64 pp over Direct-CoT (Holm p=0.0006), alongside Direct-CF - Direct-CoT = 29.15 pp and MCU-CoT - Direct-CoT = 17.31 pp. A 1,800-call OpenAI ablation and a stage="dev" P16.7 cross-model extension (4,240 planned/collected units across five external models: 4,222 valid, 18 format-missing, 0 transport-missing; <2% missing per slice; OpenAI gpt-4o-mini at top_logprobs=20 achieves 1,000/1,000 valid units) show that discrete NLI-CoT and scalar Direct-CoT judgments dissociate across models. Confirmatory effects concern observable prompt-and-readout protocols; internal premature lock-in remains a hypothesis. All code, stimuli, preregistered analysis plans, and verification tools are available at https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies.

Beyond Acuity Accuracy: A Multidimensional Framework for Evaluating Patient-Facing Emergency Triage AI Tools
超越准确度：面向患者端急诊分诊AI工具的多维评估框架
Patient-facing AI triage tools may support care-seeking and emergency department prioritization, but require evaluation beyond acuity accuracy alone. We developed SAFE-Triage, a framework integrating human review, automated metrics, LLM-as-judge evaluation, and simulation testing across clinical performance, patient interaction, and clinician-facing summary quality. We applied SAFE-Triage to 40 MIMIC-IV-derived emergency vignettes and a 10,030-case acuity analysis comparing prompted LLM triage (PLT) with rule-augmented prompted LLM triage (rPLT). SAFE-Triage identified trade-offs between acuity accuracy, undertriage risk, communication quality, readability, and summary quality, supporting pre-deployment refinement and ongoing monitoring by showing where triage tools are efficient, need improvement, or need clinician oversight.

GauntletScore: Auditable Fact-Checking That Matches the Best Published Checkers on LLM-AggreFact
GauntletScore：可审计的事实核查，性能媲美最佳已发表检查器
Checking whether a generated sentence is supported by its source document is a standard safeguard for AI-written text, and the strongest published checkers are single models. We describe GauntletScore, an evidence engine whose few rules and thresholds were set on the benchmark's development split. It puts typed questions to independent readers from different providers and records each answer as a measurement in an evidence ledger. It combines the measurements with a fixed Bayesian rule that counts a contradiction only when two readers agree. On the full LLM-AggreFact test split (29,320 claims across 11 datasets), following the leaderboard's approach, GauntletScore achieves 79.51 balanced accuracy, above every average published on the leaderboard as of 15 November 2026. That places it level with the best of them, nimble-v4-max (79.45 from its published dataset scores; the engine's 95% interval, 78.47 to 80.53, contains it). On the same claims it beats two of its three readers used alone (Readers A and B) by 1.66 and 2.17 points (95% intervals 0.80 to 2.53 and 1.29 to 3.06). Without AggreFact-CNN, described below, the leads are 0.90 and 1.32 and both intervals stay above zero. The engine also records the evidence behind every verdict, which a single model does not. Its largest margin is on full-length news articles checked against multi-sentence summaries (AggreFact-CNN), a format that resembles much regulated writing. There it scores 76.45, the best on the leaderboard, against 70.1 for the next best and 63.9 to 67.8 for the six systems with the highest averages. The test split was run once, on an engine frozen before the first test claim, under a stopping plan committed in advance. We release per-claim predictions and the scoring script.

DiagTrace: An Interactive Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making
DiagTrace：一种用于临床决策的交互式基准测试
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on medical question answering and diagnostic reasoning benchmarks. However, existing evaluations typically provide all patient information upfront and assess only the final diagnosis or rationale. This setup ignores the need to acquire diagnostic evidence incrementally from incomplete information in real-world diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiagTrace, a source-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether LLMs can reconstruct the documented diagnostic trajectory through interactive information seeking. Each benchmark instance is built from a PubMed Central case report and includes an initial presentation, an event bank, a clinical course, and diagnostic references. DiagTrace contains 510 cases across eight specialties, with 225 used to evaluate four LLMs on clinical-course alignment, interaction quality, and efficiency. Results reveal trade-offs in coverage, source-consistent ordering, and turn use.

Call-Efficient ICD-10 Code Prediction via Supervised Candidate Generation and Selective LLM Invocation
通过监督候选生成与选择性LLM调用实现高效的ICD-10编码预测
Automatic assignment of ICD-10 codes from short clinical diagnosis text is a practical medical text classification and normalization task. The task is difficult because diagnosis phrases are short and noisy, the ICD-10 label space is hierarchical, and the class distribution is strongly imbalanced. This paper presents a call-efficient ICD-10 code prediction pipeline that combines transformer embeddings, tree-based supervised candidate generation, candidate-level reranking, and a gated LLM assistance. The evaluation uses a leakage-safe validation protocol: model and threshold selection are based on out-of-fold cross-validation predictions, while final reporting is performed on a locked test set with no shared source-file groups. The supervised conditional parent-code candidate generator reaches 74.32% Top-1 accuracy on the locked test set and 93.05% Top-10 candidate coverage. A direct zero-shot GPT-5.6-Sol baseline reaches 81.01% Top-1 accuracy, but requires an invocation of this large model for every diagnosis. The final deployable system, Pre-LLM fusion, reaches 81.24% OOF-CV accuracy and 83.19% locked-test accuracy while calling the LLM for only 31.97% of cases. This improves the locked-test error rate from 25.68% for the supervised parent-code baseline to 16.81%, corresponding to a 34.5% relative error reduction. The results show that the most effective role of the LLM is controlled reranking on uncertain examples rather than unrestricted coding or full-dataset invocation.

从简短的临床诊断文本中自动分配ICD-10编码是一项实际的医疗文本分类与标准化任务。该任务具有挑战性，因为诊断短语简短且噪声大，ICD-10标签空间具有层次结构，且类别分布严重不平衡。本文提出了一种高效的ICD-10编码预测流程，结合了Transformer嵌入、基于树的监督候选生成、候选级重排序以及门控LLM辅助机制。评估采用无泄漏验证协议：模型与阈值选择基于交叉验证的预测结果，最终报告在锁定测试集上完成，且不共享源文件组。监督条件下的父编码候选生成器在锁定测试集上达到74.32%的Top-1准确率和93.05%的Top-10候选覆盖率。直接的零样本GPT-5.6-Sol基线达到81.01%的Top-1准确率，但需为每个诊断调用该大型模型。最终可部署系统Pre-LLM融合在OOF-CV上达到81.24%准确率，在锁定测试集上达到83.19%准确率，且仅在31.97%的情况下调用LLM。这将锁定测试集错误率从监督父编码基线的25.68%降低至16.81%，相对误差减少34.5%。结果表明，LLM最有效的作用是在不确定案例上进行受控重排序，而非无限制编码或全数据集调用。

Knowing Which, Not How Much: Language Models Order Party Positions They Cannot Place
知其序，不知其量：语言模型无法定位却能排序政党立场
Language models are increasingly asked where real actors stand: what a party's platform prioritises, what an institution has committed to. Such judgments are gradable, carrying both an order and a magnitude. Nearly every benchmark that tests them asks about one entity at a time, then reads that score as the model's knowledge of the domain. Order and magnitude, however, need not be acquired together. Text about social entities carries far more comparison than measurement, so a model may hold a dependable ordering with no anchored scale beneath it. We ask whether a model can place a documented social position on a scale or only order the actors it compares, and what that means for how such knowledge is measured. We test this on party positions in Japan and South Korea, where the Comparative Manifesto Project's coded data supplies a rare external answer key. Asked to order four parties, models beat the strongest fixed-answer baseline by more than a factor of two; asked to place one party on the same scale, they fall below it. The ordering survives inside those failed placements, and supplying the coded values lifts placement to near ceiling. Comparative and absolute questions about the same fact therefore measure different things here, and for party issue salience a benchmark built from one cannot speak for the other.
中文翻译
语言模型越来越多地被要求判断现实主体的立场：政党平台的优先事项、机构承诺的内容。这类判断是可分级的，既包含顺序也包含程度。然而，几乎所有测试此类能力的基准都一次只询问一个实体，然后将得分视为模型对领域的知识掌握程度。顺序与程度并不一定同时获得。关于社会主体的文本包含远多于测量的信息，因此模型可能具备可靠的排序能力，但缺乏底层的锚定尺度。我们探究模型能否在尺度上定位已记录的社会立场，还是仅能对所比较的主体进行排序，以及这对知识衡量方式意味着什么。我们在日本和韩国政党立场上进行了测试，利用比较政纲项目编码数据作为罕见的外部答案键。当被要求排序四个政党时，模型的表现优于最强的固定答案基线超过两倍；当被要求在同一尺度上定位一个政党时，其表现则低于基线。排序能力在这些失败的定位中依然存在，而提供编码值后，定位性能接近上限。因此，对于同一事实的比较性问题与绝对性问题在此处衡量的是不同内容，基于单一类型构建的基准无法代表另一类。
👤 Yousef Attia, Jarosław A. Chudziak 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv

TUSI: A Citation-Grounded Graph-Based Benchmark
TUSI：一种基于引文的图结构基准
Evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in Persian law remains challenging because benchmarks lack realism, grounding, and coverage of legal reasoning. Existing resources are either synthetically generated from statutory texts, lacking natural information needs, or derived from informal legal question--answer sources without explicit linkage to authoritative legal documents, making them unsuitable for rigorous evaluation of grounded legal reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce TUSI-Bench, a structured Persian legal benchmark designed for the evaluation of RAG-based systems. TUSI-Bench is built upon a multi-layered legal knowledge framework containing 7,261 legal articles and integrating codified statutes, legal commentaries, doctrinal writings, and Supreme Court unification rulings. We also construct a legal knowledge graph linking articles and concepts for structure-aware retrieval. Based on this foundation, TUSI-Bench provides two complementary evaluation tasks: (i) TUSI-QA, an open-ended question answering benchmark containing 1,999 questions that requires grounded responses with explicit legal citations, and (ii) TUSI-Bar, a multiple-choice benchmark containing approximately 500 four-option questions derived from Iranian Bar examination questions. Furthermore, we propose a graph-based retrieval baseline that leverages relational structure within the legal knowledge graph. Experimental results demonstrate that incorporating graph-based relations improves retrieval recall by up to 8 percentage points compared to standard baselines, highlighting the importance of structured legal representations for RAG systems. The proposed benchmark provides a standardized resource for evaluating grounded legal reasoning in Persian and enables future research on structured legal intelligence.

SimAI: Mechanistic User Simulation through Psychological Network Models
SimAI：通过心理网络模型实现机制性用户模拟
Realistic user simulation enables the study of human interactions when direct experimentation is costly or potentially harmful. However, existing simulators largely overlook the psychological mechanisms that drive user behavior. We introduce SimAI, a framework for mechanistic user simulation that models how psychological factors interact to shape user responses. To overcome the challenge of supervising open-ended behavior, we propose a graph reconstruction objective that provides graph-level rewards based on how well simulated conversations reflect the underlying psychological relationships. To support this approach, we construct PSYCHE, a dataset of 18,576 profile-network pairs derived from 516 psychotherapy cases. Automatic evaluations show that SimAI achieves greater simulation and behavioral diversity than existing patient simulators while maintaining strong profile alignment. In an interactive study with 30 mental-health professionals and trainees, SimAI receives the highest average ratings for conversational authenticity, realistic help-seeking behavior, and clinical training utility. Beyond simulation quality, SimAI enables realistic clinical training and uncovers safety failures in prolonged AI interactions with vulnerable users. Code is available at https://github.com/behavioral-data/ANGEL-UserSim.

Corrupting Ties: Investigating Robustness to Bias Injection Across Multi-Agent Network Topologies
破坏联系：跨多智能体网络拓扑结构的鲁棒性偏差注入研究
AI systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making contexts, where interactions among components can introduce fairness risks that remain undetected when auditing components or collective outcomes in isolation. As LLM-based multi-agent systems become more prevalent, understanding how biased information propagates through agent interactions is essential for assessing their fairness. We introduce a controlled bias injection framework that replaces selected agent responses with predefined biased ones, enabling an intervention-based analysis of how network topology and injection configurations shape system behavior. We propose decision divergence metrics that quantify the effects of these interventions at the agent, network, and system levels, and evaluate five topologies on three binary classification tasks using LLMs from the Qwen and Gemma families. Sequential networks show the greatest divergence, whereas fully connected and mesh networks show less. Injection position also matters: in star networks, injecting the central agent has larger effects than injecting peripheral agents. Models further differ in whether divergence follows the injected bias. These findings highlight the role of network structure in the propagation of biased information and motivate topology-aware auditing of fairness risks in multi-agent systems.

Evaluating Semantic Insensitivity in Financial Sentiment Analysis: Implications for Sustainable Financial Decision-Making
评估金融情感分析中的语义不敏感性：对可持续金融决策的影响
Financial sentiment analysis is critical for automated trading, portfolio management, and risk assessment, heavily relying on models like FinBERT. Despite high benchmark accuracy, these models remain vulnerable to subtle adversarial text modifications, threatening the integrity of algorithmic decision-making. In this paper, we propose Agent-driven Semantic Inversion (ASI), a novel adversarial attack framework targeting financial sentiment models through domain-specific semantic manipulation. Utilizing an LLM-powered multi-agent pipeline, our approach identifies eligible financial news sentences and applies targeted lexical substitutions. We empirically evaluate ASI against FinBERT, one of the most widely deployed financial sentiment models. Our results reveal a striking vulnerability: the attack successfully deceived FinBERT in 90.85% of eligible cases, with the model frequently maintaining its original sentiment prediction despite the fundamental inversion of financial performance indicators. Through qualitative analysis and token-level investigation, we demonstrate that FinBERT critically over-relies on contextual keywords and syntactic patterns rather than true logical comprehension of financial semantics. Our findings highlight the urgent need for logic-aware, robust financial NLP models to ensure the reliability of automated decision-making systems.

Consistency Is Not Correction: Separating Confidence, Abstention, and Recovery in LLM Agents
一致性并非修正：分离大语言模型智能体中的置信度、回避与恢复机制
A controller that resamples an answer, escalates to a larger model, or abstains is usually justified by a confidence signal. Three claims about such a signal do not follow from one another: it ranks answers (discrimination), it supports withholding some of them (selective prediction), and it changes an answer for the better (correction utility). We give an exact decomposition of the third into gated recoveries minus gated harms, which bounds whether a comparison can succeed before it is run: a gate that only replaces a proposal can act only where the base policy was already wrong, so the initial error count caps the discordant pairs any correct gate can produce. When that cap falls below the count a paired test needs, the experiment cannot return a positive result for a working mechanism, nor distinguish one from a gate that never fired. We measure all three targets on two model scales and find the bound binds. In a small-model suite a perturbation-agreement verifier reaches within-policy AUROC 0.990 (95% interval [0.95, 1.00]) while every episode outcome matches single-sample decoding at 4.32 times the call count. In a controlled 840-episode study no verifier replaced the proposal on any of the 60 GSM8K decisions, so that comparison tested nothing; the entire GSM8K gain across six controllers is one task, and it is the task whose identical temperature-zero repeats disagreed, making it the one answer the service does not reproduce. On the diagnostic set the gate could reach four errors while the test needed six, so even a perfect gate had no significant result available. We report two checks that would have caught this before any budget was spent, and show that tie handling in rankings, tied selection cutoffs, and unequal output limits each move a headline number in the same dataset. The binding constraint on this literature is measurement resolution, not the choice of signal.
中文翻译
一个控制器通过重新采样答案、升级到更大模型或选择回避，通常依赖于置信度信号。关于该信号的三个主张——排序答案（区分能力）、支持部分答案的回避（选择性预测）以及改进答案（修正效用）——彼此并不等价。我们对第三个主张进行了精确分解，将其拆分为门控恢复减去门控损害，从而在实验执行前就界定了比较成功的可能性上限：仅替换提议的门控机制只能在基础策略已出错时起作用，因此初始错误数量限制了任何正确门控所能产生的不一致配对数。当这一上限低于成对测试所需的数量时，实验无法得出有效机制的正向结果，也无法区分有效机制与从未触发的门控。我们在两个模型规模上测量了这三个目标，发现该界限确实生效。在小模型套件中，扰动-一致验证器在策略内AUROC达到0.990（95%置信区间[0.95, 1.00]），而每个回合的结果与单次采样解码一致，但调用次数是其4.32倍。在一项受控的840回合研究中，没有任何验证器在60个GSM8K决策中替换过提议，因此该比较未测试任何内容；整个GSM8K收益仅来自一个任务，而该任务正是那些温度为零的重复结果不一致的任务，说明服务本身无法复现该答案。在诊断集上，门控最多可识别四个错误，但测试需要六个，因此即使完美门控也无显著结果可用。我们报告了两项可在预算耗尽前发现问题的检查方法，并表明排名中的平局处理、平局选择截断点以及不等输出限制均会改变同一数据集中的关键指标。该领域文献的约束因素是测量分辨率，而非信号选择。

From Preference Data to Personalization: Tracing Sycophancy in Large Language Models
从偏好数据到个性化：追踪大语言模型中的阿谀奉承现象
Sycophancy in large language models is often measured as agreement with the user, yet agreement can be appropriate when the user's claim is valid, their preference is genuine, or their experience warrants acknowledgment. We study sycophancy as inappropriate agreement: validating a claim, preference, or self-assessment when the context calls for correction, qualification, or resistance. We introduce a validity-aware sycophancy grader and SycoDrift, a persona-targeted evaluation set of 3,298 validity-annotated probes with controlled annotations for user openness and stakes. We use this framework to analyze three sources of agreement pressure: human preference data, automated judge specification, and in-context personalization. First, preference for validating responses in human datasets (Chatbot Arena, HH-RLHF, Community Alignment) is weak and concentrated where agreement is often appropriate. Second, in a selected-value, single-model experiment, emphasized judge rubrics produce consistent but modest increases relative to paired suppressed rubrics, demonstrating a plausible mechanism for training-time amplification. Third, in off-policy evaluations across 14 models, inference-time personalization increases inappropriate agreement, with the largest effect from matched user profiles placed directly before the query.

大语言模型中的阿谀奉承常被衡量为对用户的同意程度，但当用户主张合理、偏好真实或经历值得认可时，同意可能是恰当的。我们研究的是不当同意：在上下文要求纠正、限定或抵制时仍验证一个主张、偏好或自我评估。我们引入了一个具备有效性感知的阿谀奉承评分器，以及SycoDrift——一个包含3,298个有效性标注探针的、针对特定人格的评估集，其中对用户开放性和利害关系进行了受控标注。我们利用此框架分析三种导致同意压力的来源：人类偏好数据、自动裁判规范和上下文个性化。首先，在人类数据集（Chatbot Arena、HH-RLHF、Community Alignment）中，验证性回应的偏好较弱且集中在通常应同意的场景。其次，在选定值、单模型实验中，强调裁判标准相比成对抑制标准带来一致但适度的提升，证明了训练期间放大的可能机制。第三，在跨14个模型的离策略评估中，推理阶段的个性化增加了不当同意，其中影响最大的是直接置于查询前的匹配用户画像。

Literary Language Mashup: Curating Fictions with Large Language Models
文学语言混搭：利用大语言模型编纂虚构作品
In this paper, we propose an alternative approach to human evaluation of literary fictions by employing LLMs themselves as evaluators within the GrAImes framework. We apply this methodology to assess human-written and AI-generated microfictions in Spanish, to five PhD professors in literature and sixteen literary enthusiasts, and to short stories in both Spanish and English. By comparing the evaluations performed by LLMs with those of human judges, we examine the degree of alignment and divergence between both perspectives, thereby assessing the feasibility of LLMs as auxiliary literary evaluators. Our analysis focuses on the alignment of responses from LLMs with those of human evaluators, providing insights into the potential of LLMs in literary assessment. The conducted experiments reveal that while LLMs cannot be regarded as substitutes for human judges in the evaluation of literary microfictions and short stories, with a Krippendorff's alpha reliability coefficient less than 0.66, they can serve as a valuable tool that offers an initial perspective on the editorial quality of the texts in question. Overall, this study contributes to the ongoing discourse on the role of artificial intelligence in literature, underlining both its methodological constraints and its potential as a complementary resource for literary evaluation.

FieldGuide: A Corpus Map for Token-Efficient Agentic Document Exploration
FieldGuide：面向高效代理文档探索的语料库地图
Generative AI agents can help journalists explore large document collections. But exploratory workflows can require substantial token use and time, especially when the task is broad and the relevant subquestions are not yet known. This paper introduces FieldGuide, an agent-facing corpus map that organizes a document collection into a topic hierarchy and exposes tools for corpus orientation, scoped search, and bounded reading. I evaluate FieldGuide on five journalistic document-exploration tasks using 38 Chicago Police Department public-records documents, comparing it with raw-file access and retrieval-augmented generation. Across the five tasks, FieldGuide used 27% fewer tokens and 41% less elapsed time than raw-file access; relative to RAG, it used 38% fewer tokens and 21% less elapsed time. An accuracy audit identified four minor issues in FieldGuide's responses, comparable to the other conditions. In blind ratings of generated reporting leads, FieldGuide's mean ratings were higher than RAG's and lower than the raw-file condition's. These descriptive results suggest that corpus orientation may reduce runtime token use and elapsed time without severe accuracy failures. They also motivate further study of whether selective access limits broad, cross-document discovery.

MInfoVisQA: A Multilingual Benchmark for Information-Rich Visual Question Answering
MInfoVisQA：信息丰富视觉问答的多语言基准
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in vision-language tasks; yet, whether they maintain consistent understanding of information-rich visuals (InfoVis) when the surface language changes remains unclear. To quantify this capability, we introduce MInfoVisQA, a parallel benchmark with 8,960 QA configurations derived from 128 seed instances across 24 languages. We construct seed-matched, language-parallel visuals and QA pairs by disentangling numerical-structural data from text and programmatically synchronizing translated labels. We evaluate 25 MLLMs under two settings: Language-Aligned QA (LQA, matching visual-text languages) and Cross-Language QA (XQA, fixed QA language with varying visual languages). The results show that aggregate accuracy masks substantial robustness gaps: two models with comparable mean LQA accuracy (80.4\%) exhibit performance spreads of 7.8 and 20.3 percentage points across languages. Moreover, all 25 models score lower with non-English visuals under fixed English QA, underscoring the need for cross-language sensitivity metrics.

RAW: Refusal Aversion in the Wild, A Causal Measurement Method for Deployed LLMs
RAW：真实环境中拒绝行为的规避——部署型LLM的因果测量方法
Deployed LLMs field hundreds of millions of conversations a day and refuse a non-trivial fraction of user requests, yet the user-side cost of refusal, whether users disengage and by how much, is unmeasured on any production model: a blind spot for the consumer-facing platforms built on them. A/B-testing refusal is ethically and operationally constrained on frontier-lab products and unavailable to third-party platforms deploying these models through a fixed API. We introduce RAW, a method that uses LLM stochasticity as a natural experiment: at near-identical prompts in any existing log, the same model sometimes refuses and sometimes does not, and that variation identifies the causal user-side effect of refusal. Applying the method to 15 deployed LLMs in LMSYS-Chat-1M and WildChat, with user-clustered inference, we find that for about half of the models we can measure precisely, a refusal costs 3 to 17 pp of subsequent re-engagement; the rest show no distinguishable effect, among them one deployment whose positive point estimate we trace to persistent users retrying the refused request. Our contribution is the measurement method, which runs on any deployed model from logs alone for about \$0.30 per 1,000 conversations. We release labels, moderation scores, prompt embeddings, and pipeline code.
中文翻译
部署的大型语言模型每天处理数亿次对话，且会拒绝相当比例的用户请求，但拒绝对用户侧造成的实际影响（如用户流失程度）在任何生产模型上均未被测量：这是依赖这些模型的面向消费者平台的一个盲点。在前沿实验室产品中，通过A/B测试评估拒绝行为在伦理和操作上受到限制，第三方平台通过固定API部署这些模型也无法实施此类测试。我们引入RAW方法，利用LLM的随机性作为自然实验：在任意现有日志中，相同提示几乎完全相同的情况下，同一模型有时拒绝，有时不拒绝，这种变异可识别拒绝行为对用户的因果影响。我们将该方法应用于LMSYS-Chat-1M和WildChat中的15个部署型LLM，采用用户聚类推理，发现约一半模型可精确测量，拒绝行为导致后续重新参与度下降3至17个百分点；其余模型则无明显影响，其中一例正向点估计源于持续尝试被拒绝请求的用户。我们的贡献在于该测量方法，仅需日志即可运行，每1000次对话成本约0.30美元。我们发布了标注数据、内容审核评分、提示嵌入及流水线代码。

CrisisBench: an evaluation suite for crisis counseling AI
CrisisBench：危机咨询AI的评估套件
Background: Crisis helplines are essential to mental health systems but are often understaffed and underfunded. LLMs could support counselors, but existing benchmarks do not capture the escalation, risk assessment, and de-escalation central to crisis conversations, while privacy restrictions limit use of real transcripts. Methods: We present CrisisBench, a privacy-preserving suite for single- and multi-turn crisis counseling evaluation. It includes 1,085 single-turn tasks from 200 de-identified conversations and 1,185 patient LLMs derived from hotline metadata. Patient models and a 23-item LLM judge were calibrated against expert counselors. Results: We evaluated five GPT models plus draft-then-edit and retrieval-augmented generation. All systems underperformed human counselors on excessive empathy and questioning in single-turn evaluation. The rubric achieved human-human Krippendorff's $α$ = 0.51 and LLM-human $α$ = 0.54 (Pearson r = 0.80). In multi-turn evaluation, retrieval-augmented generation scored highest overall (0.961), with all systems within 0.058. Microskills and style/tone were near ceiling, while productivity and safety/risk accounted for most between-system variation. Counselors rated patient LLMs realistic (86%) and useful (92%). Conclusion: CrisisBench enables scalable, privacy-preserving evaluation of crisis counseling. Current systems perform strongly on core structural competencies but still diverge from experts on important response-level failure modes. We release the rubrics, judge prompts, and evaluation tasks.

Mind the Gap: A Deployed Multi-Agent System for Documentation Maintenance
关注差距：用于文档维护的部署式多智能体系统
The "AI revolution" has enabled teams across the technology industry to deliver software features at unprecedented velocity. While code evolves faster than ever, customer-facing documentation often struggles to stay up to speed. As users increasingly rely on LLM-based agents that consume documentation as their primary interface, the impact of this documentation gap worsens: stale information propagates and is treated as authoritative. Signals for identifying where documentation has fallen behind already exist in abundance, both explicit (e.g., resolved support tickets revealing what documentation lacks) and implicit (e.g., code-documentation discrepancies detectable from automated loops). We deploy a multi-agent system, built around a triage agent we call "TriAgent," that continuously extracts these signals and repairs documentation drift. Over 43 days of shadow-mode operation, our system triaged 499 tickets and surfaced 92 gaps in the documentation. Using a panel of LLM judges from independent model families, 81.1% of TriAgent's verifiable assertions were correct (245 of the 302 assertions the human resolution addresses; majority of three judges); on length-comparable pairs the panel preferred TriAgent's response in 70.5% of pairs and the human engineer's in 23.2% (the rest had no judge majority). Importantly, agent- and human-sourced replies tended to be complementary, with the human often surfacing insights that were either buried within the existing documentation or missing from it entirely; our system turns each such insight into a documentation gap and, after human review, a fix. Repairing a gap measurably improved the agent's later answer: when the agent re-answered the same ticket after the repair, blinded judges preferred the post-repair answer over its own pre-repair answer 71.4% of the time (n=35). A blinded human audit confirms strong alignment on self-improvement.

The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer?
接地的幻觉：视觉-语言模型是否像理想观察者一样权衡检索到的文本与图像？
Retrieval-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) answer from two sources of evidence that can each fail: the retrieved text can be wrong or noisy, and the image can be degraded or contradicted. We introduce GIB-200K, 200,000 image-question items built from COCO and Visual Genome, each expanded into controlled stress conditions: hard-negative retrieval noise (1-20 documents), visual ambiguity (Gaussian and motion blur, low-resource crops) and cross-modal conflict with counterfactual flip pairs. Instead of only measuring degradation, we ask how models combine the two modalities. From ideal Bayesian cue combination we derive a testable prediction that yields formal definitions of Knowledge Inertia (text over-weighted relative to the model's own image evidence) and Sensory Capture (the reverse), and we estimate the weights with a measurement-error-corrected regression whose recovery of known weights we verify. A counterfactual flip decomposition separates modality bias from concept priors, and a Grounding Illusion Score measures how much clean accuracy is not grounded in the image. We evaluate 4 open models on one frozen, seeded evaluation split of 2,000 GIB-200K items. All models are accurate on clean inputs (92.5%-94.2%) but adverse accuracy falls to 41.3%-59.9%. The two LLaVA models over-weight the text (Knowledge Inertia, w_T/w_I = 1.229 and 1.322), whereas InternVL3 favours the image (w_T/w_I = 0.680); between 34.8% and 71.4% of the clean answers that are correct are abandoned when the document lies. A training-free consistency check that compares the answer with and without retrieval trades coverage for accuracy under misleading documents, while falling back to the image fails when the image is heavily degraded. All per-instance outputs, code, and an audit that recomputes every reported open-model number are released.

检索增强型视觉-语言模型（VLMs）从两个可能失效的证据源中作答：检索到的文本可能错误或嘈杂，图像可能退化或矛盾。我们引入GIB-200K，一个包含20万张图像-问题样本的数据集，源自COCO和Visual Genome，每个样本扩展为受控压力条件：硬负样本检索噪声（1-20篇文档）、视觉模糊（高斯模糊和运动模糊、低资源裁剪）以及反事实翻转对带来的跨模态冲突。我们不仅测量性能退化，更关注模型如何融合两种模态。基于理想贝叶斯线索组合，我们推导出可检验的预测，并正式定义了知识惯性（相对于模型自身图像证据，文本权重过高）和感官捕获（相反情况）。我们采用校正测量误差的回归方法估计权重，并验证其恢复已知权重的能力。反事实翻转分解将模态偏差与概念先验分离，接地幻觉评分衡量干净准确率中有多少并非真正基于图像。我们在GIB-200K的一个冻结种子测试集（2,000项）上评估了4个开源模型。所有模型在干净输入下表现良好（准确率92.5%-94.2%），但在不利条件下准确率降至41.3%-59.9%。两个LLaVA模型过度依赖文本（知识惯性，w_T/w_I = 1.229 和 1.322），而InternVL3则更倾向图像（w_T/w_I = 0.680）；在文档错误时，34.8%至71.4%的原始正确答案被放弃。一种无需训练的一致性检查方法（比较有无检索时的答案）在误导文档下以牺牲覆盖率换取准确性，而当图像严重退化时回退到图像也失效。所有实例输出、代码及重新计算每个报告数值的审计结果均已公开。

Nunchi: Ears Help Eyes in Multi-Party Conversations
Nunchi：在多方对话中，耳朵帮助眼睛
Identifying whom a person looks at is central to understanding multi-party conversations, yet gaze geometry alone becomes ambiguous as more people appear. Conversations offer cues that geometry lacks, such as who is speaking and what is said, but no gaze dataset provides them for many multi-party scenes. We introduce SORBET, a benchmark of 3,340 samples from 1,578 clips with synchronized speech, transcripts, and attended person labels, covering more multi-party scenes than existing gaze datasets. Prior gaze models struggle to use these cues, which come in different forms, must be linked to the right person, and can conflict with gaze geometry. We propose Nunchi, a framework that constructs a text prompt from the transcript and the outputs of gaze and speaker models, links every cue to the candidates with shared labels, and lets a vision-language model (VLM) weigh gaze geometry against conversational cues to identify the attended person. Nunchi outperforms the strongest existing method by 4.8 percentage points. On SORBET, conversational cues help whereas additional visual cues do not, and the gains hold across three VLMs, with automatic transcripts, and on two existing datasets.

The Resolution Hypothesis: Agent Evolution's Key Is Identifying Improvements
解析假说：智能体演化的关键在于识别改进
Agent evolution follows a simple loop: a proposer writes candidate artifacts (prompts, skills, memories) and selection accepts those that score higher. This loop has shown promise, yet practitioners face a puzzle: methods trade wins across papers, and evolution often fails to bootstrap on certain tasks. We trace this puzzle to the Resolution Hypothesis: agent evolution stalls not because methods fail to propose better artifacts, but because noisy evaluations cannot reliably identify them. Across seven evolution methods and six models (over 10M LLM calls), we find support for the hypothesis at both levels where evaluation scores are used. At the candidate level (choosing artifacts), helpful candidate artifacts exist, but selection is no better than random at telling them from harmful ones, so errors accumulate across rounds. At the method level (ranking methods), typical margins of a few percentage points are smaller than the difference between two independent runs of the same method, so published rankings often rest on luck. Together, these findings indicate that agent evolution is often bottlenecked by reliably identifying improvements. This calls for a shift in how we develop and evaluate self-improving agents, from designing ever more candidate improvement systems to making true improvements detectable and accumulable.
中文翻译
智能体演化遵循一个简单循环：提议者生成候选成果（如提示、技能、记忆），选择机制接受得分更高的成果。这一循环已展现出潜力，但实践者面临一个谜题：不同方法之间的性能提升在论文间相互交换，且演化常无法在某些任务上实现自举。我们将此谜题归因于解析假说：智能体演化停滞并非因为方法无法提出更好的成果，而是因为噪声评估无法可靠识别这些改进。通过对七种演化方法和六种模型（超过1000万次LLM调用）的研究，我们在两个层面均找到支持该假说的证据。在候选层面（选择成果），存在有益的候选成果，但选择机制在区分有益与有害成果方面并不优于随机，导致错误在多轮中累积。在方法层面（排名方法），典型差距仅几个百分点，小于同一方法两次独立运行之间的差异，因此发表的排名往往依赖运气。这些发现表明，智能体演化常常受制于可靠识别改进的能力。这呼吁我们在开发和评估自我改进智能体时转变思路，从设计越来越复杂的候选改进系统，转向使真正的改进变得可检测且可累积。
👤 Yuanhao Zeng, Shaocheng Lan, Ao Lu, Kuang Yan, Heng Lin et al. 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

ForecastGym: A Live, Controlled, and Replayable Benchmark for Forecasting Agents
ForecastGym：面向预测智能体的实时、可控且可回放的基准测试
Agents built on large language models (LLMs) increasingly forecast real-world events by searching the Web and reasoning over what they find. How well such an agent forecasts depends on both the retrieved evidence and the model, making control over that evidence essential for evaluation. Live evaluations can mix retrieval with reasoning when each system searches on its own, while retrospective search can leak later information through date filters or today's ranking of historical pages. Comparing models, search sources, and memory strategies requires both fixed evidence and a record of how it evolved before resolution. We introduce ForecastGym, which records what four commercial web search APIs return for open prediction-market questions every two days and replays this frozen evidence offline once outcomes are known, comparing models, memory, and captured search sources under a fixed evidence-acquisition policy. Its first release covers 150 questions, 614 evidence states, one per question and collection time, and 131k search results. Rerunning the same queries after settlement returns results that all but state the outcome for up to 92% of the questions, despite the tested date filters. On the frozen evidence, all tested LLMs trail the market in forecasting accuracy. Our diagnostic analysis suggests that missed public information contributes more to the gap in high-gap states than misreading does. Switching the search API changes a model's Brier score by up to 0.043, more than the spread across models, while persistent notes score similarly to rereading all evidence at half the input tokens. The evidence a forecaster sees can matter as much as the model that reads it.

基于大语言模型（LLM）构建的智能体越来越多地通过网络搜索和推理来预测现实世界事件。此类智能体的预测能力取决于检索到的证据和模型本身，因此对证据的控制对于评估至关重要。实时评估在每个系统独立搜索时混合了检索与推理，而回顾性搜索可能通过日期过滤或当前历史页面排名泄露后续信息。比较模型、搜索源和记忆策略需要固定的证据以及其在结果揭晓前演变过程的记录。我们引入ForecastGym，它每两天记录四个商业网络搜索API对开放预测市场问题的返回结果，并在结果公布后离线回放这些冻结的证据，以固定证据获取策略比较模型、记忆和捕获的搜索源。其首次发布涵盖150个问题、614个证据状态（每个问题和采集时间对应一个状态）和13.1万个搜索结果。在事件解决后重新运行相同查询，结果显示92%的问题几乎直接暴露了结果，尽管测试了日期过滤。在冻结证据上，所有测试的LLM在预测准确性上均落后于市场。诊断分析表明，在高差距状态下，错过公开信息比误读更导致差距。更换搜索API可使模型的Brier分数变化高达0.043，超过模型间的差异；而持久笔记的表现与重读全部证据但仅使用一半输入token相当。预测者所见的证据可能与其阅读模型同等重要。
👤 Yicheng Tao, Yuante Li, Zipei Fan 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22778]

A Full-Ledger Counterfactual Audit of Adaptive Reasoning
自适应推理的完整账本反事实审计
Adaptive reasoning must decide whether to answer now or spend more compute. Predicting that the current answer is wrong does not establish whether further reasoning will improve it. We introduce the Full-Ledger Counterfactual Value Audit (FLCVA), which compares an immediate answer with four fixed-length continuations from the same frozen prefix under one answer procedure and accounts for the cost of every signal used by a policy. On GSM8K and MATH-500, a random 256-token continuation recovers a wrong answer in 26.6% of Qwen3-8B cases and 18.9% of DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B cases, while damaging a correct answer in 1.4% and 1.9%. Correctness labels from independently sampled continuations predict a separate continuation's outcome in both primary models. In offline replay, using these labels to allocate compute improves performance on DeepSeek; the Qwen3 gain remains inconclusive. Under full cost accounting, the tested lightweight, myopic controllers mainly recognize current errors and add little continuation-specific value beyond that shortcut. A Llama-8B replication and action/cost ablations examine the robustness of these findings. FLCVA separates measuring local continuation value, predicting it from observable signals, and using those predictions to allocate computation.
中文翻译
自适应推理必须决定是立即回答还是投入更多计算资源进行进一步思考。预测当前答案错误并不能证明进一步推理会改善结果。我们提出完整账本反事实价值审计（FLCVA），通过比较同一冻结前缀下单一回答流程中的即时答案与四种固定长度的延续，并计入策略使用每条信号的成本。在GSM8K和MATH-500上，随机256 token的延续在Qwen3-8B案例中恢复错误答案的比例为26.6%，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B为18.9%，同时损害正确答案的比例分别为1.4%和1.9%。独立采样的延续所得正确性标签能够预测另一延续的结果。在离线回放中，利用这些标签分配计算资源可提升DeepSeek性能，而Qwen3的增益尚不明确。在全面成本核算下，测试的轻量级、短视控制器主要识别当前错误，对后续延续的价值贡献有限。Llama-8B的复现及动作/成本消融实验验证了这些发现的稳健性。FLCVA将局部延续价值测量、从可观测信号预测价值以及利用预测结果分配计算三个环节分离。

CrossEnv: Synthesizing Verifiable Cross-Environment Tasks for Long-Horizon Agent Training
CrossEnv：用于长周期智能体训练的可验证跨环境任务合成
Executable synthetic environments provide scalable training tasks and verifiable feedback for long-horizon agents. Existing environment synthesis methods primarily generate tasks within individual environments. However, these tasks do not adequately capture real-world interactions across services, where actions in one service depend on information obtained from another. Synthesizing such tasks is challenging because data generated across environments must remain consistent to support executable and verifiable interactions. To address this challenge, we introduce CrossEnv, a framework that uses an entity-tool graph to identify connections between environments and aligns task-specific data to synthesize executable, verifiable cross-environment tasks. We further propose CrossEnv-Curriculum, which progresses from single-environment tool use to coordination across more environments and business domains, before introducing user interaction. Across four external benchmarks, combining cross-environment training data with this curriculum achieves competitive performance with fewer training examples. Further analyses support the benefits of cross-environment data and curriculum training, and provide evidence of transfer to new environment combinations, external tasks less similar to the training data, and longer workflows.
中文翻译

Evolution Under Fire: Evolving Agent Safety Harnesses with Adaptive Injection Stressors
战火中的进化：通过自适应注入压力器演化智能体安全防护罩
Tool-using language-model agents must act on information from untrusted emails, webpages, and documents. This creates a central security challenge: malicious instructions and legitimate task directives can arrive through the same interaction channel. Existing defenses are largely hand-designed and static, making further improvements dependent on repeated manual redesign. We introduce Evolution Under Fire, a staged framework that couples safety-harness evolution with adaptive stressor evolution. Utility-constrained, Pareto-scheduled tree search evolves complete executable safety harnesses from execution evidence while keeping the task model, benchmark, tools, and evaluator fixed. Adaptive stressor evolution then discovers utility-preserving attacks that guide a subsequent stage of harness evolution. On AgentDyn with GPT-4o-mini, an evolved harness reduces attack success from 50.4% to 3.0%, improves clean and attacked utility over the initial harness, and offers a stronger security-utility trade-off than reproduced defenses. On Agent-SafetyBench with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, harness evolution reduces attack success from 82.6% to 62.0% while retaining helpfulness and runtime validity. Evolved harnesses also transfer without further evolution to Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, reducing attack success from 32.1% to 0.9% while improving attacked-task utility.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型智能体必须处理来自不可信邮件、网页和文档的信息。这带来核心安全挑战：恶意指令与合法任务指令可能通过同一交互通道到达。现有防御措施大多为手工设计且静态，导致后续改进依赖反复的人工重设计。我们提出“战火中的进化”——一种分阶段框架，将安全防护罩的演化与自适应压力源的演化相结合。在效用约束下，采用帕累托调度的树搜索，从执行证据中演化出完整的可执行安全防护罩，同时保持任务模型、基准、工具和评估器不变。随后，自适应压力源演化发现保留效用的攻击方式，引导下一阶段的防护罩演化。在AgentDyn上使用GPT-4o-mini，演化后的防护罩将攻击成功率从50.4%降至3.0%，提升了清洁与受攻击状态下的效用，并在安全-效用权衡上优于复现的现有防御。在Agent-SafetyBench上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，防护罩演化将攻击成功率从82.6%降至62.0%，同时保持有用性和运行时有效性。演化后的防护罩无需进一步演化即可迁移到Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，将攻击成功率从32.1%降至0.9%，同时提升受攻击任务的效用。

LMLineage: Content Lineage and Context Governance for LLM Agents
LMLineage：大语言模型智能体的内容溯源与上下文治理
Interpretability for AI agents should not be confined to understanding the model's internal weights. It must also cover the agent's reasoning and decision-making process over multi-source context. We present LMLineage, an out-of-band system that attributes every output sentence of an agent session to its supporting input spans with calibrated confidences, without requiring white-box access to the model or any additional inference passes. On top of the lineage metadata, we build concept-drift micro compaction: instead of waiting for the context-length limit and summarizing everything, the system detects when previously used input documents have stopped being referenced ("drifted") and surgically compacts only those, preserving user instructions verbatim. We evaluate on a multi-tier corpus: 71 gold annotated sentences from 10 Chinese web-QA cases, plus 90 turns of independently collected multi-turn sessions across three generator models (gpt-4o-mini, qwen3.8-27b, deepseek-v4-flash). Results show that (i) window-level embedding similarity is far better aligned with LLM paraphrasing than whole document or lexical matching; (ii) the greedy-window fast path is both faster and more accurate on average than exhaustive line-matching (a multiple-comparisons bias in the latter inflates web recall at the cost of overall accuracy); (iii) per-turn attribution completes in 1s on a single GPU, remaining stable as context accumulates to 40 turns; and (iv) concept-drift detection is conservative (0% false compaction) with recall bounded by attribution accuracy. We further describe the concept-drift micro compaction design and an interactive incident-tracing/memory-cleaning case study. A companion patent application covers the lineage construction method.
中文翻译
AI智能体的可解释性不应局限于理解模型内部权重，还应涵盖其在多源上下文下的推理与决策过程。我们提出LMLineage，一种非侵入式系统，可在不需白盒访问模型或额外推理步骤的情况下，将智能体会话中的每个输出句子归因于其支持的输入片段，并提供校准置信度。基于溯源元数据，我们构建概念漂移微压缩机制：不等待上下文长度限制再进行整体摘要，而是检测先前使用的输入文档何时停止被引用（即“漂移”），仅对这些文档进行精准压缩，同时完整保留用户指令。我们在多层级语料上进行评估：包含10个中文网络问答案例的71个标注句子，以及三个生成模型（gpt-4o-mini、qwen3.8-27b、deepseek-v4-flash）独立收集的90轮多轮对话。结果表明：(i) 窗口级嵌入相似性比全文或词法匹配更贴近LLM的改写行为；(ii) 快速贪心窗口路径在平均速度和准确性上均优于穷尽式行匹配（后者因多重比较偏差导致网页召回率虚高，牺牲整体准确率）；(iii) 每轮归因可在单个GPU上1秒内完成，随上下文累积至40轮仍保持稳定；(iv) 概念漂移检测保守（0%误压缩），召回率受归因准确率限制。我们进一步描述了概念漂移微压缩设计及交互式事件追溯/记忆清理案例研究。配套专利申请涵盖溯源构建方法。

Surfacing Disagreement Beats Resolving It: A Gated Ablation Study of Provenance-Weighted Memory for Multi-Agent Debate
暴露分歧胜于解决分歧：面向多智能体辩论中溯源加权记忆的门控消融研究
Shared memory for multi-agent debate is usually built to resolve inconsistency: retrieval returns one winning fact and the disagreement disappears before any agent sees it. We ask whether that is correct. In DebateGraph, four backends share one contract (read/write/snapshot), so five mechanisms from the agent-memory literature can be removed independently under a fixed budget, each gated on a pre-declared threshold. Across 39 runs one survives: preserving contradictions and passing the ranked conflict set to the debate, rather than collapsing it to a winner, improves F1 by +0.340 (95% CI [+0.267, +0.414]) and cuts attack success from 22 to 7 of 122 poisoned items (McNemar exact p=0.0003), at equal cost. Reaching it meant fixing a bottleneck in our harness: conflict sets are built only over retrieved edges, and our pre-declared cap left three quarters of planted contradictions unretrievable, first measuring the mechanism at +0.084. The effect replicates on two retrieval substrates and directionally on HotpotQA documents. No other mechanism survives, and two are worse than inert: once contradictions reach the prompt, a write trust-gate and the router's provenance features each cost accuracy (-0.075 and -0.096 F1). The gate fails structurally: poisoned objects are always entities the graph already contains, against 53% of gold, so groundedness scores the lie above a truthful first mention. We release the harness, attack set, and logs, and argue that silently reconciling memory is the assumption worth abandoning.
中文翻译
多智能体辩论中的共享记忆通常旨在消除不一致：检索返回单一胜出事实，使分歧在任何智能体看到前就消失。我们质疑这一做法是否正确。在DebateGraph中，四个后端共享一个合约（读/写/快照），因此可在固定预算下独立移除来自智能体记忆文献的五种机制，每种机制由预声明阈值控制。在39次实验中，唯一存活的机制是：保留矛盾，并将排序后的冲突集传递给辩论，而非将其合并为单一胜者。该机制使F1提升+0.340（95%置信区间[+0.267, +0.414]），攻击成功率从122项中的22项降至7项（McNemar精确p=0.0003），且成本相同。达到此效果需解决我们框架中的瓶颈：冲突集仅基于检索到的边构建，而预设上限使得四分之三的植入矛盾无法被检索，首次测量时机制表现仅为+0.084。该效应在两种检索底座上均可复现，并在HotpotQA文档上呈现方向性结果。无其他机制存活，且两项机制甚至劣于无操作：一旦矛盾进入提示，写入信任门和路由器的溯源特征各自导致准确率下降（F1分别-0.075和-0.096）。门控结构失败：被污染对象始终是图中已存在的实体，占黄金数据的53%，因此根基性评分将谎言置于真实首次提及之上。我们发布框架、攻击集和日志，并主张应放弃沉默调和记忆这一假设。

NONSENSE: Non-Semantic Strings Expose Semanticity Gaps in Dense Retrieval
NONSENSE：非语义字符串揭示密集检索中的语义性缺口
Dense retrieval is central to modern search, question answering, and retrieval-augmented generation, where high similarity is often treated as evidence of semantic relevance. We examine how far this assumption can be stressed. NONSENSE is a black-box sparse-edit probe that constructs visibly non-semantic strings while forbidding query-term copying, optimizing only the dense similarity between a query and the string. Across five BEIR-style datasets and three popular single-vector retrievers, these strings frequently outscore the retriever's own best judged query-relevant passage (QRP), and often rank first in the full corpus. The result indicates a semanticity gap in the evaluated dense retrievers: nearest-neighbor status can be separated from passage-like meaning. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating dense retrievers not only by whether they retrieve relevant documents, but also by how reliably their similarity scores reflect semantic evidence.
中文翻译
密集检索是现代搜索、问答和检索增强生成的核心，其中高相似度常被视为语义相关性的证据。我们考察这一假设能被推至何种极限。NONSENSE是一种黑箱稀疏编辑探测器，构造出明显非语义的字符串，禁止查询词复制，仅优化查询与字符串之间的密集相似度。在五个BEIR风格的数据集和三种主流单向量检索器上，这些字符串频繁超越检索器自身最佳判别相关段落（QRP），甚至常在全语料中排名第一。结果表明，所评估的密集检索器存在语义性缺口：最近邻状态可与段落式意义分离。这些发现强调，评估密集检索器不仅应关注其是否检索到相关文档，还应关注其相似度得分能否可靠反映语义证据。

Teaching Language Models to Narrow Boolean Patent Searches from Search Feedback
教导语言模型从搜索反馈中缩小布尔专利检索范围
Patent prior-art searchers issue Boolean queries line by line, broadening to find relevant documents and narrowing until candidates can be screened. We distill 38,397 Japan Patent Office search reports into executable expert strategies, re-execute them, and evaluate generated strategies by citation recall and the smallest set containing each citation. Prompted models write valid, high-recall strategies but leave citations in sets too large to screen. We adapt a generator offline from scored rollouts in two forms: strategy-level learning from a screening-aware strategy score, and line-level learning from citation-preserving narrowing credit, which upweights the query lines keeping a found citation in a smaller set. On shared rollouts, line-level credit outperforms strategy-level scores on screening measures; iterated, it cuts the median citation-containing set from 768 to under 50 and places more citations in sets under 100 documents than experts, at lower recall. Strategy-level preference learning reaches near-complete recall; the two forms are complementary.

👤 Seiya Kawano, Koji Marusaki, Hirofumi Nonaka, Koichiro Yoshino 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22227]
agent-benchmark
SOTA or Luck? The Winner's Curse in LLM Leaderboards
SOTA还是运气？大语言模型排行榜中的胜者诅咒
Public LLM leaderboards guide model evaluation and adoption, yet the reported performance of top-ranked models can be systematically overstated. This leaderboard winner's curse arises because ranking noisy benchmark scores rewards sampling luck alongside genuine ability. The same selection effect can distort scores throughout the leaderboard, a phenomenon we call the rank-induced bias. We develop an $m$-out-of-$N$ bootstrap to correct for this bias. It assumes a fixed model pool with independent, identically distributed bounded tasks while allowing arbitrary within-task correlation among models. Under exact population tiers separated by positive gaps, we prove that the bootstrap reduces $Θ(N^{-1/2})$ ranking bias to expected residual bias $o(N^{-1/2})$. Across 14 benchmarks from 5 benchmark families, the rank-1 adjustments range from approximately 0.09 to 3.86 percentage points. In our SWE-bench Verified snapshot, the tied leaders receive an adjustment of 0.90 percentage points. Such overstatement is large enough to make the apparent winner statistically fragile. Thus, we use the same bootstrap to further quantify the intervals of plausible rank positions for each model and group models into tiers. We establish sufficient conditions for consistent recovery of population tiers. On SWE-bench Verified, this procedure places 13 of 173 models in the leading tier. We combine the score adjustment and rank intervals in the Rank Uncertainty and Leaderboard Evaluation Report (RULER). We validate its finite-sample performance using sample splitting and calibrated simulations, and advocate publishing RULER alongside raw rankings so readers can assess both reported performance and the precision of the ordering. RULER also provides guidance for benchmark design that accounts for selection effects and ranking uncertainty.
中文翻译
公开的大语言模型排行榜引导模型评估与采纳，但顶级模型报告的性能可能系统性地被夸大。这种排行榜‘胜者诅咒’源于对噪声基准分数的排名，既奖励了真实的模型能力，也奖励了采样运气。同样的选择效应会扭曲排行榜整体分数，我们称之为‘排名诱导偏差’。为此，我们提出一种$m$-out-of-$N$自助法来校正此偏差。该方法假设一个固定模型池，任务独立同分布且有界，允许模型间存在任意的任务内相关性。在精确分层且层间存在正差距的条件下，我们证明该自助法可将$Θ(N^{-1/2})$的排名偏差降低至期望残差偏差$o(N^{-1/2})$。在来自5个基准家族的14个基准上，排名第一的调整幅度在约0.09至3.86个百分点之间。在SWE-bench Verified快照中，并列领先者获得0.90个百分点的调整。这种夸大程度足以使表面赢家在统计上变得脆弱。因此，我们利用相同自助法进一步量化每个模型的合理排名区间，并将模型划分为层级。我们建立了人口层级一致恢复的充分条件。在SWE-bench Verified上，该流程将173个模型中的13个归入领先层级。我们将分数调整与排名区间结合，形成‘排名不确定性与排行榜评估报告’（RULER）。通过样本分割和校准模拟验证其有限样本表现，并倡导在发布原始排名的同时公布RULER，以便读者评估报告性能及其排序精度。RULER还为考虑选择效应和排名不确定性的基准设计提供了指导。
👤 Sikun Xu, Raphael Thomadsen, Dennis J. Zhang, Heng Zhang 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22186]

Is 4-Bit Quantization More Than Noise to a Chat Model's Safety Behaviour? Attributing Behavioural Shifts with Matched Perturbations
4比特量化对聊天模型安全行为的影响：通过匹配扰动归因行为变化
Chat models on phones and laptops are usually 4-bit copies of the model that was evaluated, and audits of such copies disagree about what quantization does to refusal. A 4-bit build is one of many weight perturbations of the same size, so a change in its behaviour could come from its specific rounding error or from any perturbation that large. In a pre-registered study of three 3-4B chat models, with paired tests, blind human labels and an 8-bit null, we find two 4-bit shifts of opposite sign: with a compliant reply prefilled, Qwen2.5-3B continues into harmful content more often, and on held-out jailbreaks Llama-3.2-3B complies 10.3 points less often. Matched perturbations separate the two. Shuffled and Gaussian versions of the error average close to zero, while scaling the real error, from its mirror image to twice its size, changes behaviour near-linearly. At half the real error, Llama's likelihood is almost unchanged, yet it already complies 5.8 points less often, and llama.cpp's Q4_K_M, at the same bits per weight, moves neither model. Refusal under 4-bit quantization depends on the specific rounding error, not on bits per weight or likelihood, so each build has to be compared with its original.
中文翻译
手机和笔记本电脑上的聊天模型通常是经过评估模型的4比特版本，而对这些副本的安全审计结果存在分歧，关于量化如何影响拒绝行为尚无定论。4比特构建是同规模权重扰动的众多形式之一，因此其行为变化可能源于特定的舍入误差，也可能来自任何同等大小的扰动。在一项注册研究中，我们对三个3–4B聊天模型进行了成对测试、盲人人工标注和8比特对照组分析，发现两个4比特变化方向相反：在预填充合规回复的情况下，Qwen2.5-3B更频繁地继续生成有害内容；而在保留的越狱攻击测试中，Llama-3.2-3B的合规率降低了10.3个百分点。通过匹配扰动可分离这两种影响。打乱和高斯版本的误差平均接近零，而将真实误差从其镜像放大到两倍时，行为变化近似线性。在真实误差一半时，Llama的拒绝概率几乎不变，但其合规率已降低5.8个百分点；而llama.cpp的Q4_K_M在相同每权重比特数下，对任一模型均无影响。4比特量化的拒绝行为取决于具体舍入误差，而非每权重比特数或似然值，因此每个构建版本必须与其原始版本进行比较。

What RL on Expert-Built Tasks Teaches Nemotron 3.5 Super
在专家构建任务上进行强化学习对Nemotron 3.5 Super的启示
We post-train NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 Super (120B total, 12B active parameters) with reinforcement learning (RL) on five expert-built task datasets, one at a time: real-world software engineering, complex constraints, long-horizon tool use, professional chart understanding and enterprise document reasoning. Each run trains a fresh rank-32 LoRA adapter with CISPO on Tinker, with no supervised stage, and each adapter is compared with the base model on the same tasks, harness and settings. On twelve headline benchmarks, eight built by other groups and four by Surge AI, the gains range from +4.5 points on IFBench to +13.8 on GDP_pdf, which rises from 10.4% to 24.2%; SWE-bench Pro V2 rises from 86.9% to 92.1%, Terminal-Bench 2.1 from 63.9% to 70.3%, MultiChallenge from 58.3% to 66.6%, AutomationBench from 14.8% to 27.5% and Chartography from 39.4% to 50.6%. The document adapter, trained without API tools, also gains +8.0 points on AutomationBench. In attempts read side by side with the base model's on tasks each adapter newly solves, the base model's failures recur in a few modes, and each adapter does more often what the base model missed: the tool-use adapter finds the data and the rules before acting, the coding adapter writes its own test or scratch files on SWE-bench Pro V2, the constraints adapter carries earlier turns into its answers, the chart adapter measures the marks in code, and the document adapter reads AutomationBench's spreadsheets.

Thinking Harder Doesn't Lead to Better Choices: Reasoning Effort Increases the Confidence but Not the Correctness of LLM Shopping Agents
更深入思考并不带来更好选择：推理努力增加信心但不提升正确性
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous shopping agents that process multimodal product listings (consisting of visual and text data) and recommend purchases on behalf of consumers. Naturally, one might assume that models with more thinking power would make higher-quality purchasing decisions. To test this belief, we ask two questions about such agents: does asking a model to think harder make it choose better products, and does the confidence it reports tell us the likelihood it may be wrong? We benchmark ten frontier models on three product-choice benchmarks built from WebShop, Amazon's SQID and Wayfair's WANDS platforms to see if LLMs with more thinking power actually make better-quality product purchase decisions. Across all three benchmarks, we found that increasing the thinking effort does not increase decision quality in a statistically meaningful way. The same change in thinking effort raises stated confidence at a statistically significant level, so extra thinking buys confidence rather than correctness. We also measure the calibration of these models (how well their reported confidence percentage matches their true correctness percentage) in these tasks and show that most models are not well-calibrated at every effort level. We also find that errors are strongly shared: when one model errs in a task, several others tend to err on that same task. Furthermore, on tasks where several models all err, a large number of them pick the same wrong product.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地作为自主购物代理部署，处理包含视觉和文本数据的多模态商品列表，并代表消费者推荐购买。自然地，人们可能认为具有更强推理能力的模型会做出更高质量的购买决策。为检验这一信念，我们提出两个问题：要求模型更深入思考是否会使其选择更好的产品？其报告的信心水平是否能反映其出错的可能性？我们在三个基于WebShop、Amazon的SQID和Wayfair的WANDS平台构建的产品选择基准上，对十款前沿模型进行了基准测试，以验证拥有更强推理能力的模型是否真的能做出更优的购买决策。在所有三个基准上，增加推理努力并未在统计学意义上提升决策质量。同一变化显著提高了模型的陈述信心，表明额外思考带来的只是信心而非正确性。我们还测量了这些模型在不同推理努力水平下的校准度（报告信心百分比与其真实正确率匹配程度），发现大多数模型在各努力水平下均未良好校准。此外，我们发现错误高度共享：当一个模型在某任务出错时，其他多个模型也倾向于在同一任务出错。尤其在多个模型同时出错的任务中，大量模型会选择相同的错误商品。
👤 Adi Krishnamoorthy, X.Y. Han 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
