SkillBuffBench: Measuring What Agent Skills Contribute across Harnesses, Models, Skill Access Modes and Memory
SkillBuffBench：跨Harness、模型、技能访问模式与记忆衡量代理技能贡献
Agent Skills, file-based packages of procedural knowledge that a coding agent can discover and load on demand, are now shipped by every major agent harness, yet the evidence for how much they help comes almost entirely from settings in which the right Skill is hand-picked for the task and the agent starts from a blank slate. Neither holds in deployment, where an agent chooses from whatever library happens to be installed and frequently carries memory from earlier work. We present SkillBuffBench, a benchmark and paired evaluation protocol that treats Skill access as a three-level factor (no Skill, a curated Skill, and automatic invocation from an installed library), crosses it with memory (off and on), with four agent harnesses and with multiple models (the state-of-the-art proprietary models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI, and the state-of-the-art open-weight models from Chinese providers), every model being run under every harness, and evaluates the result on research tasks contributed by domain experts together with the Skills they use, currently in materials science and biology, with the suite extended on a rolling basis. Every run is marked by a deterministic verifier; uncertainty is reported with a paired, task-level bootstrap; and cost, agent rounds and wall-clock time are reported alongside the score rather than folded into it. This version of the paper describes the design of the benchmark, its evaluation protocol and its current tasks and Skills. The evaluation is in progress: results will be added once the runs are complete, and the task suite, results and analysis will continue to be updated on a rolling basis.
中文翻译
代理技能——即文件形式的程序知识包，编码代理可按需发现并加载——如今已被各大代理Harness提供，但现有证据几乎完全来自理想化场景：任务中手动挑选正确技能，且代理从空白状态开始。然而在实际部署中，代理只能从当前安装的库中选择，且经常携带先前工作的记忆。我们提出SkillBuffBench，一个基准测试及其配套评估协议，将技能访问视为三级因素（无技能、精选技能、自动调用已安装库中的技能），与记忆状态（开启/关闭）、四种代理Harness以及多种模型（Anthropic、Google和OpenAI的前沿专有模型，以及中国厂商提供的前沿开源模型）交叉组合，每种模型在每种Harness下运行，并在领域专家提供的研究任务上评估结果，目前涵盖材料科学和生物学领域，任务套件将持续滚动扩展。每次运行均由确定性验证器标记；不确定性通过任务级配对Bootstrap报告；成本、代理轮次和墙钟时间与分数一同报告，而非合并进分数。本文描述了该基准的设计、评估协议及其当前任务与技能。评估正在进行中：待运行完成后将补充结果，任务套件、结果与分析将持续滚动更新。

Beyond Bitwidth: Deployment-Constrained Mixed-Precision Quantization for Multilingual LLM Inference on NVIDIA Blackwell
超越位宽：面向NVIDIA Blackwell部署约束的多语言LLM混合精度量化推理
Low-bit LLM quantization is useful only if a model preserves service-relevant accuracy, executes through supported GPU kernels, and meets measured memory and latency limits. We study Qwen3-8B on NVIDIA B200 with vLLM, including INT2/INT3, GPTQ, NVFP4, and mixed-precision configurations. We verify native packed execution and precision on all 144 fused linear modules and evaluate Korean, English, numerical, and reasoning quality under preregistered gates. Packed INT2 and INT3 execute, but all seven tested INT2 grids degenerate on generation. Isolated module-restoration effects are strongly non-additive; Korean and reasoning sensitivity rankings disagree, and a promising INT4/INT8 development candidate fails independent final evaluation. A configuration with 16-bit activations, 98 NVFP4 weight-only modules, 46 INT8 modules, and an INT8 output head passes all gates on the untouched FINAL-81 set (5,168 items): 5.917 GiB of weight-loading memory (limit 5.98), Korean teacher-correct retention 0.904 (gate 0.90), and TPOT ratio 0.939 relative to BF16. It improves paired accuracy over NVFP4 W4A4 by +1.4 percentage points (Bonferroni-adjusted interval [+0.2, +2.6]), compared with +3.4 points on the selection set. This comparison changes both activation precision and INT8 protection; exploratory analyses suggest that the activation change contributes substantially, but the isolated allocation effect is not established. Final checkpoints were not retained, and bitwise rebuild reproducibility remains unverified.

低比特LLM量化只有在模型保持服务相关准确性、能在支持的GPU内核上执行，并满足实测内存与延迟限制时才有效。我们研究了在NVIDIA B200上使用vLLM运行的Qwen3-8B模型，包括INT2/INT3、GPTQ、NVFP4以及混合精度配置。我们验证了所有144个融合线性模块的原生打包执行与精度，并在预注册门控条件下评估韩语、英语、数值和推理质量。打包的INT2和INT3可执行，但所有七个测试的INT2网格在生成阶段均出现退化。模块恢复效应高度非加性：韩语与推理敏感性排名不一致，一个有前景的INT4/INT8开发候选在独立最终评估中失败。一个包含16位激活、98个NVFP4权重模块、46个INT8模块和一个INT8输出头的配置成功通过未修改的FINAL-81集（5,168项）的所有门控条件：权重加载内存为5.917 GiB（上限5.98），韩语教师校正保留率0.904（门限0.90），TPOT比率为BF16的0.939。相比NVFP4 W4A4，该配置在配对准确率上提升+1.4个百分点（Bonferroni校正区间[+0.2, +2.6]），在选择集上提升+3.4点。这一比较同时改变了激活精度和INT8保护策略；探索性分析表明激活变化贡献显著，但孤立分配效应尚未确立。最终检查点未保存，位级重建可复现性仍未验证。

Bookkeeping Is Not Judgement: EJBench, an Engineering Judgement Benchmark Built from a Conjugate Heat Transfer Design Campaign
账目管理并非判断：基于共轭传热设计活动构建的工程判断基准EJBench
We introduce EJBench, a benchmark of engineering judgement: whether a model can predict how a design behaves at new conditions from a few earlier results, and decide on it. Unlike engineering benchmarks that test whether an agent can operate simulation or CAD software, EJBench is software-agnostic and asks what an engineer would conclude without running a simulation. Its tasks come from a conjugate heat transfer design campaign for battery cold plates, in three families: matching unlabelled test results to their operating points, choosing which stored runs to reveal before signing off a design, and ranking new duties by peak cell temperature. Every answer is taken from a converged simulation, not from an LLM judge. The best of ten frontier models, GPT-6 Astra, scores 0.71, and models state almost the same confidence on tasks they get wrong as on tasks they get right. We also post-train Qwen3.5-9B with LoRA and reinforcement learning, rewarded by the share of answer criteria the simulation results confirm; on the held-out test split its score rises from 0.071 to 0.106. We release the train, validation and test splits, the evaluation harness and the leaderboard.

Asking What Matters: Disagreement-Guided Ambiguity Detection for Interactive Text-to-SQL
关注关键问题：基于分歧引导的交互式文本转SQL模糊性检测
Interactive text-to-SQL requires an LLM agent not only to generate executable SQL, but also to decide which uncertainty to clarify within a limited interaction budget. However, current clarification policies have three limitations: (i) they target surface ambiguity without testing its SQL impact; (ii) they miss ambiguities implicit in schema routes or domain knowledge; and (iii) they spend limited turns on unfocused or redundant questions. These limitations expose a critical challenge: identifying SQL-impacting ambiguity before the intended SQL is known. In this paper, we propose DisGuAD, a Disagreement-Guided Ambiguity Detection framework that specializes clarification selection. DisGuAD treats disagreement among plausible SQL interpretations as direct evidence of which uncertainty matters to the final query. Specifically, it constructs a query-term inventory, elicits contrastive SQL interpretations through complementary probe families, compares their canonical SQL components, and attributes recurring differences to user-facing clarification targets. A lightweight prioritization and dialogue-state mechanism then selects one high-impact unresolved target for clarification. Experiments on BIRD-Interact Lite and Full with two LLM backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in task success and clarification quality. Compared with the original interaction policy, DisGuAD improves normalized Reward by 4.40--8.97 points and raises overall critical-ambiguity coverage from 48.62% to 82.75% and from 45.76% to 85.14%. Its clarification signals further improve two independently designed static text-to-SQL frameworks by up to 13.97 Reward points while keeping their SQL generators fixed. These results demonstrate that disagreement among plausible SQL queries provides actionable evidence for selecting clarification targets that matter to downstream generation.
中文翻译
交互式文本转SQL要求大语言模型代理不仅生成可执行的SQL，还需在有限的交互预算内决定澄清哪些不确定性。然而，现有的澄清策略存在三个局限：(i) 仅针对表面模糊性，未检验其对SQL的影响；(ii) 忽略了模式路径或领域知识中隐含的模糊性；(iii) 在无关或冗余问题上消耗了有限的交互轮次。这些局限暴露了一个关键挑战：在知晓目标SQL之前识别出影响SQL结果的模糊性。本文提出DisGuAD（分歧引导的模糊性检测）框架，专注于澄清选择。DisGuAD将多个合理SQL解释之间的分歧视为最终查询中关键不确定性的直接证据。具体而言，它构建查询项清单，通过互补探测族获取对比性SQL解释，比较其标准SQL组件，并将反复出现的差异归因于面向用户的澄清目标。随后，轻量级优先级排序与对话状态机制选择一个高影响且未解决的目标进行澄清。在BIRD-Interact Lite和Full数据集上，使用两种LLM骨干网络的实验表明，任务成功率和澄清质量均有持续提升。相比原始交互策略，DisGuAD使归一化奖励提升4.40–8.97分，总体关键模糊性覆盖率从48.62%提升至82.75%，从45.76%提升至85.14%。其澄清信号还使两个独立设计的静态文本转SQL框架的性能最高提升13.97分奖励，同时保持其SQL生成器不变。结果表明，合理SQL查询间的分歧为选择对下游生成至关重要的澄清目标提供了可操作的证据。

Structure--Residual Inductive Biases for Language Model Pretraining
语言模型预训练中的结构-残差归纳偏置
Next-token prediction is relied upon to learn both predictive behavior and internal representations. We ask whether representation structure itself should be an explicit inductive bias. Motivated by the two-part coding view of Kolmogorov complexity, we study a structure--residual principle in which a representation separates structure from realization detail. We instantiate this principle in two complementary ways. Boundary Bottleneck Concept (BBC) routes information from token segments through boundary carriers while preserving local autoregressive computation. Continuous Concept (CC) regularizes representation geometry and induces a spectral decomposition into low-frequency structure and high-frequency residual. We derive coding bounds for both views and show that multiplicity-corrected boundary attention becomes exact as the within-segment path energy vanishes. Empirically, BBC and CC exhibit strikingly different depth preferences: BBC works best in early layers, whereas CC is most effective in later layers. At 300M training tokens, combining BBC and CC improves NLL by 1.44\% on GPT-2 Medium. These gains persist with training runs up to 20B tokens. Representation diagnostics show that BBC increases the relative predictive importance of boundary states, while CC reduces spectral residual energy. Together, these results suggest that structure--residual biases can improve language-model pretraining beyond next-token prediction. Code: https://github.com/haihuang-ml/structure-residual
中文翻译
当前语言模型依赖于下一个词预测来学习预测行为和内部表征。我们探讨是否应将表征结构本身作为显式的归纳偏置。受柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂性两部分编码观点的启发，我们研究了一种结构-残差原则，即表征将结构与实现细节分离。我们以两种互补方式实现了这一原则：边界瓶颈概念（BBC）通过边界载体传递词元片段的信息，同时保持局部自回归计算；连续概念（CC）正则化表征几何，并诱导出低频结构与高频残差的谱分解。我们推导了两种视角下的编码界，并证明当段内路径能量趋近于零时，多重性修正后的边界注意力趋于精确。实证结果表明，BBC和CC表现出截然不同的深度偏好：BBC在早期层表现最佳，而CC在后期层最有效。在3亿训练词元下，结合BBC和CC使GPT-2 Medium的负对数似然（NLL）降低1.44%。这些收益在长达200亿词元的训练中依然持续。表征诊断显示，BBC提升了边界状态的相对预测重要性，而CC降低了谱残差能量。综合来看，这些结果表明，结构-残差偏置可在超越下一个词预测的基础上提升语言模型的预训练效果。代码地址：https://github.com/haihuang-ml/structure-residual
👤 Hai Huang, Bartosz Żuk, Rassul Magauin, Jiaxin Huang, Xue Liu 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.23106]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Natural Language Programming: Rethinking Language, Memory, and Cognition in the Era of Generative AI
自然语言编程：生成式AI时代语言、记忆与认知的重新思考
Generative Artificial Intelligence is transforming the role of natural language in computational systems. In classical Natural Language Processing (NLP), language was treated as an object of computation: something to analyse, classify, translate, or generate. Large Language Models (LLMs) challenge this perspective, enabling natural language to function increasingly as an executable cognitive medium through which humans structure reasoning, orchestrate problem solving, and regulate hybrid human/AI workflows. This paper introduces Natural Language Programming (NLPg) as a theoretical and epistemological extension of traditional NLP. Rather than focusing on language understanding alone, NLPg conceptualises prompts as dynamic cognitive artefacts that externalise goals, constraints, assumptions, validation criteria, and intermediate reasoning states. Prompting is thus not merely a technical interaction technique, but a form of dialogic cognitive programming operating within distributed human/AI memory architectures. Building on theories of distributed cognition, extended mind, metacognition, and dialogic learning, the paper proposes the notion of Dialogic Working Memory, describing how prompts and conversational states function as temporary external memory structures supporting reasoning continuity and reflective regulation. We further map existing memory architectures (BTMA, SKGE, RMR) onto this framework, illustrating how episodic, semantic, and metacognitive layers support long-horizon conversational coherence. Finally, implications for computational linguistics and AI/mediated knowledge systems are discussed, suggesting a post symbolic and post interface paradigm in which natural language operates simultaneously as communication medium, executable specification, cognitive scaffold, and distributed memory architecture.
中文翻译
生成式人工智能正在重塑自然语言在计算系统中的角色。在传统的自然语言处理（NLP）中，语言被视为一种计算对象：用于分析、分类、翻译或生成的内容。大型语言模型（LLMs）挑战了这一观点，使自然语言越来越多地作为可执行的认知媒介，帮助人类组织推理、协调问题解决，并调控人机混合工作流程。本文提出自然语言编程（NLPg），作为传统NLP在理论和认识论上的延伸。NLPg不仅关注语言理解，更将提示视为动态的认知产物，外化目标、约束、假设、验证标准及中间推理状态。因此，提示不仅仅是技术交互手段，而是一种在分布式人机记忆架构中运行的对话式认知编程形式。基于分布式认知、扩展心智、元认知和对话学习等理论，本文提出了‘对话工作记忆’的概念，描述提示与对话状态如何作为临时外部记忆结构，支持推理连续性和反思性调节。我们进一步将现有的记忆架构（BTMA、SKGE、RMR）映射到该框架，展示情景、语义和元认知层次如何支持长时程对话连贯性。最后，讨论了对计算语言学和AI/媒介知识系统的启示，建议进入一个后符号、后界面的范式，在此范式中，自然语言同时作为通信媒介、可执行规范、认知支架和分布式记忆架构发挥作用。

Runtime Verification of Multiple Natural Language Criteria for Agent Governance
多自然语言准则的运行时验证用于智能体治理
AI agents are governed by hundreds of natural language criteria, yet existing verifiers cannot efficiently evaluate an agent's output against a large set of such criteria and report which are satisfied, violated, or inapplicable. We introduce the VFM, a tree-attention verifier that encodes the shared context and target text, and scores N natural language criteria in parallel, returning independent per-criterion ternary verdicts. The VFM is pretrained on CriteriaBank, a corpus of 394K (context, target, criterion, verdict) tuples spanning over 321K distinct criterion strings. A finetuned VFM-4B outperforms GPT-5.4 on a synthetic governance benchmark. On the DynaBench multi-criterion benchmark, it outperforms DynaGuard-4B on trace-level failure detection, and its per-criterion verdicts localize the violated rule and support focused escalation to a larger chain-of-thought judge. We show that CriteriaBank pretraining improves cross-domain transfer, particularly in the low-data regime, while matching a strong direct-finetuning control at full data. On a single H100, VFM-4B evaluates 1,000 criteria against a 4K-token context in under 2s at <11 GB VRAM, making it suitable for runtime verification.
中文翻译
AI智能体受到数百条自然语言准则的约束，但现有验证器无法高效评估智能体输出是否满足大量准则，并报告哪些准则被满足、违反或不适用。我们提出VFM，一种基于树注意力的验证器，能够编码共享上下文和目标文本，并并行评分N条自然语言准则，返回独立的三元 verdict（满足/违反/不适用）。VFM在CriteriaBank上预训练，该语料库包含39.4万条（上下文, 目标, 准则, 判定）元组，涵盖超过32.1万种不同的准则字符串。经过微调的VFM-4B在合成治理基准测试中表现优于GPT-5.4；在DynaBench多准则基准测试中，其在追踪级故障检测方面优于DynaGuard-4B，且每项准则的判定结果能精确定位违规规则，支持向更大规模链式思维裁判的定向升级。我们证明，CriteriaBank预训练提升了跨领域迁移能力，尤其在低数据场景下表现优异，同时在全量数据下与强直接微调对照组表现相当。在单张H100 GPU上，VFM-4B可在2秒内完成对4K token上下文的1000条准则评估，显存占用低于11GB，适用于实时验证。

When Passing Checks Do Not Establish Completion: Evidence Interpretation in Coding Agents
当通过检查并不能证明完成时：编码代理中的证据解释
Verification procedures in coding agents must preserve execution facts, interpret their scope and discharge checking obligations. We study these responsibilities through qualified repository checks, controlled evidence consumers and executable workflows. A prospective thirteen-task factorial study finds that correct reported facts need not yield the requested finite verdict. Conversely, all 122 delivered labels in a held-out receipt-supply study are correct, while sixteen responses misreport facts and eleven others exceed authorized scope. Production repair supplies successful controls and finite-supported preservation failures. Across 118 repair episodes on 31 tasks, source interventions separate startup reachability from repair and witness production from the claimed operation. Lifetime and resource controls delimit what passing validation establishes. Generated-note comparisons yield local repair differences but can state false explanations; strong ordinary baselines, new tasks and Skill-transfer studies supply no consistent overall advantage. These results characterize evidence interpretation, applicability and fulfilled actions while limiting claims of general diagnostic superiority, Skill deterioration or deployed folding effects.
中文翻译
编码代理中的验证流程必须保持执行事实的完整性，正确解释其作用范围并履行检查义务。我们通过受控的仓库检查、受控证据消费者和可执行工作流来研究这些责任。一项涉及十三个任务的因子实验发现，正确的报告事实并不必然导致所需的有限结论。相反，在保留收据的研究中，122个交付标签全部正确，但有十六个回应错误报告事实，十一个超出授权范围。生产修复成功实施了控制措施，并揭示了有限支持的保存失败。在31个任务上的118次修复事件中，源干预手段区分了启动可达性与修复过程，以及见证生成与声称操作之间的关系。生命周期和资源控制限定了通过验证所能确立的内容。生成笔记的对比显示局部修复差异，但可能陈述错误解释；强常规基线、新任务和技能迁移研究均未显示出一致的整体优势。这些结果刻画了证据解释、适用性与已履行动作的特征，同时限制了对通用诊断优越性、技能退化或部署折叠效应的主张。

JET: Just-Enough Exploration Tuning for Efficient Tool-Calling Agents in Repository-Level Question Answering
JET：面向仓库级问答中高效工具调用智能体的恰到好处探索调优方法
In repository-level question answering (QA), an agent explores a code repository with tools and should answer a question once enough information is gathered. Nevertheless, we find that LLM agents often continue to invoke tools even after the gathered information is already sufficient to answer. We call this phenomenon over-exploration. Moreover, even though existing methods improve task performance through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories, SFT also propagates the over-exploration in the trajectories to the agent. To mitigate over-exploration, we propose Just-Enough Exploration Tuning (JET), a fine-tuning method that trains the agent to answer once the gathered information is sufficient. JET truncates each training trajectory at the step where the gathered information becomes sufficient, inserts a rationale that explains why the agent can answer at that step, and fine-tunes the agent with a higher loss weight on the rationale. Experiments on two repository-level QA benchmarks with two open-weight 8B models demonstrate that JET reduces tool calls by 11 to 33% on self-generated trajectories while largely preserving the score. On expert trajectories, JET makes 18 to 28% fewer tool calls than SFT while retaining most of the score gain of SFT. In both cases, JET also lowers the inference cost.
中文翻译
在仓库级问答（QA）中，智能体需通过工具探索代码仓库，并在收集足够信息后回答问题。然而，我们发现LLM智能体常在信息已足够时仍继续调用工具。我们将此现象称为过度探索。此外，尽管现有方法通过专家轨迹的监督微调（SFT）提升了任务性能，但SFT也把轨迹中的过度探索行为传播给了智能体。为缓解过度探索，我们提出恰到好处探索调优（JET），一种微调方法，训练智能体在信息足够时即刻作答。JET在信息足够时截断每条训练轨迹，插入解释为何此时可作答的理由，并以更高的损失权重对理由部分进行微调。在两个仓库级QA基准上使用两个开源8B模型的实验表明，JET在自生成轨迹上减少了11%至33%的工具调用，同时基本保持分数不变；在专家轨迹上，JET比SFT减少18%至28%的工具调用，同时保留SFT大部分分数增益。两种情况下，JET还降低了推理成本。

LMMR: Learned Multigranular Memory Retrieval for Small Language Model Agents
LMMR：面向小型语言模型代理的可学习多粒度记忆检索
Small language model agents have limited space to read the memory needed for multi-hop questions. Relevant facts are often scattered across records whose surrounding text consumes much of that space. We propose LMMR, a learned multigranular memory retrieval method that selects both the next source and how much of it to read. LMMR links paragraphs and sentence fragments to their original sources and pairs views that supply the same missing support during training. Relevant distractors and repeated information provide contrasting examples. At inference, the reranker scores what each candidate adds to an anchor passage, and a selector chooses the evidence within a token budget. On 22,398 official validation questions from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, and MuSiQue, a frozen 2B answer model reaches 61.47, 43.26, and 30.58 F1 under a 400-token budget. The gains over query expansion are 3.06, 2.14, and 4.55 points, while memory input decreases by 23.1-26.9%. LMMR also uses 23.5-27.7% fewer input tokens than a coarse retriever trained on the same questions. Against that retriever followed by LLMLingua-2 at comparable input lengths, F1 gains reach 3.99 points and are significant on HotpotQA and 2WikiMultihopQA after Holm correction. All eight small answer models evaluated on HotpotQA improve over query expansion and MMR in both F1 and exact match.

EGS: A Complementary Safety Framework for Educational LLMs
EGS：教育型大语言模型的互补安全框架
Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong potential for personalized education, but adolescent-facing deployment raises risks of toxic, biased, misleading, or developmentally inappropriate content. We argue that educational safety should move beyond binary refusal: student queries are often ambiguous or curiosity-driven, requiring contextual guidance rather than simple blocking. We introduce the Educational Guardrail System (EGS), a lightweight, model-agnostic runtime framework that complements intrinsic alignment through query moderation, scenario-aware pedagogical guidance, and response moderation. EGS triages inputs by risk level, redirects gray-zone queries with category-specific educational interventions, and audits outputs before delivery. Experiments across general and education-specific benchmarks show that EGS improves moderation performance, refusal quality, and jailbreak robustness across diverse LLMs. These findings suggest that defense-in-depth guardrails can strengthen safety while preserving the pedagogical utility required in educational LLMs. Our code and model weights will be publicly available to support community adoption.

What MoE Routing Controls: Causal Interventions on Expert Trajectories
MoE路由控制什么：对专家轨迹的因果干预
In mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models, a token is routed to a sequence of experts across layers. We investigate how forcing tokens onto chosen expert paths influences model behavior. We build a target path from the experts that a concept's or role's tokens most often use, and we add a fixed bias to each target expert's router logit during generation to steer the token toward this expert path. We study two kinds of targets. Format targets concern the form of the output, and concept targets concern its topic. In Qwen3-30B-A3B, we show that steering a format path changes the text the model writes. We also show that steering a concept path shifts the model's next-token probabilities toward the concept, even when the path is built in another language, yet this shift has little effect on the generated text. We investigate why formats reach the text but concepts do not by steering only part of the generation, and find evidence that a format, once started, reinforces itself. We show that the concepts religion and money steer most strongly, and that the effect is concentrated in the middle layers. We attempt to explain why the strength of the effect varies by concept through an analysis of embedding distinctiveness and word frequency.
中文翻译
在混合专家（MoE）语言模型中，一个标记会跨层被路由到一系列专家。我们研究强制标记沿选定专家路径运行如何影响模型行为。我们从概念或角色标记最常使用的专家中构建目标路径，并在生成过程中向目标专家的路由器logit添加固定偏置，以引导标记走向该路径。我们研究两类目标：格式目标关注输出形式，概念目标关注其主题。在Qwen3-30B-A3B中，我们展示引导格式路径会改变模型所写文本。我们还发现，引导概念路径会使模型的下一个词概率向该概念偏移，即使路径是在另一种语言中构建的，但这种偏移对生成文本影响甚微。我们通过仅部分生成过程的引导来探究为何格式能影响文本而概念不能，发现一旦格式启动，便会自我强化。我们显示宗教和金钱概念的引导效果最强，且效应集中在中间层。我们通过嵌入区分度和词频分析尝试解释不同概念效应强度差异的原因。

DiagTrace: An Interactive Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making
DiagTrace：一种用于临床决策的交互式基准测试
Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on medical question answering and diagnostic reasoning benchmarks. However, existing evaluations typically provide all patient information upfront and assess only the final diagnosis or rationale. This setup ignores the need to acquire diagnostic evidence incrementally from incomplete information in real-world diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiagTrace, a source-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether LLMs can reconstruct the documented diagnostic trajectory through interactive information seeking. Each benchmark instance is built from a PubMed Central case report and includes an initial presentation, an event bank, a clinical course, and diagnostic references. DiagTrace contains 510 cases across eight specialties, with 225 used to evaluate four LLMs on clinical-course alignment, interaction quality, and efficiency. Results reveal trade-offs in coverage, source-consistent ordering, and turn use.

[2611.21921]

Latent Fact Composition under Next-Token Training
基于下一个词训练的潜在事实组合
Controlled studies of latent two-hop reasoning train transformers to predict only the answer, whereas language models are trained to predict every token. We ask whether a model trained on every token learns to answer two-hop questions whose intermediate entity appears in no two-hop training example. We build a synthetic corpus with such questions. On this corpus every term of the loss can be computed exactly. A looped transformer, a standard transformer and DiscoLoop answer almost none of these questions. We therefore introduce the Baton family of looped transformers. They pass the intermediate entity to the second loop as a token embedding. When trained to predict only the answer, two of its three variants answer these questions almost perfectly. We then relate the success and failure of each model to three design conditions. Experiments that change one condition at a time agree with this hypothesis. When Baton is trained on every token, however, its accuracy on these questions rises and then declines. We further split the gradient of the next-token loss into three terms. Removing the two terms that depend on the particular training set raises the peak accuracy but does not remove the decline. The decline arises in the second hop while the model still recalls both facts.
中文翻译
对潜在两跳推理的受控研究通常训练Transformer模型仅预测答案，而语言模型则被训练预测每一个词。我们探讨了在每个词上进行训练的模型是否能回答中间实体未出现在任何两跳训练样本中的两跳问题。我们构建了一个包含此类问题的合成语料库，该语料库中损失函数的每一项均可精确计算。循环Transformer、标准Transformer和DiscoLoop几乎无法回答这些问题。因此，我们引入了循环Transformer家族——Baton。它将中间实体作为词嵌入传递给第二轮循环。当仅训练预测答案时，其三种变体中的两种几乎完美地回答了这些问题。随后，我们将各模型的成功与失败归因于三个设计条件。每次只改变一个条件的实验结果支持该假设。然而，当Baton在每个词上进行训练时，其在这些问题上的准确率先上升后下降。我们进一步将下一个词损失的梯度分解为三项。移除依赖特定训练集的两项后，峰值准确率提高，但下降趋势依然存在。下降现象出现在第二跳阶段，此时模型仍能回忆起两个事实。
👤 Tianyi Zhang, Feiying Chen 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21891]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Call-Efficient ICD-10 Code Prediction via Supervised Candidate Generation and Selective LLM Invocation
通过监督候选生成与选择性LLM调用实现高效的ICD-10编码预测
Automatic assignment of ICD-10 codes from short clinical diagnosis text is a practical medical text classification and normalization task. The task is difficult because diagnosis phrases are short and noisy, the ICD-10 label space is hierarchical, and the class distribution is strongly imbalanced. This paper presents a call-efficient ICD-10 code prediction pipeline that combines transformer embeddings, tree-based supervised candidate generation, candidate-level reranking, and a gated LLM assistance. The evaluation uses a leakage-safe validation protocol: model and threshold selection are based on out-of-fold cross-validation predictions, while final reporting is performed on a locked test set with no shared source-file groups. The supervised conditional parent-code candidate generator reaches 74.32% Top-1 accuracy on the locked test set and 93.05% Top-10 candidate coverage. A direct zero-shot GPT-5.6-Sol baseline reaches 81.01% Top-1 accuracy, but requires an invocation of this large model for every diagnosis. The final deployable system, Pre-LLM fusion, reaches 81.24% OOF-CV accuracy and 83.19% locked-test accuracy while calling the LLM for only 31.97% of cases. This improves the locked-test error rate from 25.68% for the supervised parent-code baseline to 16.81%, corresponding to a 34.5% relative error reduction. The results show that the most effective role of the LLM is controlled reranking on uncertain examples rather than unrestricted coding or full-dataset invocation.

从简短的临床诊断文本中自动分配ICD-10编码是一项实际的医疗文本分类与标准化任务。该任务具有挑战性，因为诊断短语简短且噪声大，ICD-10标签空间具有层次结构，且类别分布严重不平衡。本文提出了一种高效的ICD-10编码预测流程，结合了Transformer嵌入、基于树的监督候选生成、候选级重排序以及门控LLM辅助机制。评估采用无泄漏验证协议：模型与阈值选择基于交叉验证的预测结果，最终报告在锁定测试集上完成，且不共享源文件组。监督条件下的父编码候选生成器在锁定测试集上达到74.32%的Top-1准确率和93.05%的Top-10候选覆盖率。直接的零样本GPT-5.6-Sol基线达到81.01%的Top-1准确率，但需为每个诊断调用该大型模型。最终可部署系统Pre-LLM融合在OOF-CV上达到81.24%准确率，在锁定测试集上达到83.19%准确率，且仅在31.97%的情况下调用LLM。这将锁定测试集错误率从监督父编码基线的25.68%降低至16.81%，相对误差减少34.5%。结果表明，LLM最有效的作用是在不确定案例上进行受控重排序，而非无限制编码或全数据集调用。

Knowing Which, Not How Much: Language Models Order Party Positions They Cannot Place
知其序，不知其量：语言模型无法定位却能排序政党立场
Language models are increasingly asked where real actors stand: what a party's platform prioritises, what an institution has committed to. Such judgments are gradable, carrying both an order and a magnitude. Nearly every benchmark that tests them asks about one entity at a time, then reads that score as the model's knowledge of the domain. Order and magnitude, however, need not be acquired together. Text about social entities carries far more comparison than measurement, so a model may hold a dependable ordering with no anchored scale beneath it. We ask whether a model can place a documented social position on a scale or only order the actors it compares, and what that means for how such knowledge is measured. We test this on party positions in Japan and South Korea, where the Comparative Manifesto Project's coded data supplies a rare external answer key. Asked to order four parties, models beat the strongest fixed-answer baseline by more than a factor of two; asked to place one party on the same scale, they fall below it. The ordering survives inside those failed placements, and supplying the coded values lifts placement to near ceiling. Comparative and absolute questions about the same fact therefore measure different things here, and for party issue salience a benchmark built from one cannot speak for the other.
中文翻译
语言模型越来越多地被要求判断现实主体的立场：政党平台的优先事项、机构承诺的内容。这类判断是可分级的，既包含顺序也包含程度。然而，几乎所有测试此类能力的基准都一次只询问一个实体，然后将得分视为模型对领域的知识掌握程度。顺序与程度并不一定同时获得。关于社会主体的文本包含远多于测量的信息，因此模型可能具备可靠的排序能力，但缺乏底层的锚定尺度。我们探究模型能否在尺度上定位已记录的社会立场，还是仅能对所比较的主体进行排序，以及这对知识衡量方式意味着什么。我们在日本和韩国政党立场上进行了测试，利用比较政纲项目编码数据作为罕见的外部答案键。当被要求排序四个政党时，模型的表现优于最强的固定答案基线超过两倍；当被要求在同一尺度上定位一个政党时，其表现则低于基线。排序能力在这些失败的定位中依然存在，而提供编码值后，定位性能接近上限。因此，对于同一事实的比较性问题与绝对性问题在此处衡量的是不同内容，基于单一类型构建的基准无法代表另一类。

TUSI: A Citation-Grounded Graph-Based Benchmark
TUSI：一种基于引文的图结构基准
Evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in Persian law remains challenging because benchmarks lack realism, grounding, and coverage of legal reasoning. Existing resources are either synthetically generated from statutory texts, lacking natural information needs, or derived from informal legal question--answer sources without explicit linkage to authoritative legal documents, making them unsuitable for rigorous evaluation of grounded legal reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce TUSI-Bench, a structured Persian legal benchmark designed for the evaluation of RAG-based systems. TUSI-Bench is built upon a multi-layered legal knowledge framework containing 7,261 legal articles and integrating codified statutes, legal commentaries, doctrinal writings, and Supreme Court unification rulings. We also construct a legal knowledge graph linking articles and concepts for structure-aware retrieval. Based on this foundation, TUSI-Bench provides two complementary evaluation tasks: (i) TUSI-QA, an open-ended question answering benchmark containing 1,999 questions that requires grounded responses with explicit legal citations, and (ii) TUSI-Bar, a multiple-choice benchmark containing approximately 500 four-option questions derived from Iranian Bar examination questions. Furthermore, we propose a graph-based retrieval baseline that leverages relational structure within the legal knowledge graph. Experimental results demonstrate that incorporating graph-based relations improves retrieval recall by up to 8 percentage points compared to standard baselines, highlighting the importance of structured legal representations for RAG systems. The proposed benchmark provides a standardized resource for evaluating grounded legal reasoning in Persian and enables future research on structured legal intelligence.

在波斯语法律领域评估检索增强生成（RAG）系统仍然具有挑战性，因为现有基准缺乏真实性、可溯源性和法律推理的覆盖范围。现有资源要么是从法规文本合成生成的，缺乏自然的信息需求，要么来自非正式的法律问答来源，未明确链接到权威法律文件，因而不适合严格评估基于证据的法律推理。为解决这一空白，我们提出了TUSI-Bench，一个专为评估基于RAG系统的波斯语法律基准。TUSI-Bench建立在包含7,261条法律条文的多层次法律知识框架之上，整合了成文法、法律评注、学说著作和最高法院统一裁决。我们还构建了一个法律知识图谱，用于连接条文与概念，实现结构感知的检索。基于此基础，TUSI-Bench提供了两个互补的评估任务：(i) TUSI-QA，一个开放式的问答基准，包含1,999个问题，要求回答必须附带明确的法律引用；(ii) TUSI-Bar，一个多项选择题基准，包含约500道四选项题目，源自伊朗律师资格考试题。此外，我们提出了一种基于图结构的检索基线，利用法律知识图谱中的关系结构。实验结果表明，引入基于图的关系可使检索召回率相比标准基线提升最多8个百分点，凸显了结构化法律表示对RAG系统的重要性。所提出的基准为波斯语中基于证据的法律推理评估提供了标准化资源，并推动未来在结构化法律智能领域的研究。

SimAI: Mechanistic User Simulation through Psychological Network Models
SimAI：通过心理网络模型实现机制性用户模拟
Realistic user simulation enables the study of human interactions when direct experimentation is costly or potentially harmful. However, existing simulators largely overlook the psychological mechanisms that drive user behavior. We introduce SimAI, a framework for mechanistic user simulation that models how psychological factors interact to shape user responses. To overcome the challenge of supervising open-ended behavior, we propose a graph reconstruction objective that provides graph-level rewards based on how well simulated conversations reflect the underlying psychological relationships. To support this approach, we construct PSYCHE, a dataset of 18,576 profile-network pairs derived from 516 psychotherapy cases. Automatic evaluations show that SimAI achieves greater simulation and behavioral diversity than existing patient simulators while maintaining strong profile alignment. In an interactive study with 30 mental-health professionals and trainees, SimAI receives the highest average ratings for conversational authenticity, realistic help-seeking behavior, and clinical training utility. Beyond simulation quality, SimAI enables realistic clinical training and uncovers safety failures in prolonged AI interactions with vulnerable users. Code is available at https://github.com/behavioral-data/ANGEL-UserSim.

Disambiguating Moral Circuits and Moral Features in Language Models: Moral content, normative judgment or cue words? Evidence from 14 models
解构语言模型中的道德电路与道德特征：道德内容、规范判断还是提示词？来自14个模型的证据
Interpretability methods can locate the attention heads and sparse autoencoder (SAE) features that change the answer a language model gives to a moral prompt, e.g., whether "Stealing medicine for a dying child is morally" continues with "justified" or "wrong". Such a component could respond to moral content, to the judgment of conduct by any norm, or to the word "morally" in the prompt. We built 227 minimal pairs of short phrases, validated by five annotators, whose two versions differ in a moral standard, in another kind of norm (prudence, etiquette, aesthetics or evidence) or in a descriptive quality, and showed them to 14 open-weight models (Llama, Gemma and Qwen, 1B to 14B, base and instruction-tuned), with and without the word "morally". In every model, the heads that change the moral answer belong to an evaluation circuit the models use for every kind of normative judgment we tested: these heads carry the same share of the effect for moral as for other normative pairs (pooled ratio 1.00, 97.5% confidence interval [0.92, 1.07], with the word; 0.90, [0.84, 0.96], without it), and the models need them as much for both. The models need these heads also when nothing in the prompt asks for a judgment, and on 400 scenarios of the ETHICS benchmark, where removing them takes away 37 to 39% of the difference between acceptable and unacceptable scenarios, against 1 to 3% for ten random heads. The SAE features we located respond mostly to the word "morally" rather than to what the phrase is about. The models represent the category of a pair (the standard the annotators assigned to it): it can be read from their hidden states, and, in the eight models we tested, removing the direction that carries it weakens the moral verdict by a few percent, and more than the other verdicts in seven of them. We release the pairs, the ratings, the code, the trained SAE dictionaries and the result files.

可解释性方法可以定位改变语言模型对道德提示回答的注意力头和稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征，例如“为垂死的孩子偷药在道德上”是接“合理”还是“错误”。这种组件可能响应道德内容、任何规范性判断的行为，或提示中的“道德上”一词。我们构建了227组最小对比短语对，经五名标注者验证，其两个版本在道德标准、其他规范（审慎、礼仪、美学或证据）或描述性特征上有所不同，并向14个开源模型（Llama、Gemma和Qwen，1B至14B，基础版和指令微调版）展示，无论是否包含“道德上”一词。在每个模型中，改变道德答案的注意力头属于模型用于所有规范性判断的评估电路：这些头在道德与其他规范对上的效应占比相同（合并比率为1.00，97.5%置信区间[0.92, 1.07]，含词；0.90，[0.84, 0.96]，不含词），且模型对两者同样依赖。即使提示中未要求判断，模型也需要这些头；在ETHICS基准的400个场景中，移除它们会消除37至39%可接受与不可接受场景之间的差异，而随机头仅减少1至3%。我们定位到的SAE特征主要响应“道德上”一词而非短语内容。模型能表示一对的类别（标注者分配的标准）：可从隐藏状态读取，且在我们测试的八个模型中，移除承载该类别的方向会使道德判断减弱几百分点，且在七种情况下比其他判断削弱更多。我们公开了成对样本、评分、代码、训练好的SAE词典和结果文件。

From Preference Data to Personalization: Tracing Sycophancy in Large Language Models
从偏好数据到个性化：追踪大语言模型中的阿谀奉承现象
Sycophancy in large language models is often measured as agreement with the user, yet agreement can be appropriate when the user's claim is valid, their preference is genuine, or their experience warrants acknowledgment. We study sycophancy as inappropriate agreement: validating a claim, preference, or self-assessment when the context calls for correction, qualification, or resistance. We introduce a validity-aware sycophancy grader and SycoDrift, a persona-targeted evaluation set of 3,298 validity-annotated probes with controlled annotations for user openness and stakes. We use this framework to analyze three sources of agreement pressure: human preference data, automated judge specification, and in-context personalization. First, preference for validating responses in human datasets (Chatbot Arena, HH-RLHF, Community Alignment) is weak and concentrated where agreement is often appropriate. Second, in a selected-value, single-model experiment, emphasized judge rubrics produce consistent but modest increases relative to paired suppressed rubrics, demonstrating a plausible mechanism for training-time amplification. Third, in off-policy evaluations across 14 models, inference-time personalization increases inappropriate agreement, with the largest effect from matched user profiles placed directly before the query.
中文翻译
大语言模型中的阿谀奉承常被衡量为对用户的同意程度，但当用户主张合理、偏好真实或经历值得认可时，同意可能是恰当的。我们研究的是不当同意：在上下文要求纠正、限定或抵制时仍验证一个主张、偏好或自我评估。我们引入了一个具备有效性感知的阿谀奉承评分器，以及SycoDrift——一个包含3,298个有效性标注探针的、针对特定人格的评估集，其中对用户开放性和利害关系进行了受控标注。我们利用此框架分析三种导致同意压力的来源：人类偏好数据、自动裁判规范和上下文个性化。首先，在人类数据集（Chatbot Arena、HH-RLHF、Community Alignment）中，验证性回应的偏好较弱且集中在通常应同意的场景。其次，在选定值、单模型实验中，强调裁判标准相比成对抑制标准带来一致但适度的提升，证明了训练期间放大的可能机制。第三，在跨14个模型的离策略评估中，推理阶段的个性化增加了不当同意，其中影响最大的是直接置于查询前的匹配用户画像。

Can an LLM dream? Testing the overfitted brain hypothesis on an agent with text memory
大语言模型能做梦吗？在具有文本记忆的智能体上检验过拟合大脑假说
Hoel (2021) proposed that dreams distort replayed experience so that the brain does not overfit to recent events. We test this hypothesis on an LLM agent (Claude Opus 5.5) that stores what it learns in a text memory and rewrites that memory each night from the day's conversation transcript. The agent goes through ten simulated days of conversation built from LongMemEval. We corrupt 5, 10, 20 or 40 percent of the transcript's turns before each nightly rewrite in one of two ways. Detail corruption changes one or two details in a turn to plausible wrong ones, or replaces the turn with one by the same speaker from an earlier day. Random-text corruption replaces half of the selected turns with a passage of Moby-Dick and inserts Moby-Dick words into the other half. A size limit on the memory, rising from 19,500 to 117,000 tokens over ten nights, set from human free-recall data, forces it to forget. We learned that corruption did not help the agent apply what it learned in earlier conversations to new tasks in any of 30 runs (ten conditions, three different conversation histories). Replacing details in 40 percent of turns lowered recall from 76 to 60 percent. Random text had no consistent effect on recall, with a paired difference of 0 $\pm$ 2 points at 40 percent, because the model recognized it as corrupted and left it out of memory. Replaced details were stored as fact even when another turn held the correct value, and the agent answered 40 percent of those questions correctly, compared with 75 percent without corruption. These results suggest that a pretrained model can separate corrupted text from real events, so a fair test needs a learner that cannot make this separation as easily, such as a model fine-tuned on the corrupted days or an agent whose days take place in an invented world.
中文翻译
Hoel（2021）提出，梦境通过扭曲重放的经历来防止大脑对近期事件产生过拟合。我们在此对一个存储学习内容于文本记忆的大语言模型智能体（Claude Opus 5.5）测试了这一假说，该智能体每天晚上根据当日对话记录重写其记忆。我们让该智能体经历由LongMemEval构建的十天模拟对话。在每次夜间重写前，以两种方式之一对对话记录的5%、10%、20%或40%进行篡改：细节篡改将某一轮中的一个或两个细节改为看似合理的错误信息，或用同一位说话者早前一天的轮次替换；随机文本篡改则将选定轮次的一半替换为《白鲸》段落，并在另一半中插入《白鲸》词汇。记忆大小限制从19,500增至117,000 token，依据人类自由回忆数据设定，迫使智能体遗忘。在30次运行中（十种条件，三种不同对话历史），篡改并未帮助智能体将早期对话中学到的内容应用于新任务。在40%轮次中修改细节后，回忆率从76%降至60%。随机文本篡改对回忆率无一致影响，40%水平下配对差异为0±2分，因为模型识别出其为篡改并将其排除在记忆之外。被替换的细节即使其他轮次存在正确值，仍被存储为事实，智能体对此类问题的回答正确率为40%，而无篡改情况下为75%。这些结果表明，预训练模型能够区分篡改文本与真实事件，因此公平测试需使用难以做出这种区分的学习者，如在篡改日上微调的模型，或在虚构世界中运行的智能体。

FieldGuide: A Corpus Map for Token-Efficient Agentic Document Exploration
FieldGuide：面向高效代理文档探索的语料库地图
Generative AI agents can help journalists explore large document collections. But exploratory workflows can require substantial token use and time, especially when the task is broad and the relevant subquestions are not yet known. This paper introduces FieldGuide, an agent-facing corpus map that organizes a document collection into a topic hierarchy and exposes tools for corpus orientation, scoped search, and bounded reading. I evaluate FieldGuide on five journalistic document-exploration tasks using 38 Chicago Police Department public-records documents, comparing it with raw-file access and retrieval-augmented generation. Across the five tasks, FieldGuide used 27% fewer tokens and 41% less elapsed time than raw-file access; relative to RAG, it used 38% fewer tokens and 21% less elapsed time. An accuracy audit identified four minor issues in FieldGuide's responses, comparable to the other conditions. In blind ratings of generated reporting leads, FieldGuide's mean ratings were higher than RAG's and lower than the raw-file condition's. These descriptive results suggest that corpus orientation may reduce runtime token use and elapsed time without severe accuracy failures. They also motivate further study of whether selective access limits broad, cross-document discovery.

Mind the Gap: A Deployed Multi-Agent System for Documentation Maintenance
关注差距：用于文档维护的部署式多智能体系统
The "AI revolution" has enabled teams across the technology industry to deliver software features at unprecedented velocity. While code evolves faster than ever, customer-facing documentation often struggles to stay up to speed. As users increasingly rely on LLM-based agents that consume documentation as their primary interface, the impact of this documentation gap worsens: stale information propagates and is treated as authoritative. Signals for identifying where documentation has fallen behind already exist in abundance, both explicit (e.g., resolved support tickets revealing what documentation lacks) and implicit (e.g., code-documentation discrepancies detectable from automated loops). We deploy a multi-agent system, built around a triage agent we call "TriAgent," that continuously extracts these signals and repairs documentation drift. Over 43 days of shadow-mode operation, our system triaged 499 tickets and surfaced 92 gaps in the documentation. Using a panel of LLM judges from independent model families, 81.1% of TriAgent's verifiable assertions were correct (245 of the 302 assertions the human resolution addresses; majority of three judges); on length-comparable pairs the panel preferred TriAgent's response in 70.5% of pairs and the human engineer's in 23.2% (the rest had no judge majority). Importantly, agent- and human-sourced replies tended to be complementary, with the human often surfacing insights that were either buried within the existing documentation or missing from it entirely; our system turns each such insight into a documentation gap and, after human review, a fix. Repairing a gap measurably improved the agent's later answer: when the agent re-answered the same ticket after the repair, blinded judges preferred the post-repair answer over its own pre-repair answer 71.4% of the time (n=35). A blinded human audit confirms strong alignment on self-improvement.
中文翻译
“人工智能革命”使整个科技行业团队以前所未有的速度交付软件功能。尽管代码演进速度前所未有，但面向客户的技术文档却常常难以跟上步伐。随着用户越来越多地依赖基于大语言模型的智能体作为主要接口，文档滞后问题愈发严重：过时信息被传播并被视为权威。识别文档滞后信号已大量存在，包括显式信号（如已解决的支持工单揭示文档缺失内容）和隐式信号（如可由自动化循环检测出的代码-文档差异）。我们部署了一个多智能体系统，核心是一个名为“TriAgent”的分类代理，持续提取这些信号并修复文档漂移。在为期43天的影子模式运行中，系统处理了499个工单，发现了92处文档缺口。通过来自独立模型家族的LLM评审小组评估，TriAgent可验证断言的正确率达81.1%（在302个由人工解决的断言中，245个被判定正确；多数三位评审员一致）；在长度相近的对比对中，评审组更偏好TriAgent的回答占比70.5%，而人类工程师的回答仅占23.2%（其余无多数意见）。重要的是，智能体与人类提供的回复往往互补，人类常能揭示隐藏于现有文档中或完全缺失的信息；我们的系统将每一条此类洞察转化为文档缺口，并经人工审核后形成修复。修复缺口后，智能体后续回答明显改善：当智能体在修复后重新回答同一工单时，盲测评审者在71.4%的情况下更偏好修复后的答案（n=35）。盲测人工审计确认了系统在自我改进方面的强一致性。

The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer?
接地的幻觉：视觉-语言模型是否像理想观察者一样权衡检索到的文本与图像？
Retrieval-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) answer from two sources of evidence that can each fail: the retrieved text can be wrong or noisy, and the image can be degraded or contradicted. We introduce GIB-200K, 200,000 image-question items built from COCO and Visual Genome, each expanded into controlled stress conditions: hard-negative retrieval noise (1-20 documents), visual ambiguity (Gaussian and motion blur, low-resource crops) and cross-modal conflict with counterfactual flip pairs. Instead of only measuring degradation, we ask how models combine the two modalities. From ideal Bayesian cue combination we derive a testable prediction that yields formal definitions of Knowledge Inertia (text over-weighted relative to the model's own image evidence) and Sensory Capture (the reverse), and we estimate the weights with a measurement-error-corrected regression whose recovery of known weights we verify. A counterfactual flip decomposition separates modality bias from concept priors, and a Grounding Illusion Score measures how much clean accuracy is not grounded in the image. We evaluate 4 open models on one frozen, seeded evaluation split of 2,000 GIB-200K items. All models are accurate on clean inputs (92.5%-94.2%) but adverse accuracy falls to 41.3%-59.9%. The two LLaVA models over-weight the text (Knowledge Inertia, w_T/w_I = 1.229 and 1.322), whereas InternVL3 favours the image (w_T/w_I = 0.680); between 34.8% and 71.4% of the clean answers that are correct are abandoned when the document lies. A training-free consistency check that compares the answer with and without retrieval trades coverage for accuracy under misleading documents, while falling back to the image fails when the image is heavily degraded. All per-instance outputs, code, and an audit that recomputes every reported open-model number are released.
中文翻译
检索增强型视觉-语言模型（VLMs）从两个可能失效的证据源中作答：检索到的文本可能错误或嘈杂，图像可能退化或矛盾。我们引入GIB-200K，一个包含20万张图像-问题样本的数据集，源自COCO和Visual Genome，每个样本扩展为受控压力条件：硬负样本检索噪声（1-20篇文档）、视觉模糊（高斯模糊和运动模糊、低资源裁剪）以及反事实翻转对带来的跨模态冲突。我们不仅测量性能退化，更关注模型如何融合两种模态。基于理想贝叶斯线索组合，我们推导出可检验的预测，并正式定义了知识惯性（相对于模型自身图像证据，文本权重过高）和感官捕获（相反情况）。我们采用校正测量误差的回归方法估计权重，并验证其恢复已知权重的能力。反事实翻转分解将模态偏差与概念先验分离，接地幻觉评分衡量干净准确率中有多少并非真正基于图像。我们在GIB-200K的一个冻结种子测试集（2,000项）上评估了4个开源模型。所有模型在干净输入下表现良好（准确率92.5%-94.2%），但在不利条件下准确率降至41.3%-59.9%。两个LLaVA模型过度依赖文本（知识惯性，w_T/w_I = 1.229 和 1.322），而InternVL3则更倾向图像（w_T/w_I = 0.680）；在文档错误时，34.8%至71.4%的原始正确答案被放弃。一种无需训练的一致性检查方法（比较有无检索时的答案）在误导文档下以牺牲覆盖率换取准确性，而当图像严重退化时回退到图像也失效。所有实例输出、代码及重新计算每个报告数值的审计结果均已公开。

Large-scale Neuron Community Discovery Reveals Neuron-Sparse Behaviors in Language Models
大规模神经元社区发现揭示语言模型中的神经元稀疏行为
What reusable units of computation can we discover within a language model's neurons? Prior work has shown that individual forward passes can often be sparsified post-hoc to small groups of neurons, but it has been unclear whether these groups form coherent computational entities reused across inputs. We introduce neuron community discovery, an unsupervised method for finding neuron groups that recur across prompts and are causally necessary and sufficient, within the layers they span, for common computations. The method combines gradient-based attribution, a graph connecting neurons that are repeatedly attributed together, and community detection. Applied to Llama-3.1-8B using ten million prompts, it discovers thousands of candidate communities. We evaluate them on held-out prompts through three causal tests: community necessity, sufficiency within a layer subset, and layer necessity. These tests identify groups whose removal disrupts predictions and whose retention preserves predictions despite ablating the remaining MLP neurons in the tested layers. Discovered communities support functions like grammatical agreement, token-probability calibration, fixed phrases, in-context copying, addition, and multiplication. With communities capped at 128 neurons across all layers, 10.3% of held-out prompts have at least one layer's contribution explained on a neuron-sparse basis. Coverage remains 8.7% when requiring at least four layers and 3.3% when requiring at least twelve. These results show that small groups of existing neurons can provide reusable computational components across prompts, and that such groups can be discovered at scale.
中文翻译
我们能在语言模型的神经元中发现哪些可复用的计算单元？以往研究显示，单次前向传播通常可以事后稀疏化为少量神经元组，但这些神经元组是否在不同输入间形成可重复使用的、连贯的计算实体仍不明确。我们提出了神经元社区发现方法，这是一种无监督技术，用于识别在多个提示中反复出现且在所跨越层内具有因果必要性和充分性的神经元组。该方法结合了基于梯度的归因、将频繁共同被归因的神经元连接成图，以及社区检测。在使用一千万个提示对Llama-3.1-8B进行测试时，发现了数千个候选社区。通过三种因果测试（社区必要性、层内子集充分性、层必要性）在保留提示上评估这些社区：移除这些社区会破坏预测，而保留它们即使在剔除测试层中其余MLP神经元的情况下仍能维持预测。发现的社区支持语法一致、词元概率校准、固定短语、上下文复制、加法和乘法等功能。当所有层的社区规模限制在128个神经元以内时，有10.3%的保留提示至少有一个层的贡献可通过神经元稀疏方式解释；当要求至少四个层时覆盖率降至8.7%，要求至少十二个层时降至3.3%。结果表明，现有神经元的小群体可在不同提示间提供可复用的计算组件，且这类群体可大规模发现。

Storing the Gemma 4 K=V Cache Once: Exact Global-KV Memory Savings in a Paged Serving Engine with Unchanged Attention Kernels
一次性存储Gemma 4 K=V缓存：在页式服务引擎中实现精确全局KV内存节省且无需改变注意力核函数
In the global attention layers of Gemma 4 31B, the value is the normalized key projection, and the key is the value scaled by a fixed weight, with rotary position encoding on one quarter of its dimensions. The Gemma 4 report states that this design reduces the global KV cache by up to 37.5%, but the open serving engines that we checked still store a full key and a full value: 1,024 values per KV head and token. We store one 640-value row instead, exact in real arithmetic, and an unchanged paged attention kernel reads it through two overlapping strided views as its key cache and its value cache. In a vLLM prototype with the 16-bit 31B model, the outputs are bit-identical to an emulation of the same arithmetic on the stock cache, 2 of 432 final answers differ from the stock engine (within its run-to-run noise), and the steady decode KV memory per request falls by a factor of 1.34 to 1.40 at 32K to 64K tokens. On a prefill/decode pair of two A100 GPUs with NIXL transfer and CPU offload, a decode engine with a full 15 GiB pool ran 1.38 times the stock decode tokens per step for 16K-token prompts (repeated to 0.06%) and 1.24 times for 8K-token prompts; a model of the vLLM block pool predicted both ratios to within 1% (the engine was 0.7% to 0.9% higher) and matched the change between them. The eager decode throughput rose by 1.30 and 1.11 times, and on this path the compact engine gave the same output tokens as the emulation for 8 prompts at batch 1. The run-time prototype makes an eager step 4% to 9% slower at equal batch, and the method does not yet stack with an 8-bit KV cache at the default scale.

在Gemma 4 31B的全局注意力层中，值是归一化键投影，键是值乘以固定权重，且在四分之一维度上应用旋转位置编码。Gemma 4报告称该设计可将全局KV缓存减少最多37.5%，但我们检查的开源服务引擎仍存储完整的键和完整的值：每KV头和每标记1024个值。我们改为存储一行640个值，实数运算下完全精确，且不变的页式注意力核函数通过两个重叠的步进视图将其分别作为键缓存和值缓存读取。在vLLM原型中使用16位31B模型，输出与相同算术的原始缓存模拟比特完全一致，432个最终答案中有2个与原始引擎不同（在其运行噪声范围内），在32K至64K标记的稳定解码阶段，每请求的KV内存下降1.34至1.40倍。在配备NIXL传输和CPU卸载的两块A100 GPU上的预填充/解码对中，具有完整15 GiB池的解码引擎在16K标记提示下每步解码速度为原始引擎的1.38倍（重复至0.06%），在8K标记提示下为1.24倍；vLLM块池模型预测这两个比率均在1%以内（引擎实际高出0.7%至0.9%），并准确匹配两者之间的变化。急切解码吞吐量分别提升了1.30和1.11倍，沿此路径紧凑引擎在批量为1时与模拟输出相同。运行时原型在相同批量下使急切步骤慢4%至9%，且该方法目前尚未与默认尺度下的8位KV缓存叠加。

CrossEnv: Synthesizing Verifiable Cross-Environment Tasks for Long-Horizon Agent Training
CrossEnv：用于长周期智能体训练的可验证跨环境任务合成
Executable synthetic environments provide scalable training tasks and verifiable feedback for long-horizon agents. Existing environment synthesis methods primarily generate tasks within individual environments. However, these tasks do not adequately capture real-world interactions across services, where actions in one service depend on information obtained from another. Synthesizing such tasks is challenging because data generated across environments must remain consistent to support executable and verifiable interactions. To address this challenge, we introduce CrossEnv, a framework that uses an entity-tool graph to identify connections between environments and aligns task-specific data to synthesize executable, verifiable cross-environment tasks. We further propose CrossEnv-Curriculum, which progresses from single-environment tool use to coordination across more environments and business domains, before introducing user interaction. Across four external benchmarks, combining cross-environment training data with this curriculum achieves competitive performance with fewer training examples. Further analyses support the benefits of cross-environment data and curriculum training, and provide evidence of transfer to new environment combinations, external tasks less similar to the training data, and longer workflows.

The Between-Round Decision in Autonomous Research: An LLM Controller That Reads the Whole Trajectory
自主研究中的回合间决策：一个读取完整轨迹的LLM控制器
Executing an idea often changes how promising it looks, so the next step in autonomous research should rest on results, not on proposals. After each round of experiments, the system must decide whether to refine the best solution or start a new direction, which solution to build from, and what goal to set. We call this the between-round decision. Existing systems wrap it in machinery such as rules, scores, search trees, and schedules; where an LLM makes it, what the LLM needs to read is left open. We ask whether an LLM can make this decision from accumulated evidence, without a search structure around it, and how much of that evidence it needs to read. We present BRED (Between-Round Evidence-based Decision), an LLM-based controller that maintains the best verified solution so far (the Champion) and a log of past rounds, and makes one decision per round. On three widely used research-agent benchmark tasks, BRED improves on the task baselines by 4.80% in MAE on ETTh1 (to 0.4172) and by 3.44 and 3.43 points of accuracy on SST-2 (to 95.07%) and CIFAR-100 (to 84.63%), ahead of the published results. When the controller does not read the accumulated evidence, the same loop gains less: with no history it reaches 0.4299 on ETTh1 and 83.71% on CIFAR-100, whereas the full controller's MAE is 2.95% lower and its accuracy 0.92 points higher. On a new task, speech-based depression detection under a leakage-free protocol, it reaches a macro-F1 of 0.6164 on seeds held out from selection, 5.5 points above its baseline and 4.0 above the strongest published system. Code is available at https://github.com/learninginvision/BRED.
中文翻译
执行一个想法往往会改变其前景，因此自主研究中的下一步应基于结果而非提案。每次实验后，系统需决定是否优化最佳解决方案、开启新方向、选择哪个方案作为基础以及设定什么目标。我们称此为回合间决策。现有系统通常通过规则、评分、搜索树或调度机制封装该决策；当由LLM做出时，LLM需要阅读的内容仍不明确。我们探讨LLM能否在无搜索结构的情况下，仅基于累积证据做出该决策，以及需要阅读多少证据。我们提出BRED（基于回合间证据的决策）——一种基于LLM的控制器，它维护迄今为止的最佳已验证解决方案（冠军）和过往回合日志，并每轮做出一次决策。在三个广泛使用的研究代理基准任务上，BRED在ETTh1上的MAE提升4.80%（降至0.4172），在SST-2和CIFAR-100上的准确率分别提升3.44和3.43个百分点（分别达到95.07%和84.63%），优于已发表结果。当控制器不读取累积证据时，相同循环的表现下降：无历史记录时，ETTh1的MAE为0.4299，CIFAR-100准确率为83.71%，而完整控制器的MAE低2.95%，准确率高0.92个百分点。在一项新的泄漏无关协议下的语音抑郁检测任务中，其宏F1达到0.6164（在未参与选择的种子上），比基线高出5.5点，比最强已发表系统高出4.0点。代码可在https://github.com/learninginvision/BRED 获取。

Evolution Under Fire: Evolving Agent Safety Harnesses with Adaptive Injection Stressors
战火中的进化：通过自适应注入压力器演化智能体安全防护罩
Tool-using language-model agents must act on information from untrusted emails, webpages, and documents. This creates a central security challenge: malicious instructions and legitimate task directives can arrive through the same interaction channel. Existing defenses are largely hand-designed and static, making further improvements dependent on repeated manual redesign. We introduce Evolution Under Fire, a staged framework that couples safety-harness evolution with adaptive stressor evolution. Utility-constrained, Pareto-scheduled tree search evolves complete executable safety harnesses from execution evidence while keeping the task model, benchmark, tools, and evaluator fixed. Adaptive stressor evolution then discovers utility-preserving attacks that guide a subsequent stage of harness evolution. On AgentDyn with GPT-4o-mini, an evolved harness reduces attack success from 50.4% to 3.0%, improves clean and attacked utility over the initial harness, and offers a stronger security-utility trade-off than reproduced defenses. On Agent-SafetyBench with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, harness evolution reduces attack success from 82.6% to 62.0% while retaining helpfulness and runtime validity. Evolved harnesses also transfer without further evolution to Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, reducing attack success from 32.1% to 0.9% while improving attacked-task utility.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型智能体必须处理来自不可信邮件、网页和文档的信息。这带来核心安全挑战：恶意指令与合法任务指令可能通过同一交互通道到达。现有防御措施大多为手工设计且静态，导致后续改进依赖反复的人工重设计。我们提出“战火中的进化”——一种分阶段框架，将安全防护罩的演化与自适应压力源的演化相结合。在效用约束下，采用帕累托调度的树搜索，从执行证据中演化出完整的可执行安全防护罩，同时保持任务模型、基准、工具和评估器不变。随后，自适应压力源演化发现保留效用的攻击方式，引导下一阶段的防护罩演化。在AgentDyn上使用GPT-4o-mini，演化后的防护罩将攻击成功率从50.4%降至3.0%，提升了清洁与受攻击状态下的效用，并在安全-效用权衡上优于复现的现有防御。在Agent-SafetyBench上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，防护罩演化将攻击成功率从82.6%降至62.0%，同时保持有用性和运行时有效性。演化后的防护罩无需进一步演化即可迁移到Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，将攻击成功率从32.1%降至0.9%，同时提升受攻击任务的效用。
👤 Yujun Wang, Yilun Liu, Jinhe Bi, Krishna Kanth Nakka, Xuebing Zhou et al. 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

From Perception to Simulation: Unveiling and Empowering Mental Spatial Dynamics in MLLMs
从感知到仿真：揭示并赋能多模态大模型中的心理空间动态
Spatial reasoning is a fundamental component of world understanding for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing research has advanced the perception of spatial relations in static scenes, the domain of dynamic spatial simulation remains relatively unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce MentalGrid, a benchmark focused on spatial reasoning tasks that require models to simulate evolving spatial states without intermediate visual feedback. Extensive evaluation on our benchmark reveals a significant deficiency in the spatial simulation capabilities of existing models. Specifically, we find that performance is constrained by perceptual limitations in simple simulation scenarios and hindered by inadequate multi-step reasoning as complexity increases. To address these challenges, we propose a three-stage curriculum learning framework utilizing 16k specialized spatial reasoning samples. This approach yields SpatialWalker, which achieves a 33.8% average improvement over its base model, surpassing proprietary models like Gemini-3-Flash in spatial simulation. Furthermore, SpatialWalker demonstrates robust generalization, maintaining a 4.9% average gain on out-of-domain benchmarks, demonstrating the pivotal role of spatial simulation as a fundamental building block for broader spatial intelligence.

When Agent Skills Go Stale: Runtime Behavior and Selective Maintenance in Evolving Environments
当智能体技能过时：动态环境中的运行时行为与选择性维护
Agent skills use past experience and current observations to guide decisions and actions on related tasks. This experience is retained in skill state, whose reuse can amortize inference-time reasoning and environment interaction. When environment changes, parts of this state can become outdated while others remain useful. Across four task settings and two models, we compare skill reuse before and after environment changes. Previously beneficial skills can reduce task performance by over 10 percentage points or increase execution cost by nearly 40% relative to no skill. Task success can also remain unchanged while execution savings decline. These effects depend on which environment-specific details become outdated and the agent's ability to compensate using current observations. We introduce selective maintenance, which replays checks from successful executions without additional model calls. It verifies whether observed differences conflict with skill state, repairs affected content, and validates updates, rebuilding when local repair is insufficient. No description of the change is supplied. Relative to reusing stale state, maintenance improves task accuracy by up to 67pp and reduces execution steps by up to 37%. These results suggest that preserving a skill's inference time value as environments evolve requires selectively maintaining its state.
中文翻译
智能体技能利用过往经验与当前观测来指导相关任务的决策与行动。这些经验保留在技能状态中，其复用可分摊推理时间和环境交互成本。当环境发生变化时，部分状态可能过时而其他部分仍具价值。在四个任务设置和两种模型下，我们比较了环境变化前后技能复用的效果。先前有益的技能可能导致任务性能下降超过10个百分点，或使执行成本增加近40%（相对于不使用技能的情况）。任务成功率也可能保持不变，但执行节省却减少。这些影响取决于哪些环境特异性细节过时以及智能体利用当前观测进行补偿的能力。我们提出选择性维护机制，通过重放成功执行中的检查点而不额外调用模型，验证观测差异是否与技能状态冲突，修复受影响内容并验证更新；当局部修复不足时则重建状态。该方法无需提供变更描述。相比复用过时状态，维护机制可将任务准确率提升最高达67个百分点，并将执行步骤减少最多37%。结果表明，在环境演进过程中，为保持技能的推理价值，需有选择地维护其状态。

LMLineage: Content Lineage and Context Governance for LLM Agents
LMLineage：大语言模型智能体的内容溯源与上下文治理
Interpretability for AI agents should not be confined to understanding the model's internal weights. It must also cover the agent's reasoning and decision-making process over multi-source context. We present LMLineage, an out-of-band system that attributes every output sentence of an agent session to its supporting input spans with calibrated confidences, without requiring white-box access to the model or any additional inference passes. On top of the lineage metadata, we build concept-drift micro compaction: instead of waiting for the context-length limit and summarizing everything, the system detects when previously used input documents have stopped being referenced ("drifted") and surgically compacts only those, preserving user instructions verbatim. We evaluate on a multi-tier corpus: 71 gold annotated sentences from 10 Chinese web-QA cases, plus 90 turns of independently collected multi-turn sessions across three generator models (gpt-4o-mini, qwen3.8-27b, deepseek-v4-flash). Results show that (i) window-level embedding similarity is far better aligned with LLM paraphrasing than whole document or lexical matching; (ii) the greedy-window fast path is both faster and more accurate on average than exhaustive line-matching (a multiple-comparisons bias in the latter inflates web recall at the cost of overall accuracy); (iii) per-turn attribution completes in 1s on a single GPU, remaining stable as context accumulates to 40 turns; and (iv) concept-drift detection is conservative (0% false compaction) with recall bounded by attribution accuracy. We further describe the concept-drift micro compaction design and an interactive incident-tracing/memory-cleaning case study. A companion patent application covers the lineage construction method.
中文翻译
AI智能体的可解释性不应局限于理解模型内部权重，还应涵盖其在多源上下文下的推理与决策过程。我们提出LMLineage，一种非侵入式系统，可在不需白盒访问模型或额外推理步骤的情况下，将智能体会话中的每个输出句子归因于其支持的输入片段，并提供校准置信度。基于溯源元数据，我们构建概念漂移微压缩机制：不等待上下文长度限制再进行整体摘要，而是检测先前使用的输入文档何时停止被引用（即“漂移”），仅对这些文档进行精准压缩，同时完整保留用户指令。我们在多层级语料上进行评估：包含10个中文网络问答案例的71个标注句子，以及三个生成模型（gpt-4o-mini、qwen3.8-27b、deepseek-v4-flash）独立收集的90轮多轮对话。结果表明：(i) 窗口级嵌入相似性比全文或词法匹配更贴近LLM的改写行为；(ii) 快速贪心窗口路径在平均速度和准确性上均优于穷尽式行匹配（后者因多重比较偏差导致网页召回率虚高，牺牲整体准确率）；(iii) 每轮归因可在单个GPU上1秒内完成，随上下文累积至40轮仍保持稳定；(iv) 概念漂移检测保守（0%误压缩），召回率受归因准确率限制。我们进一步描述了概念漂移微压缩设计及交互式事件追溯/记忆清理案例研究。配套专利申请涵盖溯源构建方法。

Surfacing Disagreement Beats Resolving It: A Gated Ablation Study of Provenance-Weighted Memory for Multi-Agent Debate
暴露分歧胜于解决分歧：面向多智能体辩论中溯源加权记忆的门控消融研究
Shared memory for multi-agent debate is usually built to resolve inconsistency: retrieval returns one winning fact and the disagreement disappears before any agent sees it. We ask whether that is correct. In DebateGraph, four backends share one contract (read/write/snapshot), so five mechanisms from the agent-memory literature can be removed independently under a fixed budget, each gated on a pre-declared threshold. Across 39 runs one survives: preserving contradictions and passing the ranked conflict set to the debate, rather than collapsing it to a winner, improves F1 by +0.340 (95% CI [+0.267, +0.414]) and cuts attack success from 22 to 7 of 122 poisoned items (McNemar exact p=0.0003), at equal cost. Reaching it meant fixing a bottleneck in our harness: conflict sets are built only over retrieved edges, and our pre-declared cap left three quarters of planted contradictions unretrievable, first measuring the mechanism at +0.084. The effect replicates on two retrieval substrates and directionally on HotpotQA documents. No other mechanism survives, and two are worse than inert: once contradictions reach the prompt, a write trust-gate and the router's provenance features each cost accuracy (-0.075 and -0.096 F1). The gate fails structurally: poisoned objects are always entities the graph already contains, against 53% of gold, so groundedness scores the lie above a truthful first mention. We release the harness, attack set, and logs, and argue that silently reconciling memory is the assumption worth abandoning.
中文翻译
多智能体辩论中的共享记忆通常旨在消除不一致：检索返回单一胜出事实，使分歧在任何智能体看到前就消失。我们质疑这一做法是否正确。在DebateGraph中，四个后端共享一个合约（读/写/快照），因此可在固定预算下独立移除来自智能体记忆文献的五种机制，每种机制由预声明阈值控制。在39次实验中，唯一存活的机制是：保留矛盾，并将排序后的冲突集传递给辩论，而非将其合并为单一胜者。该机制使F1提升+0.340（95%置信区间[+0.267, +0.414]），攻击成功率从122项中的22项降至7项（McNemar精确p=0.0003），且成本相同。达到此效果需解决我们框架中的瓶颈：冲突集仅基于检索到的边构建，而预设上限使得四分之三的植入矛盾无法被检索，首次测量时机制表现仅为+0.084。该效应在两种检索底座上均可复现，并在HotpotQA文档上呈现方向性结果。无其他机制存活，且两项机制甚至劣于无操作：一旦矛盾进入提示，写入信任门和路由器的溯源特征各自导致准确率下降（F1分别-0.075和-0.096）。门控结构失败：被污染对象始终是图中已存在的实体，占黄金数据的53%，因此根基性评分将谎言置于真实首次提及之上。我们发布框架、攻击集和日志，并主张应放弃沉默调和记忆这一假设。

Teaching Language Models to Narrow Boolean Patent Searches from Search Feedback
教导语言模型从搜索反馈中缩小布尔专利检索范围
Patent prior-art searchers issue Boolean queries line by line, broadening to find relevant documents and narrowing until candidates can be screened. We distill 38,397 Japan Patent Office search reports into executable expert strategies, re-execute them, and evaluate generated strategies by citation recall and the smallest set containing each citation. Prompted models write valid, high-recall strategies but leave citations in sets too large to screen. We adapt a generator offline from scored rollouts in two forms: strategy-level learning from a screening-aware strategy score, and line-level learning from citation-preserving narrowing credit, which upweights the query lines keeping a found citation in a smaller set. On shared rollouts, line-level credit outperforms strategy-level scores on screening measures; iterated, it cuts the median citation-containing set from 768 to under 50 and places more citations in sets under 100 documents than experts, at lower recall. Strategy-level preference learning reaches near-complete recall; the two forms are complementary.

Is 4-Bit Quantization More Than Noise to a Chat Model's Safety Behaviour? Attributing Behavioural Shifts with Matched Perturbations
4比特量化对聊天模型安全行为的影响：通过匹配扰动归因行为变化
Chat models on phones and laptops are usually 4-bit copies of the model that was evaluated, and audits of such copies disagree about what quantization does to refusal. A 4-bit build is one of many weight perturbations of the same size, so a change in its behaviour could come from its specific rounding error or from any perturbation that large. In a pre-registered study of three 3-4B chat models, with paired tests, blind human labels and an 8-bit null, we find two 4-bit shifts of opposite sign: with a compliant reply prefilled, Qwen2.5-3B continues into harmful content more often, and on held-out jailbreaks Llama-3.2-3B complies 10.3 points less often. Matched perturbations separate the two. Shuffled and Gaussian versions of the error average close to zero, while scaling the real error, from its mirror image to twice its size, changes behaviour near-linearly. At half the real error, Llama's likelihood is almost unchanged, yet it already complies 5.8 points less often, and llama.cpp's Q4_K_M, at the same bits per weight, moves neither model. Refusal under 4-bit quantization depends on the specific rounding error, not on bits per weight or likelihood, so each build has to be compared with its original.
中文翻译
手机和笔记本电脑上的聊天模型通常是经过评估模型的4比特版本，而对这些副本的安全审计结果存在分歧，关于量化如何影响拒绝行为尚无定论。4比特构建是同规模权重扰动的众多形式之一，因此其行为变化可能源于特定的舍入误差，也可能来自任何同等大小的扰动。在一项注册研究中，我们对三个3–4B聊天模型进行了成对测试、盲人人工标注和8比特对照组分析，发现两个4比特变化方向相反：在预填充合规回复的情况下，Qwen2.5-3B更频繁地继续生成有害内容；而在保留的越狱攻击测试中，Llama-3.2-3B的合规率降低了10.3个百分点。通过匹配扰动可分离这两种影响。打乱和高斯版本的误差平均接近零，而将真实误差从其镜像放大到两倍时，行为变化近似线性。在真实误差一半时，Llama的拒绝概率几乎不变，但其合规率已降低5.8个百分点；而llama.cpp的Q4_K_M在相同每权重比特数下，对任一模型均无影响。4比特量化的拒绝行为取决于具体舍入误差，而非每权重比特数或似然值，因此每个构建版本必须与其原始版本进行比较。

Long-Context Generation Is a Sampling Problem (When You Turn Up the Heat)
长上下文生成本质上是一个采样问题（当你提高温度时）
Open-weight language models are widely observed to get worse the longer they generate. Often, attempts to fix this involve more training. At high temperature, The sampler, i.e. the rule that picks the next token out of the model's probabilities, decides whether long and agentic generation survives and/or thrives. We test 29 open-weight models from 1B to 122B parameters on ten established benchmarks plus needle retrieval out to one million tokens, covering SWE-bench Verified coding agents, long chain-of-thought reasoning, long-form writing, long structured output and long-context reading. Samplers that keep their pool of candidate tokens small and steady as temperature rises don't lose accuracy. Samplers that let in low-probability tokens fall apart. At temperature 2, coding agents running top-p, top-k or Top-H resolve 0 of 100 GitHub issues, while the same agents running top-nsigma resolve 46 to 62 and win every issue the two samplers disagree on, on three Qwen3.5 models. Over all benchmarks top-nsigma keeps a median 100% of its temperature-1 score where top-p keeps 26%. Counting how many tokens each sampler lets through explains why. In one Llama-3.1-8B story, going from temperature 1 to 2 grows top-p's pool from 4 tokens to 51,845 while top-nsigma stays at 3. Across 133 combinations of sampler setting and temperature, the typical pool size predicts judged quality (Spearman correlation of -0.77), and when we pick the pool at temperature 1 and only apply temperature afterwards, Top-H, min-p and p-less stop collapsing. At the default temperature of 1 most samplers perform about the same, We release all code and data to the public.
中文翻译
开放权重语言模型在生成长度增加时通常表现下降。修复此问题的常见方法是更多训练。但在高温度下，采样器（即从模型概率中选择下一个词元的规则）决定了长时程、高自主性生成能否存活甚至繁荣。我们在十个已建立的基准测试及长达一百万词元的针堆检索任务上测试了29个参数规模从1B到122B的开放权重模型，涵盖SWE-bench Verified编码代理、长链式思维推理、长篇写作、长结构化输出和长上下文阅读。保持候选词池小且稳定的采样器在温度升高时不会损失准确性；而允许低概率词元进入的采样器则会崩溃。在温度2时，使用top-p、top-k或Top-H的编码代理在100个GitHub问题中解决0个，而使用top-nsigma的同一类代理解决了46至62个，并在两个采样器意见不一致的问题中全部胜出，覆盖三个Qwen3.5模型。在所有基准测试中，top-nsigma维持了其温度1得分的中位数100%，而top-p仅维持26%。统计每个采样器通过的词元数量可解释原因：在一个Llama-3.1-8B的故事中，温度从1升至2时，top-p的词元池从4个增至51,845个，而top-nsigma始终保持在3个。在133种采样器设置与温度组合中，典型词元池大小与人工评分质量呈显著负相关（Spearman相关系数-0.77）。当我们在温度1时确定词元池大小，仅在之后应用温度调整，Top-H、min-p和p-less不再崩溃。在默认温度1下，大多数采样器表现相近。我们已公开所有代码和数据。

[2611.22055]
agent-memoryagentic-rl
Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers
权重空间编程不需要Transformer
Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. In weight-space programming, a large language model (LLM) writes target weights by hand without gradient-based training, so those reasons no longer apply. Should we keep the Transformer? Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same LLM wrote while using about 1,800x fewer parameters. Without gradients, they choose a table, in which the LLM can state what each weight means and can find and rewrite a wrong one. Each wrong answer points to one row, and removing a row changes about one other answer. From this, we propose an associative-memory-based model up to 17x faster on GPUs than a same-sized Transformer. We also test pretrained weights, other benchmarks, and tasks where models generate answers. Pretrained LLMs remain more accurate; the claim concerns which structure to build when writing weights.
中文翻译
标准神经架构如Transformer之所以被采用，是因为它们能通过梯度下降有效训练并在GPU上快速运行。但在权重空间编程中，大型语言模型（LLM）手动编写目标权重，无需基于梯度的训练，因此这些理由不再适用。我们是否仍应保留Transformer？在仅有少量引导、十个标注示例和沙盒环境的前提下，我们让三个前沿LLM分别执行两项任务：生成固定架构的权重，或自行设计架构。当允许自由设计时，所有三个模型都将纯文本事实编译为由固定程序查询的稀疏表，最高测试准确率达54.4%。一个由LLM设计的模型在测试准确率上超过同一LLM用固定Transformer架构写出的最准确版本，且仅使用约1/1800的参数量。在无梯度的情况下，它们选择了一种表格结构，使LLM能够明确说明每个权重的含义，并可定位和修改错误权重。每个错误答案对应一行，移除一行大约影响另一个答案。基于此，我们提出一种基于关联记忆的模型，其在GPU上的速度比同尺寸Transformer快达17倍。我们还测试了预训练权重、其他基准和模型生成答案的任务。预训练LLM仍更准确；本研究关注的是在手动写权重时应构建何种结构。

SkillBuffBench: Measuring What Agent Skills Contribute across Harnesses, Models, Skill Access Modes and Memory
SkillBuffBench：跨Harness、模型、技能访问模式与记忆衡量代理技能贡献
Agent Skills, file-based packages of procedural knowledge that a coding agent can discover and load on demand, are now shipped by every major agent harness, yet the evidence for how much they help comes almost entirely from settings in which the right Skill is hand-picked for the task and the agent starts from a blank slate. Neither holds in deployment, where an agent chooses from whatever library happens to be installed and frequently carries memory from earlier work. We present SkillBuffBench, a benchmark and paired evaluation protocol that treats Skill access as a three-level factor (no Skill, a curated Skill, and automatic invocation from an installed library), crosses it with memory (off and on), with four agent harnesses and with multiple models (the state-of-the-art proprietary models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI, and the state-of-the-art open-weight models from Chinese providers), every model being run under every harness, and evaluates the result on research tasks contributed by domain experts together with the Skills they use, currently in materials science and biology, with the suite extended on a rolling basis. Every run is marked by a deterministic verifier; uncertainty is reported with a paired, task-level bootstrap; and cost, agent rounds and wall-clock time are reported alongside the score rather than folded into it. This version of the paper describes the design of the benchmark, its evaluation protocol and its current tasks and Skills. The evaluation is in progress: results will be added once the runs are complete, and the task suite, results and analysis will continue to be updated on a rolling basis.

代理技能——即文件形式的程序知识包，编码代理可按需发现并加载——如今已被各大代理Harness提供，但现有证据几乎完全来自理想化场景：任务中手动挑选正确技能，且代理从空白状态开始。然而在实际部署中，代理只能从当前安装的库中选择，且经常携带先前工作的记忆。我们提出SkillBuffBench，一个基准测试及其配套评估协议，将技能访问视为三级因素（无技能、精选技能、自动调用已安装库中的技能），与记忆状态（开启/关闭）、四种代理Harness以及多种模型（Anthropic、Google和OpenAI的前沿专有模型，以及中国厂商提供的前沿开源模型）交叉组合，每种模型在每种Harness下运行，并在领域专家提供的研究任务上评估结果，目前涵盖材料科学和生物学领域，任务套件将持续滚动扩展。每次运行均由确定性验证器标记；不确定性通过任务级配对Bootstrap报告；成本、代理轮次和墙钟时间与分数一同报告，而非合并进分数。本文描述了该基准的设计、评估协议及其当前任务与技能。评估正在进行中：待运行完成后将补充结果，任务套件、结果与分析将持续滚动更新。

Modeling Document and Section Hierarchies in Embedding Space
嵌入空间中文档与章节层次结构的建模
Embedded topic models integrate word embeddings into the topic generation process, producing coherent topics and robust performance on large vocabularies. However, each document is represented by a single topic distribution, ignoring its internal structure. Many real world collections, including legal documents, company and government reports, and online discussion forums, exhibit hierarchical organization in which documents consist of multiple sections that often address distinct subtopics. We introduce the Hierarchical Embedded Topic Model (hETM), a structured topic model that couples the topic distribution of a document and those of its sections through a hierarchical prior in the embedding space. We derive an amortized variational inference procedure for hETM and evaluate it on structured collections from each of these domains. Experimental results show that hETM achieves the lowest or near-lowest held-out perplexity among flat and hierarchical topic model baselines, along with comparable or higher topic quality. It also performs strongly on a downstream clustering task, achieving the best clustering performance on most of the collections.

嵌入主题模型将词嵌入融入主题生成过程，生成语义连贯的主题并在大规模词汇上表现稳健。然而，每篇文档仅用单一主题分布表示，忽略了其内部结构。现实世界中的许多文档集合，如法律文件、公司及政府报告、在线讨论论坛等，具有层次化组织特征，即文档由多个章节组成，通常涵盖不同的子主题。为此，我们提出层次嵌入主题模型（hETM），一种通过嵌入空间中的层次先验耦合文档及其各章节主题分布的结构化主题模型。我们推导了hETM的近似变分推断方法，并在来自上述各领域的结构化数据集上进行了评估。实验结果表明，hETM在所有平坦和层次化主题模型基线中实现了最低或接近最低的保留困惑度，同时保持相当或更高的主题质量。此外，在下游聚类任务中表现优异，在多数数据集中达到了最佳聚类性能。

It's My Turn! Interpretable Methods Reveal Developmental Differences from Mother-Child Conversational Dynamics
轮到我了！可解释方法揭示母子对话动态中的发展差异
Understanding conversational interactions between mothers and children is an important challenge for computational behavioral modeling. Existing methods use traditional statistical approaches and subjective measures of conversational dynamics, without incorporating temporal or probabilistic modeling. We developed an interpretable computational framework that combines sequence analysis, information-theoretic measures, and Bayesian hierarchical modeling to characterize temporal conversational dynamics in parent-child play. We applied this framework to interactions from 94 mother-child dyads with two-year-old children. Sequence analysis revealed that dyads with lower language development (as defined by scores on the Bayley developmental test) exhibited more heterogeneous conversational organization and fewer serve-and-return exchanges than higher-development dyads, a reciprocal turn-taking pattern in which one partner initiates communication and the other responds. Mutual information analyses suggested differences in conversational predictability between lower- and higher-development dyads, although the bootstrap confidence intervals overlapped. Bayesian hierarchical models estimated temporal trajectories for maternal consecutive turns, child responses following maternal speech, and overlapping responses; HDIs showed that trajectories differed in whether they excluded 0, but there were no statistically credible differences between the Bayley groups. Overall, these results indicate that children with higher developmental scores have more organized and predictable communication styles, with more ``serve-and-return" patterns with their mothers. These results demonstrate that interpretable computational representations of conversational dynamics capture developmental differences beyond simple measures of speech quantity.

理解母亲与儿童之间的对话互动是计算行为建模的重要挑战。现有方法依赖传统统计手段和主观的对话动态度量，缺乏时间序列或概率建模。我们开发了一种可解释的计算框架，结合序列分析、信息论度量与贝叶斯分层建模，以刻画亲子游戏中的时间对话动态。我们将该框架应用于94对两岁儿童母子的互动数据。序列分析显示，语言发展水平较低的母子对（根据贝利发育测试得分定义）表现出更异质的对话组织结构，且较少出现“发起-回应”交流模式（即一方发起沟通，另一方回应）。互信息分析提示低发展组与高发展组在对话可预测性上存在差异，尽管Bootstrap置信区间重叠。贝叶斯分层模型估计了母亲连续发言、儿童在母亲发言后的回应以及重叠回应的时间轨迹；HDIs显示轨迹是否排除0存在差异，但在贝利组间无统计上可信的差异。总体而言，这些结果表明，发育评分较高的儿童具有更有序、可预测的沟通风格，与母亲有更多的“发起-回应”互动。这些结果表明，可解释的对话动态计算表征能够捕捉超出简单言语数量测量的发展差异。
