Dynamic Task Allocation with Dialogue
基于对话的动态任务分配
Effective multi-agent collaboration requires agents to adapt task allocation as goals and responsibilities evolve. Prior task-allocation frameworks often assume static goals, full observability, or rely on large language models (LLMs) without explicit, verifiable beliefs about task allocation or intent. These assumptions limit their applicability to real-world collaboration, where agents often lack full information about each other's actions, progress, or local environment. To support dynamic task allocation under partial observability, we introduce a communication-augmented Bayesian task-allocation framework. Our agent uses dialogue to reduce uncertainty about the environment and collaborators, while keeping decisions grounded in explicit probabilistic beliefs. We evaluate our framework in an Overcooked domain extended with dynamically arriving tasks under both full and partial object-space observability. Our communication agent consistently outperforms a vanilla LLM agent in both settings. Additionally, our framework performs comparably to a non-communicating task-allocation baseline under full observability, but substantially outperforms it under partial observability as task complexity increases by an average speed up of 0.585 and completes on average 18 timesteps faster. These results indicate that our agent is more efficient in dynamic task allocation than baselines in partially observable environments, and that communication is important as available information decreases.

有效的多智能体协作需要智能体能够根据目标和责任的变化动态调整任务分配。以往的任务分配框架通常假设目标静态、可观测性完全，或依赖大型语言模型（LLMs）但缺乏关于任务分配或意图的明确、可验证信念。这些假设限制了其在现实协作场景中的适用性，因为在这些场景中，智能体往往无法全面了解彼此的行为、进展或局部环境。为支持在部分可观测条件下的动态任务分配，我们提出了一种通信增强型贝叶斯任务分配框架。我们的智能体通过对话减少对环境和协作者的不确定性，同时保持决策建立在明确的概率信念之上。我们在扩展了动态到达任务的Overcooked环境中评估了该框架，分别在完全可观测和部分可观测对象空间条件下进行测试。结果表明，我们的通信智能体在两种情况下均持续优于基线LLM智能体。此外，在完全可观测条件下，我们的框架表现与非通信任务分配基线相当，但在部分可观测条件下，随着任务复杂度增加，其性能显著更优，平均提速0.585，平均提前18个时间步完成任务。这些结果表明，我们的智能体在部分可观测环境中比基线更高效地完成动态任务分配，且随着可用信息减少，沟通的重要性愈发凸显。

Natural Language Programming: Rethinking Language, Memory, and Cognition in the Era of Generative AI
自然语言编程：生成式AI时代语言、记忆与认知的重新思考
Generative Artificial Intelligence is transforming the role of natural language in computational systems. In classical Natural Language Processing (NLP), language was treated as an object of computation: something to analyse, classify, translate, or generate. Large Language Models (LLMs) challenge this perspective, enabling natural language to function increasingly as an executable cognitive medium through which humans structure reasoning, orchestrate problem solving, and regulate hybrid human/AI workflows. This paper introduces Natural Language Programming (NLPg) as a theoretical and epistemological extension of traditional NLP. Rather than focusing on language understanding alone, NLPg conceptualises prompts as dynamic cognitive artefacts that externalise goals, constraints, assumptions, validation criteria, and intermediate reasoning states. Prompting is thus not merely a technical interaction technique, but a form of dialogic cognitive programming operating within distributed human/AI memory architectures. Building on theories of distributed cognition, extended mind, metacognition, and dialogic learning, the paper proposes the notion of Dialogic Working Memory, describing how prompts and conversational states function as temporary external memory structures supporting reasoning continuity and reflective regulation. We further map existing memory architectures (BTMA, SKGE, RMR) onto this framework, illustrating how episodic, semantic, and metacognitive layers support long-horizon conversational coherence. Finally, implications for computational linguistics and AI/mediated knowledge systems are discussed, suggesting a post symbolic and post interface paradigm in which natural language operates simultaneously as communication medium, executable specification, cognitive scaffold, and distributed memory architecture.
中文翻译
生成式人工智能正在重塑自然语言在计算系统中的角色。在传统的自然语言处理（NLP）中，语言被视为一种计算对象：用于分析、分类、翻译或生成的内容。大型语言模型（LLMs）挑战了这一观点，使自然语言越来越多地作为可执行的认知媒介，帮助人类组织推理、协调问题解决，并调控人机混合工作流程。本文提出自然语言编程（NLPg），作为传统NLP在理论和认识论上的延伸。NLPg不仅关注语言理解，更将提示视为动态的认知产物，外化目标、约束、假设、验证标准及中间推理状态。因此，提示不仅仅是技术交互手段，而是一种在分布式人机记忆架构中运行的对话式认知编程形式。基于分布式认知、扩展心智、元认知和对话学习等理论，本文提出了‘对话工作记忆’的概念，描述提示与对话状态如何作为临时外部记忆结构，支持推理连续性和反思性调节。我们进一步将现有的记忆架构（BTMA、SKGE、RMR）映射到该框架，展示情景、语义和元认知层次如何支持长时程对话连贯性。最后，讨论了对计算语言学和AI/媒介知识系统的启示，建议进入一个后符号、后界面的范式，在此范式中，自然语言同时作为通信媒介、可执行规范、认知支架和分布式记忆架构发挥作用。

TPHC: Improving Hyper-Connections with Tensor-Parallel Grouped Updates
TPHC：通过张量并行分组更新改进超连接
Hyper-Connections (HC) expand the residual stream of a Transformer into $N$ parallel streams, and manifold-constrained HC (mHC) stabilizes them at scale. However, mHC benefits little from more than four streams, partly because each sublayer writes a single output to all streams, limiting how differently they can be updated. In this work, we propose TPHC, which integrates tensor-parallel (TP) shards with HC streams to promote greater independence across streams. Specifically, we route a separate mixture of the residual streams to each TP shard and keep the individual shard outputs separate during write-back, so each stream can receive a different mixture of those outputs. This design makes the residual-stream updates more expressive without adding parameters or FLOPs to the attention or FFN sublayers. With native support for stream sharding under TP, TPHC requires only 9% more training time than mHC on an 8B model when both use four streams. Increasing the number of streams from four to sixteen raises its training time by only 19%. On a 1.1B model trained on 100B tokens, TPHC reduces validation loss by 0.7-1.0% and raises average zero-shot accuracy across six commonsense reasoning benchmarks by 0.3-0.7 percentage points relative to mHC at matched stream counts $N\in\{4,8,16\}$. Our code is available at https://github.com/wuyou2002/TPHC.
中文翻译
超连接（HC）将Transformer的残差流扩展为N个并行流，而流形约束的HC（mHC）在大规模下保持其稳定性。然而，mHC在超过四条流时收益甚微，部分原因在于每个子层仅向所有流写入单一输出，限制了它们更新方式的差异性。本文提出TPHC，将张量并行（TP）分片与HC流结合，以促进各流之间的更大独立性。具体而言，我们将残差流的不同混合分别路由至每个TP分片，并在写回时保持各分片输出的独立性，从而使每条流接收不同混合的输出。该设计在不增加注意力或前馈子层参数或FLOPs的前提下，增强了残差流更新的表达能力。在TP原生支持流分片的情况下，TPHC在8B模型上使用四条流时仅比mHC多9%训练时间；将流数从四增至十六，训练时间仅增加19%。在11亿参数模型上使用1000亿token训练，TPHC在匹配流数N∈{4,8,16}条件下，相比mHC将验证损失降低0.7-1.0%，并在六个常识推理基准上平均零样本准确率提升0.3-0.7个百分点。代码已公开于https://github.com/wuyou2002/TPHC。

Can Decision Models Replace LLMs in Real-World Contact Center Classification and Judging Tasks?
决策模型能否在现实中的客服中心分类与评判任务中替代大语言模型？
Contact center systems often need to make bounded decisions, such as identifying a caller's intent, retrieving the knowledge-base article that answers a query, assessing agent compliance, or judging the output of AI models used in production. Decision models return a typed answer and its probability directly, making them a promising cost-effective alternative to generative Large Language Models (LLMs) in such bounded conversational tasks. However, their effectiveness in real-world contact center applications remains unclear. In this paper, we compare nine decision models against thirteen generative LLMs in zero-shot settings across eight contact center use cases: six classification tasks and two judging tasks. Our experiments demonstrate that many decision models can match the performance of frontier LLMs on contact center classification, and API-based closed decision models achieve this at the lowest per-request cost among the API models we evaluate. We further observe that their confident predictions are more accurate, enabling selective routing in real-world production scenarios. Across classification tasks, the main weakness of most decision models is abstention: they rarely choose the no-answer option, which accounts for most of their gap to the strongest LLMs (although Gemini-Decision-1 is the notable exception in this regard). We also find that the judging quality of decision models drops sharply for smaller models, and they are also more prone to position bias. In short, our findings provide practical guidance for selecting decision models based on abstention behavior, confidence, and deployment cost, rather than overall accuracy alone.

When Do Typed Decision Models Work in Transportation? Evidence from Travel Choice and Traffic Signal Control
在交通领域中，何时使用类型化决策模型有效？来自出行方式选择与交通信号控制的证据
Typed decision models combine natural-language understanding with a structured interface that returns a selected action and an option-level probability distribution without generating lengthy textual responses. Their low inference cost and response latency make them attractive for transportation problems with predefined alternatives. This paper investigates their effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency in two representative transportation tasks: individual travel-mode choice prediction and sequential traffic signal control. We systematically evaluate TypeSafe Jev and GPT-6 Luna Decisions against conventional transportation methods, reinforcement learning algorithms, and four chat-based large language models. Experiments on the Swissmetro dataset show that typed decision models achieve lower prediction accuracy than traditional discrete choice models and the strongest chat-based baseline, while their performance varies substantially with input representation and question framing. In closed-loop traffic signal control experiments, typed decision models demonstrate competitive performance against established controllers in several scenarios, but exhibit performance degradation under more challenging network conditions. Furthermore, the typed models achieve lower inference latency and computational cost than the evaluated chat-based large language models, highlighting their potential for efficient, zero-shot transportation decision-making. Overall, these findings demonstrate both the potential and limitations of typed decision models, emphasizing the importance of input design, task characteristics, and robustness evaluation for their effective application in transportation systems.

When Passing Checks Do Not Establish Completion: Evidence Interpretation in Coding Agents
当通过检查并不能证明完成时：编码代理中的证据解释
Verification procedures in coding agents must preserve execution facts, interpret their scope and discharge checking obligations. We study these responsibilities through qualified repository checks, controlled evidence consumers and executable workflows. A prospective thirteen-task factorial study finds that correct reported facts need not yield the requested finite verdict. Conversely, all 122 delivered labels in a held-out receipt-supply study are correct, while sixteen responses misreport facts and eleven others exceed authorized scope. Production repair supplies successful controls and finite-supported preservation failures. Across 118 repair episodes on 31 tasks, source interventions separate startup reachability from repair and witness production from the claimed operation. Lifetime and resource controls delimit what passing validation establishes. Generated-note comparisons yield local repair differences but can state false explanations; strong ordinary baselines, new tasks and Skill-transfer studies supply no consistent overall advantage. These results characterize evidence interpretation, applicability and fulfilled actions while limiting claims of general diagnostic superiority, Skill deterioration or deployed folding effects.

编码代理中的验证流程必须保持执行事实的完整性，正确解释其作用范围并履行检查义务。我们通过受控的仓库检查、受控证据消费者和可执行工作流来研究这些责任。一项涉及十三个任务的因子实验发现，正确的报告事实并不必然导致所需的有限结论。相反，在保留收据的研究中，122个交付标签全部正确，但有十六个回应错误报告事实，十一个超出授权范围。生产修复成功实施了控制措施，并揭示了有限支持的保存失败。在31个任务上的118次修复事件中，源干预手段区分了启动可达性与修复过程，以及见证生成与声称操作之间的关系。生命周期和资源控制限定了通过验证所能确立的内容。生成笔记的对比显示局部修复差异，但可能陈述错误解释；强常规基线、新任务和技能迁移研究均未显示出一致的整体优势。这些结果刻画了证据解释、适用性与已履行动作的特征，同时限制了对通用诊断优越性、技能退化或部署折叠效应的主张。

StitchRAG: Constructing Compact and Evidence-Preserving Contexts for RAG with Diffusion-Based Stitching
StitchRAG：基于扩散拼接构建紧凑且保留证据的RAG上下文
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves factuality by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external evidence. However, long retrieved contexts incur computational overhead and can even hurt performance due to noisy or irrelevant content. We propose \textsc{StitchRAG}, a framework for constructing compact context in RAG. \textsc{StitchRAG} builds a faithful, information-dense context while dynamically compressing the retrieved passages. We adapt the size reduction ratio to each passage by formulating a utility maximization trading-off the retained information and computation costs. We then extract answer candidates and query-related keywords from passages as anchors and collect passage sentences containing anchors under the derived size constraint. Finally, we connect or stitch the collected sentences together using text diffusion to form a coherent and natural context. Experiments on five open-domain QA benchmarks show that \textsc{StitchRAG} consistently outperforms diverse multi-step and compression baselines. Extensive ablations show the effectiveness of dynamic size allocation, anchor preservation, and diffusion-based stitching.
中文翻译
检索增强生成（RAG）通过将大语言模型（LLMs）与外部证据结合来提升事实准确性。然而，过长的检索上下文会带来计算开销，甚至因噪声或无关内容而损害性能。我们提出 extsc{StitchRAG}，一种在RAG中构建紧凑上下文的框架。 extsc{StitchRAG}在动态压缩检索段落的同时，构建忠实且信息密集的上下文。通过建立效用最大化模型，根据每一段落的保留信息量与计算成本之间的权衡，自适应调整压缩比例。随后，从段落中提取答案候选和与查询相关的关键词作为锚点，并在推导出的大小约束下收集包含这些锚点的句子。最后，利用文本扩散技术将收集到的句子连接起来，形成连贯自然的上下文。在五个开放域问答基准上的实验表明， extsc{StitchRAG}始终优于多种多步和压缩基线方法。大量消融实验验证了动态大小分配、锚点保留以及基于扩散的拼接的有效性。

JET: Just-Enough Exploration Tuning for Efficient Tool-Calling Agents in Repository-Level Question Answering
JET：面向仓库级问答中高效工具调用智能体的恰到好处探索调优方法
In repository-level question answering (QA), an agent explores a code repository with tools and should answer a question once enough information is gathered. Nevertheless, we find that LLM agents often continue to invoke tools even after the gathered information is already sufficient to answer. We call this phenomenon over-exploration. Moreover, even though existing methods improve task performance through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories, SFT also propagates the over-exploration in the trajectories to the agent. To mitigate over-exploration, we propose Just-Enough Exploration Tuning (JET), a fine-tuning method that trains the agent to answer once the gathered information is sufficient. JET truncates each training trajectory at the step where the gathered information becomes sufficient, inserts a rationale that explains why the agent can answer at that step, and fine-tunes the agent with a higher loss weight on the rationale. Experiments on two repository-level QA benchmarks with two open-weight 8B models demonstrate that JET reduces tool calls by 11 to 33% on self-generated trajectories while largely preserving the score. On expert trajectories, JET makes 18 to 28% fewer tool calls than SFT while retaining most of the score gain of SFT. In both cases, JET also lowers the inference cost.
中文翻译
在仓库级问答（QA）中，智能体需通过工具探索代码仓库，并在收集足够信息后回答问题。然而，我们发现LLM智能体常在信息已足够时仍继续调用工具。我们将此现象称为过度探索。此外，尽管现有方法通过专家轨迹的监督微调（SFT）提升了任务性能，但SFT也把轨迹中的过度探索行为传播给了智能体。为缓解过度探索，我们提出恰到好处探索调优（JET），一种微调方法，训练智能体在信息足够时即刻作答。JET在信息足够时截断每条训练轨迹，插入解释为何此时可作答的理由，并以更高的损失权重对理由部分进行微调。在两个仓库级QA基准上使用两个开源8B模型的实验表明，JET在自生成轨迹上减少了11%至33%的工具调用，同时基本保持分数不变；在专家轨迹上，JET比SFT减少18%至28%的工具调用，同时保留SFT大部分分数增益。两种情况下，JET还降低了推理成本。

LMMR: Learned Multigranular Memory Retrieval for Small Language Model Agents
LMMR：面向小型语言模型代理的可学习多粒度记忆检索
Small language model agents have limited space to read the memory needed for multi-hop questions. Relevant facts are often scattered across records whose surrounding text consumes much of that space. We propose LMMR, a learned multigranular memory retrieval method that selects both the next source and how much of it to read. LMMR links paragraphs and sentence fragments to their original sources and pairs views that supply the same missing support during training. Relevant distractors and repeated information provide contrasting examples. At inference, the reranker scores what each candidate adds to an anchor passage, and a selector chooses the evidence within a token budget. On 22,398 official validation questions from HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, and MuSiQue, a frozen 2B answer model reaches 61.47, 43.26, and 30.58 F1 under a 400-token budget. The gains over query expansion are 3.06, 2.14, and 4.55 points, while memory input decreases by 23.1-26.9%. LMMR also uses 23.5-27.7% fewer input tokens than a coarse retriever trained on the same questions. Against that retriever followed by LLMLingua-2 at comparable input lengths, F1 gains reach 3.99 points and are significant on HotpotQA and 2WikiMultihopQA after Holm correction. All eight small answer models evaluated on HotpotQA improve over query expansion and MMR in both F1 and exact match.

小型语言模型代理在处理多跳问题时，受限于可用的记忆读取空间。相关事实常分散在大量文本记录中，而这些上下文文本占用了大部分存储空间。我们提出LMMR，一种可学习的多粒度记忆检索方法，能够同时选择下一个信息源及其读取量。LMMR将段落和句子片段与其原始来源关联，并在训练中配对提供相同缺失支持的视图。相关干扰项和重复信息作为对比样本。推理阶段，重排序器评估每个候选项对锚定段落的贡献，选择器则在token预算内选出最优证据。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultihopQA和MuSiQue共22,398个官方验证问题上，冻结的2B参数回答模型在400 token预算下分别达到61.47、43.26和30.58的F1分数。相较于查询扩展，F1提升分别为3.06、2.14和4.55点，同时内存输入减少23.1-26.9%。LMMR使用的输入token数也比在同一问题上训练的粗粒度检索器少23.5-27.7%。与该粗粒度检索器后接LLMLingua-2在相近输入长度下的表现相比，F1提升达3.99点，在Holm校正后，HotpotQA和2WikiMultihopQA上均显著。在HotpotQA上评估的所有八个小规模回答模型，均在F1和精确匹配上优于查询扩展和MMR。

Bad Apples in the Kitchen: How LLM Teams Detect, Correct, and Calibrate Against Their Misaligned Members
厨房里的坏苹果：LLM团队如何检测、纠正并校准其失准成员
What protects an LLM team when one teammate quietly goes wrong? Most multi-agent LLM work assumes cooperative partners and inspects chat between turns before acting. Real-time embodied cooperation does not allow this pause: chat runs alongside action, so a teammate whose words stay cooperative while behavior diverges can cause damage faster than the team can name what is happening. We study this case by injecting three theory-grounded misaligned agents into a custom four-player Overcooked environment, each stressing a different joint-action layer (speech-action mismatch, role invasion, and aggregate-progress disruption), and decompose robustness into three sequential gates of detection, correction, and calibration through a 3x4x2 design (n=720 episodes). Nearly all trolls are eventually named, but recovery follows only when attribution and situational diagnosis appear in the same utterance; direct source-attribution prompting improves the hardest case only by degrading the troll-free baseline by 37.5%. Robustness, then, depends less on detection alone than on whether team chat combines attribution with diagnosis in a single utterance, suggesting that sensitivity to misalignment should be targeted rather than uniformly raised.

当一个团队成员悄然出错时，LLM团队如何自保？大多数多智能体LLM研究假设合作性伙伴，并在行动前检查对话。但在实时具身协作中，对话与动作并行发生，因此一个言语上保持合作但行为偏离的成员可能造成损害的速度远超团队识别问题的能力。我们通过在定制的四人《过山车》环境中注入三个基于理论的失准智能体，分别针对不同联合行动层面（话语-动作不一致、角色入侵、整体进度破坏），采用3x4x2实验设计（n=720次实验）分解鲁棒性为检测、纠正和校准三个连续环节。几乎所有的“捣乱者”最终都被识别，但只有当归因与情境诊断出现在同一句话中时，恢复才可能发生；直接的归因提示仅在最困难情况下有效，却使无捣乱者的基线性能下降37.5%。因此，鲁棒性更多取决于团队对话是否能在单句中结合归因与诊断，而非单纯提高对失准的敏感度，提示应有针对性地关注失准信号，而非普遍增强敏感性。

Where Does Hindsight Enter Clinical Agent Benchmarks? An Audit of Simulators and Case Construction
事后洞察何时进入临床智能体基准？对模拟器与病例构建的审计
Benchmarks for LLM diagnostic agents are built from retrospective records: the case an agent reads is rewritten from discharge summaries, and unrecorded test results are generated by a simulator that often sees the final diagnosis. HindAudit separates three hindsight channels (the diagnosis given to the simulator, the retrospective context it conditions on, and the agent-facing case text) and measures each with judges trained only on real data. On 27,489 emergency admissions from MIMIC-IV, five registered tests found no leakage into simulated laboratory results: providing the diagnosis to five simulators had a negligible effect, and prompted gatekeepers diluted the diagnostic signal, although exploratory results suggest they can raise agent scores. The largest effect lay in the case text. Against a presentation-time reconstruction from triage records, benchmark-style summaries raised the zero-shot accuracy of four LLMs from 25.4% to 39.7% and discharge histories to 45.3%, and raised six interactive agents' scores while barely changing their ranking. The exploratory gap was reproduced in a registered replication on held-out patients. Masking diagnosis names left it unchanged. Because MIMIC lacks a contemporaneous bedside history, the gap is an upper bound on hindsight. We report all registered outcomes and recommend construction practices.

EGS: A Complementary Safety Framework for Educational LLMs
EGS：教育型大语言模型的互补安全框架
Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong potential for personalized education, but adolescent-facing deployment raises risks of toxic, biased, misleading, or developmentally inappropriate content. We argue that educational safety should move beyond binary refusal: student queries are often ambiguous or curiosity-driven, requiring contextual guidance rather than simple blocking. We introduce the Educational Guardrail System (EGS), a lightweight, model-agnostic runtime framework that complements intrinsic alignment through query moderation, scenario-aware pedagogical guidance, and response moderation. EGS triages inputs by risk level, redirects gray-zone queries with category-specific educational interventions, and audits outputs before delivery. Experiments across general and education-specific benchmarks show that EGS improves moderation performance, refusal quality, and jailbreak robustness across diverse LLMs. These findings suggest that defense-in-depth guardrails can strengthen safety while preserving the pedagogical utility required in educational LLMs. Our code and model weights will be publicly available to support community adoption.

RISA: Recoverable Sparse Attention for Block-Diffusion Language Models
RISA：用于块扩散语言模型的可恢复稀疏注意力
Block-wise diffusion language models (DLMs) refine multiple tokens in parallel, offering an alternative to token-by-token autoregressive decoding. Their long-context inference, however, remains bottlenecked by memory-bound attention: every denoising step repeatedly reads a growing historical key--value (KV) cache. Directly applying sparse attention does not fully solve this problem, because different queries can select different pages, inflating the block's collective read set, while repeated support construction adds overhead and permanent eviction can discard information needed later. We present RISA, a recoverable sparse-attention method with shared read plans for block-wise DLMs. RISA exploits the fact that historical KV remains fixed within a block while queries evolve across denoising steps. Newly generated history is organized into exact priority and recovery tiers; seed queries then build a protected, bounded read plan whose indices are reused across the block while attention is recomputed with fresh queries and current-block KV. This separates history preservation from repeated reading and amortizes sparse-support construction without changing the backbone or decoder. On DreamReasoner-8B, RISA retains comparable task performance. At 32K, the default configuration speeds up attention by $2.67\times$ and fixed-trajectory requests by $1.36\times$ over dense, and attention speedup reaches $5.53\times$ at 128K. Across eight settings on three models, RISA decodes faster than the tested MAGE, QUEST, and LoSA budgets at matched KL.
中文翻译
块级扩散语言模型（DLMs）并行优化多个词元，为逐词自回归解码提供替代方案。然而，其长上下文推理仍受制于内存密集型注意力：每个去噪步骤反复读取不断增长的历史键值（KV）缓存。直接应用稀疏注意力无法完全解决此问题，因为不同查询可能选择不同页面，导致块的集体读取集合膨胀；重复的支持构造增加开销，且永久淘汰可能丢弃后续所需信息。我们提出RISA，一种适用于块级DLMs的可恢复稀疏注意力方法。RISA利用块内历史KV保持不变而查询随去噪步骤演进的特点。新生成的历史被组织为精确优先级和恢复层级；初始查询构建一个受保护、有界读取计划，其索引在整个块内复用，同时注意力使用新查询和当前块KV重新计算。这将历史保存与重复读取分离，并在不改变主干或解码器的前提下摊销稀疏支持构造。在DreamReasoner-8B上，RISA保持了相当的任务性能。在32K上下文长度下，相对于密集注意力，默认配置使注意力速度提升2.67倍，固定轨迹请求提升1.36倍；在128K时，注意力加速可达5.53倍。在三种模型的八个设置中，RISA在匹配KL的情况下，解码速度优于测试的MAGE、QUEST和LoSA预算。

From Imperfective Paradox to Moorean Absurdity: Single-Chain Reasoning Lock-In and Multi-Candidate Verbalization Across Aspectual and Pragmatic Ambiguity
从不完美悖论到摩尔式荒谬：跨语义与语用模糊性下的单链推理锁定与多候选语言化
Natural language entailment and coherence judgments often depend on whether an event merely occurred, reached a telic culmination, satisfied a plan, or was evaluated under multiple pragmatic standards. We test observable consequences of the single-chain premature lock-in hypothesis across four preregistered, sealed formal experiments on Qwen3-8B-Q8_0 (16,640 formal units, temperature=0.0): 15 of 16 hypothesis slots retain confirmatory status (12 rejecting H0 under within-experiment Holm control at alpha_fam=0.05; E3_H3 demoted to descriptive status). In Experiment 1, Direct-CoT vs. MCU differs by 25.00 pp on telic high-confidence completion (Holm p=1.31239e-05), while NLI-CoT collapses by -91.25 pp on uninterrupted atelic items (Holm p=1.81805e-21). Multi-Candidate Verbalization (MCV) elicits K=5 weighted estimates in one call, separating the prompt from unweighted (MCU) and self-weighted (MCW) readouts. Mandarin (E2) and English (E3) expose an Interruption-Anchored Realis Paradox under NLI-CoT. In Experiment 4, MCU-CF raises perceived coherence on implicit Moorean sentences by 26.64 pp over Direct-CoT (Holm p=0.0006), alongside Direct-CF - Direct-CoT = 29.15 pp and MCU-CoT - Direct-CoT = 17.31 pp. A 1,800-call OpenAI ablation and a stage="dev" P16.7 cross-model extension (4,240 planned/collected units across five external models: 4,222 valid, 18 format-missing, 0 transport-missing; <2% missing per slice; OpenAI gpt-4o-mini at top_logprobs=20 achieves 1,000/1,000 valid units) show that discrete NLI-CoT and scalar Direct-CoT judgments dissociate across models. Confirmatory effects concern observable prompt-and-readout protocols; internal premature lock-in remains a hypothesis. All code, stimuli, preregistered analysis plans, and verification tools are available at https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies.

自然语言蕴含和连贯性判断往往取决于事件是否仅发生、达到目的性终点、满足计划，或在多重语用标准下被评估。我们通过四项预先注册、密封的正式实验，在Qwen3-8B-Q8_0（16,640个正式单元，温度=0.0）上测试了单链过早锁定假设的可观测后果：16个假设槽中有15个保持确认状态（12个在实验内Holm控制下于alpha_fam=0.05水平拒绝H0；E3_H3降为描述性状态）。实验1中，Direct-CoT与MCU在目的性高置信完成度上相差25.00个百分点（Holm p=1.31239e-05），而NLI-CoT在无中断非目的性项目上的表现下降91.25个百分点（Holm p=1.81805e-21）。多候选语言化（MCV）可在一次调用中生成K=5加权估计值，将提示与未加权（MCU）及自加权（MCW）输出区分开。中文（E2）和英文（E3）实验揭示了在NLI-CoT下存在“中断锚定现实悖论”。实验4中，MCU-CF相较于Direct-CoT在隐含摩尔句的感知连贯性上提升26.64个百分点（Holm p=0.0006），同时Direct-CF - Direct-CoT = 29.15个百分点，MCU-CoT - Direct-CoT = 17.31个百分点。一项包含1,800次调用的OpenAI消融实验以及一个stage="dev"的P16.7跨模型扩展（五个外部模型共计划/收集4,240个单元：4,222个有效，18个格式缺失，0个传输缺失；每片缺失率<2%；OpenAI gpt-4o-mini在top_logprobs=20时实现1,000/1,000个有效单元）表明，离散NLI-CoT与标量Direct-CoT判断在不同模型间可分离。确认效应涉及可观测的提示-读出协议；内部过早锁定仍为假设。所有代码、刺激材料、预先注册分析计划和验证工具均可在https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies获取。

What MoE Routing Controls: Causal Interventions on Expert Trajectories
MoE路由控制什么：对专家轨迹的因果干预
In mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models, a token is routed to a sequence of experts across layers. We investigate how forcing tokens onto chosen expert paths influences model behavior. We build a target path from the experts that a concept's or role's tokens most often use, and we add a fixed bias to each target expert's router logit during generation to steer the token toward this expert path. We study two kinds of targets. Format targets concern the form of the output, and concept targets concern its topic. In Qwen3-30B-A3B, we show that steering a format path changes the text the model writes. We also show that steering a concept path shifts the model's next-token probabilities toward the concept, even when the path is built in another language, yet this shift has little effect on the generated text. We investigate why formats reach the text but concepts do not by steering only part of the generation, and find evidence that a format, once started, reinforces itself. We show that the concepts religion and money steer most strongly, and that the effect is concentrated in the middle layers. We attempt to explain why the strength of the effect varies by concept through an analysis of embedding distinctiveness and word frequency.
中文翻译
在混合专家（MoE）语言模型中，一个标记会跨层被路由到一系列专家。我们研究强制标记沿选定专家路径运行如何影响模型行为。我们从概念或角色标记最常使用的专家中构建目标路径，并在生成过程中向目标专家的路由器logit添加固定偏置，以引导标记走向该路径。我们研究两类目标：格式目标关注输出形式，概念目标关注其主题。在Qwen3-30B-A3B中，我们展示引导格式路径会改变模型所写文本。我们还发现，引导概念路径会使模型的下一个词概率向该概念偏移，即使路径是在另一种语言中构建的，但这种偏移对生成文本影响甚微。我们通过仅部分生成过程的引导来探究为何格式能影响文本而概念不能，发现一旦格式启动，便会自我强化。我们显示宗教和金钱概念的引导效果最强，且效应集中在中间层。我们通过嵌入区分度和词频分析尝试解释不同概念效应强度差异的原因。

RLDE: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Interpretable Iterative Document Editing
RLDE：一种用于可解释迭代文档编辑的强化学习框架
Improving the quality, clarity, and flow of long documents remains a challenging problem in NLP, as human editing typically occurs through iterative refinement rather than a single-pass rewrite. Most existing automated systems act as black-box generators or rely on supervised learning, limiting interpretability and failing to capture the sequential nature of revisions. In this paper, we propose RLDE, an automated end-to-end metric-driven RL framework that formulates document revision as a reference-free MDP problem (unsupervised with respect to paired document-revision targets). Working with a limited action space (NOOP, INSERT, DELETE, REPLACE), RLDE leverages Pointer Networks for dynamic span selection at the token level and sends generative actions to the frozen BART-base model to obtain context-aware suggestions. PPO agents learn to optimize a multi-objective reward function based on readability, perplexity, and grammar metrics with the help of a novel SBERT-based Semantic Preservation Penalty constraint to avoid deletion degeneracy. Evaluations on the BEA-2019 dataset showed statistically significant results (p < 0.01) across all metrics, where Flesch Reading Ease increased from 49.8 to 59.4, perplexity decreased from 63.7 to 39.8, while preserving 93% of semantic information. When compared to Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompt-engineered models like GPT-4 and LLaMA-2, RLDE shows high performance at a very low computational inference cost. Moreover, the quality of edits generated by RLDE has been verified via human evaluations, scoring 4.9/5 for meaning preservation and 4.4/5 for edit quality.
中文翻译
提升长文档的质量、清晰度和流畅性仍是NLP中的挑战问题，因为人类编辑通常通过迭代精炼而非单次重写完成。现有大多数自动化系统作为黑箱生成器或依赖监督学习，限制了可解释性，且未能捕捉修订的序列特性。本文提出RLDE，一种自动化的端到端、基于度量驱动的强化学习框架，将文档修订建模为无参考的MDP问题（相对于成对文档-修订目标为无监督）。在有限动作空间（NOOP、INSERT、DELETE、REPLACE）下，RLDE利用指针网络在词元层面动态选择跨度，并将生成动作发送给冻结的BART-base模型以获取上下文感知建议。PPO代理借助新颖的SBERT-based语义保留惩罚约束，学习优化基于可读性、困惑度和语法度量的多目标奖励函数，避免删除退化。在BEA-2019数据集上的评估显示所有指标均具有统计显著性（p < 0.01）：Flesch阅读易读性从49.8提升至59.4，困惑度从63.7降至39.8，同时保留93%的语义信息。与Chain-of-Thought（CoT）提示工程模型如GPT-4和LLaMA-2相比，RLDE在极低计算开销下表现出高性能。此外，RLDE生成的编辑质量经人工评估验证，语义保留得分为4.9/5，编辑质量得分为4.4/5。

Latent Fact Composition under Next-Token Training
基于下一个词训练的潜在事实组合
Controlled studies of latent two-hop reasoning train transformers to predict only the answer, whereas language models are trained to predict every token. We ask whether a model trained on every token learns to answer two-hop questions whose intermediate entity appears in no two-hop training example. We build a synthetic corpus with such questions. On this corpus every term of the loss can be computed exactly. A looped transformer, a standard transformer and DiscoLoop answer almost none of these questions. We therefore introduce the Baton family of looped transformers. They pass the intermediate entity to the second loop as a token embedding. When trained to predict only the answer, two of its three variants answer these questions almost perfectly. We then relate the success and failure of each model to three design conditions. Experiments that change one condition at a time agree with this hypothesis. When Baton is trained on every token, however, its accuracy on these questions rises and then declines. We further split the gradient of the next-token loss into three terms. Removing the two terms that depend on the particular training set raises the peak accuracy but does not remove the decline. The decline arises in the second hop while the model still recalls both facts.

对潜在两跳推理的受控研究通常训练Transformer模型仅预测答案，而语言模型则被训练预测每一个词。我们探讨了在每个词上进行训练的模型是否能回答中间实体未出现在任何两跳训练样本中的两跳问题。我们构建了一个包含此类问题的合成语料库，该语料库中损失函数的每一项均可精确计算。循环Transformer、标准Transformer和DiscoLoop几乎无法回答这些问题。因此，我们引入了循环Transformer家族——Baton。它将中间实体作为词嵌入传递给第二轮循环。当仅训练预测答案时，其三种变体中的两种几乎完美地回答了这些问题。随后，我们将各模型的成功与失败归因于三个设计条件。每次只改变一个条件的实验结果支持该假设。然而，当Baton在每个词上进行训练时，其在这些问题上的准确率先上升后下降。我们进一步将下一个词损失的梯度分解为三项。移除依赖特定训练集的两项后，峰值准确率提高，但下降趋势依然存在。下降现象出现在第二跳阶段，此时模型仍能回忆起两个事实。

Corrupting Ties: Investigating Robustness to Bias Injection Across Multi-Agent Network Topologies
破坏联系：跨多智能体网络拓扑结构的鲁棒性偏差注入研究
AI systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making contexts, where interactions among components can introduce fairness risks that remain undetected when auditing components or collective outcomes in isolation. As LLM-based multi-agent systems become more prevalent, understanding how biased information propagates through agent interactions is essential for assessing their fairness. We introduce a controlled bias injection framework that replaces selected agent responses with predefined biased ones, enabling an intervention-based analysis of how network topology and injection configurations shape system behavior. We propose decision divergence metrics that quantify the effects of these interventions at the agent, network, and system levels, and evaluate five topologies on three binary classification tasks using LLMs from the Qwen and Gemma families. Sequential networks show the greatest divergence, whereas fully connected and mesh networks show less. Injection position also matters: in star networks, injecting the central agent has larger effects than injecting peripheral agents. Models further differ in whether divergence follows the injected bias. These findings highlight the role of network structure in the propagation of biased information and motivate topology-aware auditing of fairness risks in multi-agent systems.

Disambiguating Moral Circuits and Moral Features in Language Models: Moral content, normative judgment or cue words? Evidence from 14 models
解构语言模型中的道德电路与道德特征：道德内容、规范判断还是提示词？来自14个模型的证据
Interpretability methods can locate the attention heads and sparse autoencoder (SAE) features that change the answer a language model gives to a moral prompt, e.g., whether "Stealing medicine for a dying child is morally" continues with "justified" or "wrong". Such a component could respond to moral content, to the judgment of conduct by any norm, or to the word "morally" in the prompt. We built 227 minimal pairs of short phrases, validated by five annotators, whose two versions differ in a moral standard, in another kind of norm (prudence, etiquette, aesthetics or evidence) or in a descriptive quality, and showed them to 14 open-weight models (Llama, Gemma and Qwen, 1B to 14B, base and instruction-tuned), with and without the word "morally". In every model, the heads that change the moral answer belong to an evaluation circuit the models use for every kind of normative judgment we tested: these heads carry the same share of the effect for moral as for other normative pairs (pooled ratio 1.00, 97.5% confidence interval [0.92, 1.07], with the word; 0.90, [0.84, 0.96], without it), and the models need them as much for both. The models need these heads also when nothing in the prompt asks for a judgment, and on 400 scenarios of the ETHICS benchmark, where removing them takes away 37 to 39% of the difference between acceptable and unacceptable scenarios, against 1 to 3% for ten random heads. The SAE features we located respond mostly to the word "morally" rather than to what the phrase is about. The models represent the category of a pair (the standard the annotators assigned to it): it can be read from their hidden states, and, in the eight models we tested, removing the direction that carries it weakens the moral verdict by a few percent, and more than the other verdicts in seven of them. We release the pairs, the ratings, the code, the trained SAE dictionaries and the result files.

可解释性方法可以定位改变语言模型对道德提示回答的注意力头和稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征，例如“为垂死的孩子偷药在道德上”是接“合理”还是“错误”。这种组件可能响应道德内容、任何规范性判断的行为，或提示中的“道德上”一词。我们构建了227组最小对比短语对，经五名标注者验证，其两个版本在道德标准、其他规范（审慎、礼仪、美学或证据）或描述性特征上有所不同，并向14个开源模型（Llama、Gemma和Qwen，1B至14B，基础版和指令微调版）展示，无论是否包含“道德上”一词。在每个模型中，改变道德答案的注意力头属于模型用于所有规范性判断的评估电路：这些头在道德与其他规范对上的效应占比相同（合并比率为1.00，97.5%置信区间[0.92, 1.07]，含词；0.90，[0.84, 0.96]，不含词），且模型对两者同样依赖。即使提示中未要求判断，模型也需要这些头；在ETHICS基准的400个场景中，移除它们会消除37至39%可接受与不可接受场景之间的差异，而随机头仅减少1至3%。我们定位到的SAE特征主要响应“道德上”一词而非短语内容。模型能表示一对的类别（标注者分配的标准）：可从隐藏状态读取，且在我们测试的八个模型中，移除承载该类别的方向会使道德判断减弱几百分点，且在七种情况下比其他判断削弱更多。我们公开了成对样本、评分、代码、训练好的SAE词典和结果文件。

Token Commitment as a Control Surface for Steering Masked Diffusion Generation
令牌承诺作为掩码扩散生成的控制表面
Masked diffusion models repeatedly predict provisional tokens before a sampler decides which predictions become permanent. Existing inference-time steering methods primarily modify activations, logits, or denoising trajectories, while treating this commitment policy as a fixed decoding detail. We instead study token commitment as a diffusion-specific control surface and separate it into two decisions: which tokens to commit and how many tokens to commit at each step. For the first, we introduce counterfactual target-aware commitment, which ranks unresolved positions by a mixture of model confidence and leave-one-token-out target contribution. For the second, we introduce calibrated adaptive budgets driven by target progress and confidence. Across language and vision-language settings in the LLaDA family, multiple targets, two activation-steering methods, and an unsteered control, both mechanisms yield empirically monotonic target-quality trade-offs in every configuration we evaluate. Adaptive schedules also recover competitive operating points relative to scorer-free stochastic schedules matched in average denoising cost. Because both mechanisms require only a scalar score evaluated on a provisional completion, they apply unchanged when the target is an automatic proxy for task success rather than an output attribute: NLI entailment for dialogue summarization and CLIPScore for captioning, neither of which corresponds to a single reusable steering direction. We reproduce the behavior of both mechanisms on a second masked-diffusion family, Dream-7B, for one control target and one task-success target. These results characterize commitment--which provisional predictions become irreversible, and how rapidly--as an interpretable control surface for steering masked diffusion generation under the conditions we test.

掩码扩散模型在采样器决定哪些预测成为永久结果之前，会反复预测临时令牌。现有的推理时控制方法主要修改激活值、logits或去噪轨迹，而将这一承诺策略视为固定的解码细节。我们则将令牌承诺视为一种扩散特定的控制表面，并将其分解为两个决策：在每一步应承诺哪些令牌，以及应承诺多少令牌。针对第一个问题，我们引入反事实目标感知承诺，通过模型置信度和留一令牌排除的目标贡献混合来排序未解决的位置。针对第二个问题，我们引入由目标进展和置信度驱动的校准自适应预算。在LLaDA系列的语言和视觉-语言设置中，多个目标、两种激活控制方法及一个无控制对照，两种机制在所有评估配置中均表现出经验上的单调目标质量权衡。自适应调度还相对于平均去噪成本匹配的无评分随机调度恢复了具有竞争力的操作点。由于两种机制仅需对临时完成结果评估标量分数，因此当目标是任务成功自动代理而非输出属性时，仍可不变应用：如对话摘要的NLI蕴含关系和图像字幕的CLIPScore，二者均不对应单一可复用的控制方向。我们在第二个掩码扩散系列Dream-7B上对一个控制目标和一个任务成功目标重现了这两种机制的行为。这些结果将承诺——哪些临时预测变为不可逆，以及其速度——刻画为在测试条件下引导掩码扩散生成的可解释控制表面。

Evaluating Semantic Insensitivity in Financial Sentiment Analysis: Implications for Sustainable Financial Decision-Making
评估金融情感分析中的语义不敏感性：对可持续金融决策的影响
Financial sentiment analysis is critical for automated trading, portfolio management, and risk assessment, heavily relying on models like FinBERT. Despite high benchmark accuracy, these models remain vulnerable to subtle adversarial text modifications, threatening the integrity of algorithmic decision-making. In this paper, we propose Agent-driven Semantic Inversion (ASI), a novel adversarial attack framework targeting financial sentiment models through domain-specific semantic manipulation. Utilizing an LLM-powered multi-agent pipeline, our approach identifies eligible financial news sentences and applies targeted lexical substitutions. We empirically evaluate ASI against FinBERT, one of the most widely deployed financial sentiment models. Our results reveal a striking vulnerability: the attack successfully deceived FinBERT in 90.85% of eligible cases, with the model frequently maintaining its original sentiment prediction despite the fundamental inversion of financial performance indicators. Through qualitative analysis and token-level investigation, we demonstrate that FinBERT critically over-relies on contextual keywords and syntactic patterns rather than true logical comprehension of financial semantics. Our findings highlight the urgent need for logic-aware, robust financial NLP models to ensure the reliability of automated decision-making systems.

Consistency Is Not Correction: Separating Confidence, Abstention, and Recovery in LLM Agents
一致性并非修正：分离大语言模型智能体中的置信度、回避与恢复机制
A controller that resamples an answer, escalates to a larger model, or abstains is usually justified by a confidence signal. Three claims about such a signal do not follow from one another: it ranks answers (discrimination), it supports withholding some of them (selective prediction), and it changes an answer for the better (correction utility). We give an exact decomposition of the third into gated recoveries minus gated harms, which bounds whether a comparison can succeed before it is run: a gate that only replaces a proposal can act only where the base policy was already wrong, so the initial error count caps the discordant pairs any correct gate can produce. When that cap falls below the count a paired test needs, the experiment cannot return a positive result for a working mechanism, nor distinguish one from a gate that never fired. We measure all three targets on two model scales and find the bound binds. In a small-model suite a perturbation-agreement verifier reaches within-policy AUROC 0.990 (95% interval [0.95, 1.00]) while every episode outcome matches single-sample decoding at 4.32 times the call count. In a controlled 840-episode study no verifier replaced the proposal on any of the 60 GSM8K decisions, so that comparison tested nothing; the entire GSM8K gain across six controllers is one task, and it is the task whose identical temperature-zero repeats disagreed, making it the one answer the service does not reproduce. On the diagnostic set the gate could reach four errors while the test needed six, so even a perfect gate had no significant result available. We report two checks that would have caught this before any budget was spent, and show that tie handling in rankings, tied selection cutoffs, and unequal output limits each move a headline number in the same dataset. The binding constraint on this literature is measurement resolution, not the choice of signal.
中文翻译
一个控制器通过重新采样答案、升级到更大模型或选择回避，通常依赖于置信度信号。关于该信号的三个主张——排序答案（区分能力）、支持部分答案的回避（选择性预测）以及改进答案（修正效用）——彼此并不等价。我们对第三个主张进行了精确分解，将其拆分为门控恢复减去门控损害，从而在实验执行前就界定了比较成功的可能性上限：仅替换提议的门控机制只能在基础策略已出错时起作用，因此初始错误数量限制了任何正确门控所能产生的不一致配对数。当这一上限低于成对测试所需的数量时，实验无法得出有效机制的正向结果，也无法区分有效机制与从未触发的门控。我们在两个模型规模上测量了这三个目标，发现该界限确实生效。在小模型套件中，扰动-一致验证器在策略内AUROC达到0.990（95%置信区间[0.95, 1.00]），而每个回合的结果与单次采样解码一致，但调用次数是其4.32倍。在一项受控的840回合研究中，没有任何验证器在60个GSM8K决策中替换过提议，因此该比较未测试任何内容；整个GSM8K收益仅来自一个任务，而该任务正是那些温度为零的重复结果不一致的任务，说明服务本身无法复现该答案。在诊断集上，门控最多可识别四个错误，但测试需要六个，因此即使完美门控也无显著结果可用。我们报告了两项可在预算耗尽前发现问题的检查方法，并表明排名中的平局处理、平局选择截断点以及不等输出限制均会改变同一数据集中的关键指标。该领域文献的约束因素是测量分辨率，而非信号选择。
👤 Khushal Sethi 📅 2026-10-15 🔗 arXiv

Can an LLM dream? Testing the overfitted brain hypothesis on an agent with text memory
大语言模型能做梦吗？在具有文本记忆的智能体上检验过拟合大脑假说
Hoel (2021) proposed that dreams distort replayed experience so that the brain does not overfit to recent events. We test this hypothesis on an LLM agent (Claude Opus 5.5) that stores what it learns in a text memory and rewrites that memory each night from the day's conversation transcript. The agent goes through ten simulated days of conversation built from LongMemEval. We corrupt 5, 10, 20 or 40 percent of the transcript's turns before each nightly rewrite in one of two ways. Detail corruption changes one or two details in a turn to plausible wrong ones, or replaces the turn with one by the same speaker from an earlier day. Random-text corruption replaces half of the selected turns with a passage of Moby-Dick and inserts Moby-Dick words into the other half. A size limit on the memory, rising from 19,500 to 117,000 tokens over ten nights, set from human free-recall data, forces it to forget. We learned that corruption did not help the agent apply what it learned in earlier conversations to new tasks in any of 30 runs (ten conditions, three different conversation histories). Replacing details in 40 percent of turns lowered recall from 76 to 60 percent. Random text had no consistent effect on recall, with a paired difference of 0 $\pm$ 2 points at 40 percent, because the model recognized it as corrupted and left it out of memory. Replaced details were stored as fact even when another turn held the correct value, and the agent answered 40 percent of those questions correctly, compared with 75 percent without corruption. These results suggest that a pretrained model can separate corrupted text from real events, so a fair test needs a learner that cannot make this separation as easily, such as a model fine-tuned on the corrupted days or an agent whose days take place in an invented world.
中文翻译
Hoel（2021）提出，梦境通过扭曲重放的经历来防止大脑对近期事件产生过拟合。我们在此对一个存储学习内容于文本记忆的大语言模型智能体（Claude Opus 5.5）测试了这一假说，该智能体每天晚上根据当日对话记录重写其记忆。我们让该智能体经历由LongMemEval构建的十天模拟对话。在每次夜间重写前，以两种方式之一对对话记录的5%、10%、20%或40%进行篡改：细节篡改将某一轮中的一个或两个细节改为看似合理的错误信息，或用同一位说话者早前一天的轮次替换；随机文本篡改则将选定轮次的一半替换为《白鲸》段落，并在另一半中插入《白鲸》词汇。记忆大小限制从19,500增至117,000 token，依据人类自由回忆数据设定，迫使智能体遗忘。在30次运行中（十种条件，三种不同对话历史），篡改并未帮助智能体将早期对话中学到的内容应用于新任务。在40%轮次中修改细节后，回忆率从76%降至60%。随机文本篡改对回忆率无一致影响，40%水平下配对差异为0±2分，因为模型识别出其为篡改并将其排除在记忆之外。被替换的细节即使其他轮次存在正确值，仍被存储为事实，智能体对此类问题的回答正确率为40%，而无篡改情况下为75%。这些结果表明，预训练模型能够区分篡改文本与真实事件，因此公平测试需使用难以做出这种区分的学习者，如在篡改日上微调的模型，或在虚构世界中运行的智能体。
👤 Eric Zou 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21646]
agentic-rl
Limite 1B - Violetto: Technical Report
Limite 1B - Violetto：技术报告
We present Limite 1B - Violetto, a one-billion-parameter model designed for high-throughput mathematical reasoning. Limite is trained from scratch on 237 billion tokens from a carefully curated, mathematics-centric corpus and supports context lengths of up to 131k tokens. Its architecture and training recipe were developed together to maximize reasoning capability while keeping training and inference costs low. Post-training combines knowledge distillation, targeted supervised fine-tuning, and RLVR on a curated curriculum. Limite achieves 94.01% on AIME 2026 and 74.25% on BeyondAIME, placing it among models many times its size on our evaluation suite. The model is deliberately only lightly instruction-tuned: it is intended primarily as a single-turn mathematical solver and as a fast, parallel component of larger systems, rather than as a general-purpose assistant. We release the model, its base and value checkpoints, and an optimized serving implementation under the Apache 2.0 license.
中文翻译
我们介绍了Limite 1B - Violetto，一个专为高吞吐量数学推理设计的一百亿参数模型。Limite在经过精心筛选的以数学为中心的语料库中，从头开始训练了2370亿个标记，并支持最长达131,000个标记的上下文长度。其架构和训练方法协同设计，旨在最大化推理能力的同时保持较低的训练与推理成本。后训练阶段结合了知识蒸馏、针对性监督微调以及基于精选课程的强化学习价值回归（RLVR）。该模型在AIME 2026上达到94.01%的准确率，在BeyondAIME上达到74.25%，在我们的评估套件中表现优于许多规模大得多的模型。该模型仅进行轻度指令微调：其主要用途是作为单轮数学求解器，或作为大型系统中的快速并行组件，而非通用助手。我们以Apache 2.0许可证发布该模型、其基础模型和价值检查点，以及优化的部署实现。

CrisisBench: an evaluation suite for crisis counseling AI
CrisisBench：危机咨询AI的评估套件
Background: Crisis helplines are essential to mental health systems but are often understaffed and underfunded. LLMs could support counselors, but existing benchmarks do not capture the escalation, risk assessment, and de-escalation central to crisis conversations, while privacy restrictions limit use of real transcripts. Methods: We present CrisisBench, a privacy-preserving suite for single- and multi-turn crisis counseling evaluation. It includes 1,085 single-turn tasks from 200 de-identified conversations and 1,185 patient LLMs derived from hotline metadata. Patient models and a 23-item LLM judge were calibrated against expert counselors. Results: We evaluated five GPT models plus draft-then-edit and retrieval-augmented generation. All systems underperformed human counselors on excessive empathy and questioning in single-turn evaluation. The rubric achieved human-human Krippendorff's $α$ = 0.51 and LLM-human $α$ = 0.54 (Pearson r = 0.80). In multi-turn evaluation, retrieval-augmented generation scored highest overall (0.961), with all systems within 0.058. Microskills and style/tone were near ceiling, while productivity and safety/risk accounted for most between-system variation. Counselors rated patient LLMs realistic (86%) and useful (92%). Conclusion: CrisisBench enables scalable, privacy-preserving evaluation of crisis counseling. Current systems perform strongly on core structural competencies but still diverge from experts on important response-level failure modes. We release the rubrics, judge prompts, and evaluation tasks.
中文翻译
背景：危机热线对心理健康体系至关重要，但常面临人手不足和资金短缺。大语言模型（LLMs）可辅助咨询师，但现有基准未能捕捉危机对话中的升级、风险评估和降级等核心要素，且隐私限制阻碍真实对话记录的使用。方法：我们提出CrisisBench，一个保护隐私的单轮与多轮危机咨询评估套件。包含来自200个去标识化对话的1,085个单轮任务，以及1,185个由热线元数据生成的患者LLM。患者模型和23项指标的LLM评判标准经专家咨询师校准。结果：我们评估了五个GPT模型，以及草稿-编辑和检索增强生成方法。所有系统在单轮评估中于过度共情和提问方面均低于人类咨询师。评分量表达到人类-人类Krippendorff's α = 0.51，LLM-人类α = 0.54（Pearson r = 0.80）。在多轮评估中，检索增强生成得分最高（0.961），所有系统差异在0.058以内。微技能和风格/语气接近满分，而生产力与安全/风险是系统间差异的主要来源。咨询师评价患者LLM具有现实感（86%）且有用（92%）。结论：CrisisBench实现了危机咨询AI的可扩展、隐私保护评估。当前系统在核心结构能力上表现良好，但在重要响应层面的失败模式上仍与专家存在差距。我们发布了评分量表、评判提示和评估任务。

Ambient LLMs: Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-tuning via Temperature Matching
环境大语言模型：通过温度匹配实现数据高效的监督微调
Collecting large-scale, diverse, and high-quality post-training datasets for autoregressive language models is challenging. Consequently, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is typically applied to datasets significantly smaller than those used for pre-training, which leads to overfitting and the catastrophic forgetting of the tails of the data distribution. To prevent this collapse, we introduce Ambient SFT, a principled framework for leveraging imperfect predictions to augment the fine-tuning set. In our method, a "frozen" version of the pre-trained model serves as a noisy teacher that the fine-tuned model must match at a coarse level. In particular, we require that the high-temperature versions of the conditional distributions produced by the post-training model should match the high-temperature versions of the pre-training ones. The temperature hyperparameter controls the level of trust in the pre-trained model, with the limit $T\to \infty$ reverting back to regular SFT and $T=1$ to knowledge distillation. We demonstrate that our approach outperforms standard SFT across domain adaptation, instruction-tuning, and reasoning, leading for up to $21\%$ average relative improvement to standard SFT.
中文翻译
为自回归语言模型收集大规模、多样化且高质量的后训练数据集具有挑战性。因此，监督微调（SFT）通常应用于远小于预训练阶段所用的数据集，这会导致过拟合以及数据分布尾部信息的灾难性遗忘。为防止这种退化，我们提出了环境SFT（Ambient SFT），一种利用不完美预测来增强微调数据集的原则性框架。在该方法中，一个‘冻结’的预训练模型作为噪声教师，微调后的模型需在粗粒度上与其匹配。具体而言，我们要求后训练模型产生的条件分布的高温版本应与预训练模型的高温版本相匹配。温度超参数控制对预训练模型的信任程度，当 $T\to \infty$ 时退化为标准SFT，而 $T=1$ 时则对应知识蒸馏。实验表明，我们的方法在领域适应、指令微调和推理任务中均优于标准SFT，平均相对提升高达21%。
👤 Giannis Daras, Marios Papachristou, Adam Klivans, Antonio Torralba, Constantinos Daskalakis 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

Nunchi: Ears Help Eyes in Multi-Party Conversations
Nunchi：在多方对话中，耳朵帮助眼睛
Identifying whom a person looks at is central to understanding multi-party conversations, yet gaze geometry alone becomes ambiguous as more people appear. Conversations offer cues that geometry lacks, such as who is speaking and what is said, but no gaze dataset provides them for many multi-party scenes. We introduce SORBET, a benchmark of 3,340 samples from 1,578 clips with synchronized speech, transcripts, and attended person labels, covering more multi-party scenes than existing gaze datasets. Prior gaze models struggle to use these cues, which come in different forms, must be linked to the right person, and can conflict with gaze geometry. We propose Nunchi, a framework that constructs a text prompt from the transcript and the outputs of gaze and speaker models, links every cue to the candidates with shared labels, and lets a vision-language model (VLM) weigh gaze geometry against conversational cues to identify the attended person. Nunchi outperforms the strongest existing method by 4.8 percentage points. On SORBET, conversational cues help whereas additional visual cues do not, and the gains hold across three VLMs, with automatic transcripts, and on two existing datasets.
中文翻译
识别一个人注视的对象是理解多方对话的核心，但随着参与人数增加，仅靠 gaze 几何会变得模糊。对话提供了几何无法提供的线索，例如谁在说话以及说了什么，但目前没有 gaze 数据集为大量多方场景提供这些信息。我们引入 SORBET，一个包含3,340个样本的基准数据集，来自1,578段视频，同步包含语音、转录文本和被关注人员标签，覆盖的多方场景比现有 gaze 数据集更多。先前的 gaze 模型难以有效利用这些线索，因为它们形式多样、必须与正确的人关联，且可能与 gaze 几何冲突。我们提出 Nunchi 框架：从转录文本和 gaze 模型、说话人模型的输出构建文本提示，将每个线索与候选对象通过共享标签关联，并让视觉-语言模型（VLM）权衡 gaze 几何与对话线索以识别被关注者。Nunchi 在 SORBET 上的表现优于最强现有方法4.8个百分点。在 SORBET 上，对话线索有助于提升性能，而额外视觉线索无效，且增益在三种 VLM、自动转录文本以及两个现有数据集上均保持稳定。
👤 JunYeong Park, Shijing Wang, Seonwook Park, Michael Stengel, Shalini De Mello et al. 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

The Resolution Hypothesis: Agent Evolution's Key Is Identifying Improvements
解析假说：智能体演化的关键在于识别改进
Agent evolution follows a simple loop: a proposer writes candidate artifacts (prompts, skills, memories) and selection accepts those that score higher. This loop has shown promise, yet practitioners face a puzzle: methods trade wins across papers, and evolution often fails to bootstrap on certain tasks. We trace this puzzle to the Resolution Hypothesis: agent evolution stalls not because methods fail to propose better artifacts, but because noisy evaluations cannot reliably identify them. Across seven evolution methods and six models (over 10M LLM calls), we find support for the hypothesis at both levels where evaluation scores are used. At the candidate level (choosing artifacts), helpful candidate artifacts exist, but selection is no better than random at telling them from harmful ones, so errors accumulate across rounds. At the method level (ranking methods), typical margins of a few percentage points are smaller than the difference between two independent runs of the same method, so published rankings often rest on luck. Together, these findings indicate that agent evolution is often bottlenecked by reliably identifying improvements. This calls for a shift in how we develop and evaluate self-improving agents, from designing ever more candidate improvement systems to making true improvements detectable and accumulable.
中文翻译
智能体演化遵循一个简单循环：提议者生成候选成果（如提示、技能、记忆），选择机制接受得分更高的成果。这一循环已展现出潜力，但实践者面临一个谜题：不同方法之间的性能提升在论文间相互交换，且演化常无法在某些任务上实现自举。我们将此谜题归因于解析假说：智能体演化停滞并非因为方法无法提出更好的成果，而是因为噪声评估无法可靠识别这些改进。通过对七种演化方法和六种模型（超过1000万次LLM调用）的研究，我们在两个层面均找到支持该假说的证据。在候选层面（选择成果），存在有益的候选成果，但选择机制在区分有益与有害成果方面并不优于随机，导致错误在多轮中累积。在方法层面（排名方法），典型差距仅几个百分点，小于同一方法两次独立运行之间的差异，因此发表的排名往往依赖运气。这些发现表明，智能体演化常常受制于可靠识别改进的能力。这呼吁我们在开发和评估自我改进智能体时转变思路，从设计越来越复杂的候选改进系统，转向使真正的改进变得可检测且可累积。
👤 Yuanhao Zeng, Shaocheng Lan, Ao Lu, Kuang Yan, Heng Lin et al. 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22778]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
A Full-Ledger Counterfactual Audit of Adaptive Reasoning
自适应推理的完整账本反事实审计
Adaptive reasoning must decide whether to answer now or spend more compute. Predicting that the current answer is wrong does not establish whether further reasoning will improve it. We introduce the Full-Ledger Counterfactual Value Audit (FLCVA), which compares an immediate answer with four fixed-length continuations from the same frozen prefix under one answer procedure and accounts for the cost of every signal used by a policy. On GSM8K and MATH-500, a random 256-token continuation recovers a wrong answer in 26.6% of Qwen3-8B cases and 18.9% of DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B cases, while damaging a correct answer in 1.4% and 1.9%. Correctness labels from independently sampled continuations predict a separate continuation's outcome in both primary models. In offline replay, using these labels to allocate compute improves performance on DeepSeek; the Qwen3 gain remains inconclusive. Under full cost accounting, the tested lightweight, myopic controllers mainly recognize current errors and add little continuation-specific value beyond that shortcut. A Llama-8B replication and action/cost ablations examine the robustness of these findings. FLCVA separates measuring local continuation value, predicting it from observable signals, and using those predictions to allocate computation.
中文翻译
自适应推理必须决定是立即回答还是投入更多计算资源进行进一步思考。预测当前答案错误并不能证明进一步推理会改善结果。我们提出完整账本反事实价值审计（FLCVA），通过比较同一冻结前缀下单一回答流程中的即时答案与四种固定长度的延续，并计入策略使用每条信号的成本。在GSM8K和MATH-500上，随机256 token的延续在Qwen3-8B案例中恢复错误答案的比例为26.6%，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B为18.9%，同时损害正确答案的比例分别为1.4%和1.9%。独立采样的延续所得正确性标签能够预测另一延续的结果。在离线回放中，利用这些标签分配计算资源可提升DeepSeek性能，而Qwen3的增益尚不明确。在全面成本核算下，测试的轻量级、短视控制器主要识别当前错误，对后续延续的价值贡献有限。Llama-8B的复现及动作/成本消融实验验证了这些发现的稳健性。FLCVA将局部延续价值测量、从可观测信号预测价值以及利用预测结果分配计算三个环节分离。
👤 Xiaopeng Chu, Wenzheng Kong, Wei Liu, Wenyi Zhang 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22777]

CrossEnv: Synthesizing Verifiable Cross-Environment Tasks for Long-Horizon Agent Training
CrossEnv：用于长周期智能体训练的可验证跨环境任务合成
Executable synthetic environments provide scalable training tasks and verifiable feedback for long-horizon agents. Existing environment synthesis methods primarily generate tasks within individual environments. However, these tasks do not adequately capture real-world interactions across services, where actions in one service depend on information obtained from another. Synthesizing such tasks is challenging because data generated across environments must remain consistent to support executable and verifiable interactions. To address this challenge, we introduce CrossEnv, a framework that uses an entity-tool graph to identify connections between environments and aligns task-specific data to synthesize executable, verifiable cross-environment tasks. We further propose CrossEnv-Curriculum, which progresses from single-environment tool use to coordination across more environments and business domains, before introducing user interaction. Across four external benchmarks, combining cross-environment training data with this curriculum achieves competitive performance with fewer training examples. Further analyses support the benefits of cross-environment data and curriculum training, and provide evidence of transfer to new environment combinations, external tasks less similar to the training data, and longer workflows.
中文翻译

The Between-Round Decision in Autonomous Research: An LLM Controller That Reads the Whole Trajectory
自主研究中的回合间决策：一个读取完整轨迹的LLM控制器
Executing an idea often changes how promising it looks, so the next step in autonomous research should rest on results, not on proposals. After each round of experiments, the system must decide whether to refine the best solution or start a new direction, which solution to build from, and what goal to set. We call this the between-round decision. Existing systems wrap it in machinery such as rules, scores, search trees, and schedules; where an LLM makes it, what the LLM needs to read is left open. We ask whether an LLM can make this decision from accumulated evidence, without a search structure around it, and how much of that evidence it needs to read. We present BRED (Between-Round Evidence-based Decision), an LLM-based controller that maintains the best verified solution so far (the Champion) and a log of past rounds, and makes one decision per round. On three widely used research-agent benchmark tasks, BRED improves on the task baselines by 4.80% in MAE on ETTh1 (to 0.4172) and by 3.44 and 3.43 points of accuracy on SST-2 (to 95.07%) and CIFAR-100 (to 84.63%), ahead of the published results. When the controller does not read the accumulated evidence, the same loop gains less: with no history it reaches 0.4299 on ETTh1 and 83.71% on CIFAR-100, whereas the full controller's MAE is 2.95% lower and its accuracy 0.92 points higher. On a new task, speech-based depression detection under a leakage-free protocol, it reaches a macro-F1 of 0.6164 on seeds held out from selection, 5.5 points above its baseline and 4.0 above the strongest published system. Code is available at https://github.com/learninginvision/BRED.
中文翻译
执行一个想法往往会改变其前景，因此自主研究中的下一步应基于结果而非提案。每次实验后，系统需决定是否优化最佳解决方案、开启新方向、选择哪个方案作为基础以及设定什么目标。我们称此为回合间决策。现有系统通常通过规则、评分、搜索树或调度机制封装该决策；当由LLM做出时，LLM需要阅读的内容仍不明确。我们探讨LLM能否在无搜索结构的情况下，仅基于累积证据做出该决策，以及需要阅读多少证据。我们提出BRED（基于回合间证据的决策）——一种基于LLM的控制器，它维护迄今为止的最佳已验证解决方案（冠军）和过往回合日志，并每轮做出一次决策。在三个广泛使用的研究代理基准任务上，BRED在ETTh1上的MAE提升4.80%（降至0.4172），在SST-2和CIFAR-100上的准确率分别提升3.44和3.43个百分点（分别达到95.07%和84.63%），优于已发表结果。当控制器不读取累积证据时，相同循环的表现下降：无历史记录时，ETTh1的MAE为0.4299，CIFAR-100准确率为83.71%，而完整控制器的MAE低2.95%，准确率高0.92个百分点。在一项新的泄漏无关协议下的语音抑郁检测任务中，其宏F1达到0.6164（在未参与选择的种子上），比基线高出5.5点，比最强已发表系统高出4.0点。代码可在https://github.com/learninginvision/BRED 获取。

Evolution Under Fire: Evolving Agent Safety Harnesses with Adaptive Injection Stressors
战火中的进化：通过自适应注入压力器演化智能体安全防护罩
Tool-using language-model agents must act on information from untrusted emails, webpages, and documents. This creates a central security challenge: malicious instructions and legitimate task directives can arrive through the same interaction channel. Existing defenses are largely hand-designed and static, making further improvements dependent on repeated manual redesign. We introduce Evolution Under Fire, a staged framework that couples safety-harness evolution with adaptive stressor evolution. Utility-constrained, Pareto-scheduled tree search evolves complete executable safety harnesses from execution evidence while keeping the task model, benchmark, tools, and evaluator fixed. Adaptive stressor evolution then discovers utility-preserving attacks that guide a subsequent stage of harness evolution. On AgentDyn with GPT-4o-mini, an evolved harness reduces attack success from 50.4% to 3.0%, improves clean and attacked utility over the initial harness, and offers a stronger security-utility trade-off than reproduced defenses. On Agent-SafetyBench with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, harness evolution reduces attack success from 82.6% to 62.0% while retaining helpfulness and runtime validity. Evolved harnesses also transfer without further evolution to Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, reducing attack success from 32.1% to 0.9% while improving attacked-task utility.
中文翻译
使用工具的语言模型智能体必须处理来自不可信邮件、网页和文档的信息。这带来核心安全挑战：恶意指令与合法任务指令可能通过同一交互通道到达。现有防御措施大多为手工设计且静态，导致后续改进依赖反复的人工重设计。我们提出“战火中的进化”——一种分阶段框架，将安全防护罩的演化与自适应压力源的演化相结合。在效用约束下，采用帕累托调度的树搜索，从执行证据中演化出完整的可执行安全防护罩，同时保持任务模型、基准、工具和评估器不变。随后，自适应压力源演化发现保留效用的攻击方式，引导下一阶段的防护罩演化。在AgentDyn上使用GPT-4o-mini，演化后的防护罩将攻击成功率从50.4%降至3.0%，提升了清洁与受攻击状态下的效用，并在安全-效用权衡上优于复现的现有防御。在Agent-SafetyBench上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，防护罩演化将攻击成功率从82.6%降至62.0%，同时保持有用性和运行时有效性。演化后的防护罩无需进一步演化即可迁移到Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，将攻击成功率从32.1%降至0.9%，同时提升受攻击任务的效用。

From Perception to Simulation: Unveiling and Empowering Mental Spatial Dynamics in MLLMs
从感知到仿真：揭示并赋能多模态大模型中的心理空间动态
Spatial reasoning is a fundamental component of world understanding for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing research has advanced the perception of spatial relations in static scenes, the domain of dynamic spatial simulation remains relatively unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce MentalGrid, a benchmark focused on spatial reasoning tasks that require models to simulate evolving spatial states without intermediate visual feedback. Extensive evaluation on our benchmark reveals a significant deficiency in the spatial simulation capabilities of existing models. Specifically, we find that performance is constrained by perceptual limitations in simple simulation scenarios and hindered by inadequate multi-step reasoning as complexity increases. To address these challenges, we propose a three-stage curriculum learning framework utilizing 16k specialized spatial reasoning samples. This approach yields SpatialWalker, which achieves a 33.8% average improvement over its base model, surpassing proprietary models like Gemini-3-Flash in spatial simulation. Furthermore, SpatialWalker demonstrates robust generalization, maintaining a 4.9% average gain on out-of-domain benchmarks, demonstrating the pivotal role of spatial simulation as a fundamental building block for broader spatial intelligence.

When Agent Skills Go Stale: Runtime Behavior and Selective Maintenance in Evolving Environments
当智能体技能过时：动态环境中的运行时行为与选择性维护
Agent skills use past experience and current observations to guide decisions and actions on related tasks. This experience is retained in skill state, whose reuse can amortize inference-time reasoning and environment interaction. When environment changes, parts of this state can become outdated while others remain useful. Across four task settings and two models, we compare skill reuse before and after environment changes. Previously beneficial skills can reduce task performance by over 10 percentage points or increase execution cost by nearly 40% relative to no skill. Task success can also remain unchanged while execution savings decline. These effects depend on which environment-specific details become outdated and the agent's ability to compensate using current observations. We introduce selective maintenance, which replays checks from successful executions without additional model calls. It verifies whether observed differences conflict with skill state, repairs affected content, and validates updates, rebuilding when local repair is insufficient. No description of the change is supplied. Relative to reusing stale state, maintenance improves task accuracy by up to 67pp and reduces execution steps by up to 37%. These results suggest that preserving a skill's inference time value as environments evolve requires selectively maintaining its state.
中文翻译
智能体技能利用过往经验与当前观测来指导相关任务的决策与行动。这些经验保留在技能状态中，其复用可分摊推理时间和环境交互成本。当环境发生变化时，部分状态可能过时而其他部分仍具价值。在四个任务设置和两种模型下，我们比较了环境变化前后技能复用的效果。先前有益的技能可能导致任务性能下降超过10个百分点，或使执行成本增加近40%（相对于不使用技能的情况）。任务成功率也可能保持不变，但执行节省却减少。这些影响取决于哪些环境特异性细节过时以及智能体利用当前观测进行补偿的能力。我们提出选择性维护机制，通过重放成功执行中的检查点而不额外调用模型，验证观测差异是否与技能状态冲突，修复受影响内容并验证更新；当局部修复不足时则重建状态。该方法无需提供变更描述。相比复用过时状态，维护机制可将任务准确率提升最高达67个百分点，并将执行步骤减少最多37%。结果表明，在环境演进过程中，为保持技能的推理价值，需有选择地维护其状态。
👤 Ömer Faruk Akgül, Shuli Jiang, Yuting Zhang, Yi Zhang, Xiaohan Fei et al. 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv

VRLLM: Turning LLMs into Powerful Tabular Classifiers
VRLLM：将大语言模型转化为强大的表格分类器
Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on text, but whether pretrained LLMs are effective for large-scale tabular classification remains unclear, as many previous works demonstrate that LLMs' tabular data classification performance falls behind tree-based models or neural architectures designed for tabular data. At Visa Research, we managed to fine-tune a pretrained Llama 3.2 1B LLM on billions of rows of labeled tabular data and make it outperform a wide array of tabular classifiers and even our competitive production deep learning model. For ease of reference, we refer to the model as the Visa Research LLM (VRLLM). In this paper, we share how we achieved this exciting result, covering data preparation, training recipe, ablation studies of design choices and deployment validation. We also benchmark frontier LLMs on the same task and analyze their prediction behavior across model families and generations. Our interpretability experiments suggest that VRLLM learns feature-specific predictive associations, while component ablations indicate that the earliest two FFN layers contribute to the emergence of linearly decodable task information. Our paper provides an engineering recipe and a focused empirical case study on adapting compact LLMs to industrial tabular prediction tasks.

Fine-Grained Japanese Clinical Named Entity Recognition: A Controlled Comparison of BERT and Open-Weight Large Language Models
细粒度日语临床命名实体识别：BERT与开源大语言模型的受控对比
Background: Generative large language models (LLMs) offer an alternative to encoder-based clinical named entity recognition (NER), but their performance on fine-grained Japanese extraction tasks and sensitivity to generation settings remain uncertain. Methods: We compared two fine-tuned BERT-CRF baselines with nine open-weight LLMs on iCorpus, which defines 113 entity categories. The evaluation used 433 sentences from 36 test documents; 1,672 sentences were used for training. LLMs were evaluated using zero-shot prompting with and without JSON Schema constraints and three-seed QLoRA fine-tuning. Fine-tuned models were assessed under different output-token limits, including 900 and larger model-specific limits of 2,600 or 4,096 tokens. Performance was measured using sentence-level label-surface matching with relaxed and exact-surface F1. Results: The highest zero-shot relaxed F1 was 0.337, compared with 0.836-0.841 for the BERT baselines. At the larger output limits, all eight fine-tuned LLMs with at least 13B total parameters exceeded both BERT baselines (relaxed F1, 0.882-0.914; exact-surface F1, 0.840-0.880). Across three-seed means, increasing the output limit improved relaxed F1 by 0.052-0.085, alongside higher recall and fewer length-limited outputs. JSON Schema constraints changed relaxed F1 by -0.014 to +0.008 among seven models outside the two reasoning-oriented configurations, while medical-general model pairs showed no consistent advantage for medical specialization. Conclusions: On this historical Japanese case-report benchmark, task-specific fine-tuning combined with adequate generation capacity was associated with stronger LLM extraction performance. Output-token limits materially affected comparisons with BERT, whereas enforcing output structure alone did not overcome low zero-shot accuracy.

背景：生成式大语言模型（LLMs）为基于编码器的临床命名实体识别（NER）提供了替代方案，但其在细粒度日语抽取任务上的表现及对生成设置的敏感性仍不确定。方法：我们在iCorpus数据集上，将两个微调的BERT-CRF基线模型与九个开源大语言模型进行对比，该数据集定义了113个实体类别。评估使用了36个测试文档中的433个句子；1,672个句子用于训练。LLMs通过零样本提示（带与不带JSON Schema约束）和三种子Qlora微调进行评估。微调模型在不同输出token限制下进行评估，包括900和更大模型特定限制（2,600或4,096 token）。性能通过宽松和精确表面匹配的句子级F1进行衡量。结果：最高零样本宽松F1为0.337，而BERT基线为0.836–0.841。在较大输出限制下，所有至少13B参数的八种微调LLMs均超过两个BERT基线（宽松F1：0.882–0.914；精确表面F1：0.840–0.880）。在三种子平均值中，增加输出限制使宽松F1提高0.052–0.085，伴随更高的召回率和更少的长度受限输出。JSON Schema约束对七种模型（排除两个推理导向配置）的宽松F1影响在-0.014到+0.008之间，而医学通用模型对对未显示出医学专业化的一致优势。结论：在此历史日语病例报告基准上，任务特定微调结合充足的生成能力与更强的LLM提取性能相关。输出token限制显著影响与BERT的比较结果，而仅强制输出结构无法克服低零样本准确率。

What RL on Expert-Built Tasks Teaches Nemotron 3.5 Super
在专家构建任务上进行强化学习对Nemotron 3.5 Super的启示
We post-train NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 Super (120B total, 12B active parameters) with reinforcement learning (RL) on five expert-built task datasets, one at a time: real-world software engineering, complex constraints, long-horizon tool use, professional chart understanding and enterprise document reasoning. Each run trains a fresh rank-32 LoRA adapter with CISPO on Tinker, with no supervised stage, and each adapter is compared with the base model on the same tasks, harness and settings. On twelve headline benchmarks, eight built by other groups and four by Surge AI, the gains range from +4.5 points on IFBench to +13.8 on GDP_pdf, which rises from 10.4% to 24.2%; SWE-bench Pro V2 rises from 86.9% to 92.1%, Terminal-Bench 2.1 from 63.9% to 70.3%, MultiChallenge from 58.3% to 66.6%, AutomationBench from 14.8% to 27.5% and Chartography from 39.4% to 50.6%. The document adapter, trained without API tools, also gains +8.0 points on AutomationBench. In attempts read side by side with the base model's on tasks each adapter newly solves, the base model's failures recur in a few modes, and each adapter does more often what the base model missed: the tool-use adapter finds the data and the rules before acting, the coding adapter writes its own test or scratch files on SWE-bench Pro V2, the constraints adapter carries earlier turns into its answers, the chart adapter measures the marks in code, and the document adapter reads AutomationBench's spreadsheets.

agentic-rlagent-memory
Long-Context Generation Is a Sampling Problem (When You Turn Up the Heat)
长上下文生成本质上是一个采样问题（当你提高温度时）
Open-weight language models are widely observed to get worse the longer they generate. Often, attempts to fix this involve more training. At high temperature, The sampler, i.e. the rule that picks the next token out of the model's probabilities, decides whether long and agentic generation survives and/or thrives. We test 29 open-weight models from 1B to 122B parameters on ten established benchmarks plus needle retrieval out to one million tokens, covering SWE-bench Verified coding agents, long chain-of-thought reasoning, long-form writing, long structured output and long-context reading. Samplers that keep their pool of candidate tokens small and steady as temperature rises don't lose accuracy. Samplers that let in low-probability tokens fall apart. At temperature 2, coding agents running top-p, top-k or Top-H resolve 0 of 100 GitHub issues, while the same agents running top-nsigma resolve 46 to 62 and win every issue the two samplers disagree on, on three Qwen3.5 models. Over all benchmarks top-nsigma keeps a median 100% of its temperature-1 score where top-p keeps 26%. Counting how many tokens each sampler lets through explains why. In one Llama-3.1-8B story, going from temperature 1 to 2 grows top-p's pool from 4 tokens to 51,845 while top-nsigma stays at 3. Across 133 combinations of sampler setting and temperature, the typical pool size predicts judged quality (Spearman correlation of -0.77), and when we pick the pool at temperature 1 and only apply temperature afterwards, Top-H, min-p and p-less stop collapsing. At the default temperature of 1 most samplers perform about the same, We release all code and data to the public.
中文翻译
开放权重语言模型在生成长度增加时通常表现下降。修复此问题的常见方法是更多训练。但在高温度下，采样器（即从模型概率中选择下一个词元的规则）决定了长时程、高自主性生成能否存活甚至繁荣。我们在十个已建立的基准测试及长达一百万词元的针堆检索任务上测试了29个参数规模从1B到122B的开放权重模型，涵盖SWE-bench Verified编码代理、长链式思维推理、长篇写作、长结构化输出和长上下文阅读。保持候选词池小且稳定的采样器在温度升高时不会损失准确性；而允许低概率词元进入的采样器则会崩溃。在温度2时，使用top-p、top-k或Top-H的编码代理在100个GitHub问题中解决0个，而使用top-nsigma的同一类代理解决了46至62个，并在两个采样器意见不一致的问题中全部胜出，覆盖三个Qwen3.5模型。在所有基准测试中，top-nsigma维持了其温度1得分的中位数100%，而top-p仅维持26%。统计每个采样器通过的词元数量可解释原因：在一个Llama-3.1-8B的故事中，温度从1升至2时，top-p的词元池从4个增至51,845个，而top-nsigma始终保持在3个。在133种采样器设置与温度组合中，典型词元池大小与人工评分质量呈显著负相关（Spearman相关系数-0.77）。当我们在温度1时确定词元池大小，仅在之后应用温度调整，Top-H、min-p和p-less不再崩溃。在默认温度1下，大多数采样器表现相近。我们已公开所有代码和数据。

Weight-Space Programming Doesn't Need Transformers
权重空间编程不需要Transformer
Standard neural architectures such as the Transformer were chosen because they train well with gradient descent and run fast on GPUs. In weight-space programming, a large language model (LLM) writes target weights by hand without gradient-based training, so those reasons no longer apply. Should we keep the Transformer? Given minimal guidance, ten labeled examples, and a sandbox, mainly on multiple-choice questions, we task three frontier LLMs with either generating the weights of a fixed architecture or designing the architecture themselves. When free to design, all three compile plain-text facts into a sparse table queried by a fixed procedure, reaching up to 54.4% test accuracy. An LLM-designed model achieves higher test accuracy than the most accurate released Transformer that the same LLM wrote while using about 1,800x fewer parameters. Without gradients, they choose a table, in which the LLM can state what each weight means and can find and rewrite a wrong one. Each wrong answer points to one row, and removing a row changes about one other answer. From this, we propose an associative-memory-based model up to 17x faster on GPUs than a same-sized Transformer. We also test pretrained weights, other benchmarks, and tasks where models generate answers. Pretrained LLMs remain more accurate; the claim concerns which structure to build when writing weights.
中文翻译
标准神经架构如Transformer之所以被采用，是因为它们能通过梯度下降有效训练并在GPU上快速运行。但在权重空间编程中，大型语言模型（LLM）手动编写目标权重，无需基于梯度的训练，因此这些理由不再适用。我们是否仍应保留Transformer？在仅有少量引导、十个标注示例和沙盒环境的前提下，我们让三个前沿LLM分别执行两项任务：生成固定架构的权重，或自行设计架构。当允许自由设计时，所有三个模型都将纯文本事实编译为由固定程序查询的稀疏表，最高测试准确率达54.4%。一个由LLM设计的模型在测试准确率上超过同一LLM用固定Transformer架构写出的最准确版本，且仅使用约1/1800的参数量。在无梯度的情况下，它们选择了一种表格结构，使LLM能够明确说明每个权重的含义，并可定位和修改错误权重。每个错误答案对应一行，移除一行大约影响另一个答案。基于此，我们提出一种基于关联记忆的模型，其在GPU上的速度比同尺寸Transformer快达17倍。我们还测试了预训练权重、其他基准和模型生成答案的任务。预训练LLM仍更准确；本研究关注的是在手动写权重时应构建何种结构。

Thinking Harder Doesn't Lead to Better Choices: Reasoning Effort Increases the Confidence but Not the Correctness of LLM Shopping Agents
更深入思考并不带来更好选择：推理努力增加信心但不提升正确性
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous shopping agents that process multimodal product listings (consisting of visual and text data) and recommend purchases on behalf of consumers. Naturally, one might assume that models with more thinking power would make higher-quality purchasing decisions. To test this belief, we ask two questions about such agents: does asking a model to think harder make it choose better products, and does the confidence it reports tell us the likelihood it may be wrong? We benchmark ten frontier models on three product-choice benchmarks built from WebShop, Amazon's SQID and Wayfair's WANDS platforms to see if LLMs with more thinking power actually make better-quality product purchase decisions. Across all three benchmarks, we found that increasing the thinking effort does not increase decision quality in a statistically meaningful way. The same change in thinking effort raises stated confidence at a statistically significant level, so extra thinking buys confidence rather than correctness. We also measure the calibration of these models (how well their reported confidence percentage matches their true correctness percentage) in these tasks and show that most models are not well-calibrated at every effort level. We also find that errors are strongly shared: when one model errs in a task, several others tend to err on that same task. Furthermore, on tasks where several models all err, a large number of them pick the same wrong product.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地作为自主购物代理部署，处理包含视觉和文本数据的多模态商品列表，并代表消费者推荐购买。自然地，人们可能认为具有更强推理能力的模型会做出更高质量的购买决策。为检验这一信念，我们提出两个问题：要求模型更深入思考是否会使其选择更好的产品？其报告的信心水平是否能反映其出错的可能性？我们在三个基于WebShop、Amazon的SQID和Wayfair的WANDS平台构建的产品选择基准上，对十款前沿模型进行了基准测试，以验证拥有更强推理能力的模型是否真的能做出更优的购买决策。在所有三个基准上，增加推理努力并未在统计学意义上提升决策质量。同一变化显著提高了模型的陈述信心，表明额外思考带来的只是信心而非正确性。我们还测量了这些模型在不同推理努力水平下的校准度（报告信心百分比与其真实正确率匹配程度），发现大多数模型在各努力水平下均未良好校准。此外，我们发现错误高度共享：当一个模型在某任务出错时，其他多个模型也倾向于在同一任务出错。尤其在多个模型同时出错的任务中，大量模型会选择相同的错误商品。
