SkillBuffBench: Measuring What Agent Skills Contribute across Harnesses, Models, Skill Access Modes and Memory
SkillBuffBench：跨Harness、模型、技能访问模式与记忆衡量代理技能贡献
Agent Skills, file-based packages of procedural knowledge that a coding agent can discover and load on demand, are now shipped by every major agent harness, yet the evidence for how much they help comes almost entirely from settings in which the right Skill is hand-picked for the task and the agent starts from a blank slate. Neither holds in deployment, where an agent chooses from whatever library happens to be installed and frequently carries memory from earlier work. We present SkillBuffBench, a benchmark and paired evaluation protocol that treats Skill access as a three-level factor (no Skill, a curated Skill, and automatic invocation from an installed library), crosses it with memory (off and on), with four agent harnesses and with multiple models (the state-of-the-art proprietary models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI, and the state-of-the-art open-weight models from Chinese providers), every model being run under every harness, and evaluates the result on research tasks contributed by domain experts together with the Skills they use, currently in materials science and biology, with the suite extended on a rolling basis. Every run is marked by a deterministic verifier; uncertainty is reported with a paired, task-level bootstrap; and cost, agent rounds and wall-clock time are reported alongside the score rather than folded into it. This version of the paper describes the design of the benchmark, its evaluation protocol and its current tasks and Skills. The evaluation is in progress: results will be added once the runs are complete, and the task suite, results and analysis will continue to be updated on a rolling basis.
中文翻译
代理技能——即文件形式的程序知识包，编码代理可按需发现并加载——如今已被各大代理Harness提供，但现有证据几乎完全来自理想化场景：任务中手动挑选正确技能，且代理从空白状态开始。然而在实际部署中，代理只能从当前安装的库中选择，且经常携带先前工作的记忆。我们提出SkillBuffBench，一个基准测试及其配套评估协议，将技能访问视为三级因素（无技能、精选技能、自动调用已安装库中的技能），与记忆状态（开启/关闭）、四种代理Harness以及多种模型（Anthropic、Google和OpenAI的前沿专有模型，以及中国厂商提供的前沿开源模型）交叉组合，每种模型在每种Harness下运行，并在领域专家提供的研究任务上评估结果，目前涵盖材料科学和生物学领域，任务套件将持续滚动扩展。每次运行均由确定性验证器标记；不确定性通过任务级配对Bootstrap报告；成本、代理轮次和墙钟时间与分数一同报告，而非合并进分数。本文描述了该基准的设计、评估协议及其当前任务与技能。评估正在进行中：待运行完成后将补充结果，任务套件、结果与分析将持续滚动更新。

Large Language Models as Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: The Three Clocks of Depth, Training, and Generation
大语言模型作为非线性动力系统：深度、训练与生成的三时钟机制
Language models are dynamical systems. A transformer is iterated over its layers, over the steps of its training and over the tokens it generates, and on each of these three clocks it shows behavior that nonlinear dynamics has studied for a century: fixed points, bifurcations, synchronization, chaos and noise-driven escape from potential wells. The two communities rarely meet, so this behavior is often rediscovered under new names. We build a bridge between them. To describe, we condense about 200 papers into the Three-Clock Dictionary, which maps fifteen concepts of nonlinear dynamics onto each clock and marks every correspondence as proved, measured or conjectured, and we show each concept in a mechanical system and in a language model side by side. To explain, we derive reduced-order models: the softmax is a Boltzmann distribution, gradient descent an Euler step on a nonlinear spring, a loss spike a ball thrown out of a stiff valley, attention on the circle a Kuramoto model, and a repetition loop a well that deepens while it is occupied. We then test these explanations on open language models, on a laptop CPU. Escape from repetition loops in GPT-2 and Pythia follows the Arrhenius law with the logit gap as the barrier, and the barrier deepens and then saturates, so the temperature needed to leave a loop falls like $1/\ln N$ with the generation length $N$ and rises with model size. Coherent text lives in a narrow window of temperatures, which a small reasoning model shares at its recommended temperature. Training makes each Pythia layer more expansive, and a small transformer trains at a multi-mode edge of stability. The third step, solving problems of language models, is still ahead: controllers derived from the escape analysis remove loops but do not yet beat the best fixed temperature. We state twelve open problems where nonlinear dynamics could get there.
中文翻译
语言模型本质上是动力系统。Transformer在层间迭代、训练步骤中以及生成token的过程中，展现出非线性动力学研究百年来关注的现象：固定点、分岔、同步、混沌和噪声驱动的势阱逃逸。这两个领域很少交汇，导致许多现象被反复发现并赋予新名称。本文构建了两者的桥梁：我们提炼约200篇论文，形成‘三时钟词典’，将非线性动力学中的十五个概念映射到深度、训练和生成三个时间维度，并标注每种对应关系为已证明、已测量或假设；同时在机械系统与语言模型之间进行对比展示。为解释这些现象，我们推导出简化模型：softmax是玻尔兹曼分布，梯度下降是非线性弹簧上的欧拉步，损失尖峰如同从陡谷抛出的小球，注意力在圆周上表现为库朗莫托模型，而重复循环则是一个在占用期间不断加深的势阱。我们在开源语言模型上测试这些解释，仅使用笔记本CPU。GPT-2和Pythia中的重复循环逃逸遵循阿伦尼乌斯定律，以logit差值为势垒，该势垒先加深后饱和，因此离开循环所需的温度随生成长度N按$1/\ln N$下降，随模型规模增大而上升。连贯文本存在于狭窄的温度窗口中，一个小推理模型在其推荐温度下与之共享此特性。训练使每个Pythia层更具扩展性，小型Transformer在多模态稳定边缘训练。第三步——解决语言模型问题——仍待突破：基于逃逸分析设计的控制器可消除循环，但尚未超越最佳固定温度方案。我们提出了十二个开放问题，指出非线性动力学可能在此领域取得进展。

Beyond Bitwidth: Deployment-Constrained Mixed-Precision Quantization for Multilingual LLM Inference on NVIDIA Blackwell
超越位宽：面向NVIDIA Blackwell部署约束的多语言LLM混合精度量化推理
Low-bit LLM quantization is useful only if a model preserves service-relevant accuracy, executes through supported GPU kernels, and meets measured memory and latency limits. We study Qwen3-8B on NVIDIA B200 with vLLM, including INT2/INT3, GPTQ, NVFP4, and mixed-precision configurations. We verify native packed execution and precision on all 144 fused linear modules and evaluate Korean, English, numerical, and reasoning quality under preregistered gates. Packed INT2 and INT3 execute, but all seven tested INT2 grids degenerate on generation. Isolated module-restoration effects are strongly non-additive; Korean and reasoning sensitivity rankings disagree, and a promising INT4/INT8 development candidate fails independent final evaluation. A configuration with 16-bit activations, 98 NVFP4 weight-only modules, 46 INT8 modules, and an INT8 output head passes all gates on the untouched FINAL-81 set (5,168 items): 5.917 GiB of weight-loading memory (limit 5.98), Korean teacher-correct retention 0.904 (gate 0.90), and TPOT ratio 0.939 relative to BF16. It improves paired accuracy over NVFP4 W4A4 by +1.4 percentage points (Bonferroni-adjusted interval [+0.2, +2.6]), compared with +3.4 points on the selection set. This comparison changes both activation precision and INT8 protection; exploratory analyses suggest that the activation change contributes substantially, but the isolated allocation effect is not established. Final checkpoints were not retained, and bitwise rebuild reproducibility remains unverified.

Solver to System: LLM Reasoning Across Representations and Execution in Scientific Simulation
从求解器到系统：LLM在科学模拟中跨表示推理与执行
Simulators based on partial differential equations (PDEs) are widely used to model physical systems, but generating and interpreting PDE solver code with large language models (LLMs) remains challenging. Scientific simulation code is a useful testbed for LLM scientific reasoning because (1) one system can be represented by code, equations, natural language, and numerical trajectories and (2) code can execute successfully while implementing invalid physics. We introduce Solver2System, a dataset of PDE solvers with multiple representations, controlled corruptions, and lexical perturbations, to evaluate whether LLMs can recover the underlying physical system through (1) cross-representation consistency and (2) static and agentic detection of physical invalidity. Across eight open-weight models, LLMs systematically favor some representations when evidence conflicts, and lexical perturbations to code alter interpretation of other unchanged representations. Execution improves physical-validity judgments, but weaker agents can judge correctly without identifying the causal defect. Thus, LLMs may reach correct judgments despite inconsistent representation reconciliation or causal misdiagnosis.
中文翻译
基于偏微分方程（PDEs）的模拟器广泛用于建模物理系统，但利用大语言模型（LLMs）生成和解释PDE求解器代码仍具挑战性。科学模拟代码是LLM科学推理的理想测试平台，因为（1）同一系统可通过代码、方程、自然语言和数值轨迹等多种形式表示，（2）代码可成功执行却实现无效物理。我们引入Solver2System数据集，包含多种表示形式、受控破坏和词汇扰动的PDE求解器，以评估LLMs能否通过（1）跨表示一致性恢复底层物理系统，以及（2）静态与代理式检测物理无效性。在八种开源权重模型中，当证据冲突时，LLMs系统性偏好某些表示形式；代码的词汇扰动会改变对其他未改变表示的理解。执行有助于提升物理有效性判断，但较弱的代理即使未识别因果缺陷也能正确判断。因此，LLMs可能在表示不一致协调或因果误诊断的情况下仍能得出正确结论。
👤 Eliza Berman, Rohan Subramanian, Shreya Jha, Lohit Jagarapu, Romain Lopez et al. 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21946]
agentic-rl
Conserved Verdicts, Drifting Commitments: Identifying Harmful Overthinking
恒定判断，漂移承诺：识别有害的过度思考
A reasoning model that has reached a correct answer can lose it by continuing to think. Deciding whether to stop means comparing two probabilities at the same point of a chain: that the answer the model would give if stopped now is correct (its commitment), and that the answer it will finish with is correct (its verdict). We show that the verdict alone, which is what final-correctness probes estimate, cannot make this comparison. As a forecast of a fixed outcome it has no predictable drift, whether continuing helps or hurts. The commitment-verdict gap equals the expected remaining change in answer quality and the accuracy gained by stopping. A second identity splits any accuracy-versus-budget curve into answer change within chains and change in which chains are still running. We measure both on MATH-500. At all 379 points where DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B holds a correct answer, selected without regard to how its chain ends, continuing loses 3.6 points on average (95% CI [2.2, 5.2]), and the loss concentrates in a few problems. In six reasoning models from three families, on MATH-500 and OlympiadBench and with budgets up to 16,384 tokens, declining accuracy curves come from which chains remain, while the chains still reasoning keep or improve their answers. An oracle stopping rule gains at most +1.8 and +2.5 points at 1.5B and 7B. The framework says what to measure before building a stopping rule.
中文翻译
一个已得出正确答案的推理模型可能在继续思考后失去该答案。决定是否停止需要在同一链条中的某一点比较两个概率：即如果现在停止模型会给出的答案是正确的（其承诺），以及它最终完成时的答案是正确的（其判决）。我们表明，仅凭判决（这是最终正确性探测所估计的内容）无法进行这种比较。作为对固定结果的预测，它没有可预测的漂移，无论继续思考是有益还是有害。承诺与判决之间的差距等于答案质量预期的剩余变化量，以及通过停止所获得的准确率增益。第二个恒等式将准确率与预算的曲线分解为链内答案的变化和仍在运行的链的变化。我们在MATH-500上测量了这两者。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B保持正确答案的全部379个点上（不考虑其链条如何结束），继续思考平均损失3.6分（95%置信区间[2.2, 5.2]），且损失集中在少数问题中。在六个来自三个家族的推理模型上，使用MATH-500和OlympiadBench，并在高达16,384个token的预算下，准确率下降曲线源于哪些链条仍在运行，而仍在推理的链条则保持或提升其答案。一个理想停止规则最多可带来+1.8和+2.5分的增益（分别对应1.5B和7B模型）。该框架指明了在构建停止规则之前应测量什么。
👤 Jianghong Zhou, Hao Rong, Chen Lin 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21707]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Bookkeeping Is Not Judgement: EJBench, an Engineering Judgement Benchmark Built from a Conjugate Heat Transfer Design Campaign
账目管理并非判断：基于共轭传热设计活动构建的工程判断基准EJBench
We introduce EJBench, a benchmark of engineering judgement: whether a model can predict how a design behaves at new conditions from a few earlier results, and decide on it. Unlike engineering benchmarks that test whether an agent can operate simulation or CAD software, EJBench is software-agnostic and asks what an engineer would conclude without running a simulation. Its tasks come from a conjugate heat transfer design campaign for battery cold plates, in three families: matching unlabelled test results to their operating points, choosing which stored runs to reveal before signing off a design, and ranking new duties by peak cell temperature. Every answer is taken from a converged simulation, not from an LLM judge. The best of ten frontier models, GPT-6 Astra, scores 0.71, and models state almost the same confidence on tasks they get wrong as on tasks they get right. We also post-train Qwen3.5-9B with LoRA and reinforcement learning, rewarded by the share of answer criteria the simulation results confirm; on the held-out test split its score rises from 0.071 to 0.106. We release the train, validation and test splits, the evaluation harness and the leaderboard.
中文翻译
我们引入EJBench，一个工程判断的基准测试：评估模型能否从少量早期结果预测新条件下设计的表现，并据此做出决策。不同于测试代理是否能操作仿真或CAD软件的工程基准，EJBench与软件无关，询问的是工程师在不运行仿真时会得出什么结论。其任务源自电池冷板的共轭传热设计活动，分为三类：将未标记的测试结果匹配到其工作点，决定在批准设计前揭示哪些存储的运行结果，以及按峰值电芯温度对新工况进行排序。每个答案均来自收敛的仿真结果，而非由LLM裁判提供。十种前沿模型中表现最佳的GPT-6 Astra得分为0.71，而在错误任务和正确任务上，模型表现出几乎相同的置信度。我们还对Qwen3.5-9B使用LoRA和强化学习进行后训练，奖励机制基于仿真结果确认的答案标准占比；在保留测试集上，其得分从0.071提升至0.106。我们发布了训练、验证和测试划分数据集、评估工具包及排行榜。
👤 James Sadler, Rotimi Alabi, Kotub Uddin 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21645]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
Asking What Matters: Disagreement-Guided Ambiguity Detection for Interactive Text-to-SQL
关注关键问题：基于分歧引导的交互式文本转SQL模糊性检测
Interactive text-to-SQL requires an LLM agent not only to generate executable SQL, but also to decide which uncertainty to clarify within a limited interaction budget. However, current clarification policies have three limitations: (i) they target surface ambiguity without testing its SQL impact; (ii) they miss ambiguities implicit in schema routes or domain knowledge; and (iii) they spend limited turns on unfocused or redundant questions. These limitations expose a critical challenge: identifying SQL-impacting ambiguity before the intended SQL is known. In this paper, we propose DisGuAD, a Disagreement-Guided Ambiguity Detection framework that specializes clarification selection. DisGuAD treats disagreement among plausible SQL interpretations as direct evidence of which uncertainty matters to the final query. Specifically, it constructs a query-term inventory, elicits contrastive SQL interpretations through complementary probe families, compares their canonical SQL components, and attributes recurring differences to user-facing clarification targets. A lightweight prioritization and dialogue-state mechanism then selects one high-impact unresolved target for clarification. Experiments on BIRD-Interact Lite and Full with two LLM backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in task success and clarification quality. Compared with the original interaction policy, DisGuAD improves normalized Reward by 4.40--8.97 points and raises overall critical-ambiguity coverage from 48.62% to 82.75% and from 45.76% to 85.14%. Its clarification signals further improve two independently designed static text-to-SQL frameworks by up to 13.97 Reward points while keeping their SQL generators fixed. These results demonstrate that disagreement among plausible SQL queries provides actionable evidence for selecting clarification targets that matter to downstream generation.
中文翻译
交互式文本转SQL要求大语言模型代理不仅生成可执行的SQL，还需在有限的交互预算内决定澄清哪些不确定性。然而，现有的澄清策略存在三个局限：(i) 仅针对表面模糊性，未检验其对SQL的影响；(ii) 忽略了模式路径或领域知识中隐含的模糊性；(iii) 在无关或冗余问题上消耗了有限的交互轮次。这些局限暴露了一个关键挑战：在知晓目标SQL之前识别出影响SQL结果的模糊性。本文提出DisGuAD（分歧引导的模糊性检测）框架，专注于澄清选择。DisGuAD将多个合理SQL解释之间的分歧视为最终查询中关键不确定性的直接证据。具体而言，它构建查询项清单，通过互补探测族获取对比性SQL解释，比较其标准SQL组件，并将反复出现的差异归因于面向用户的澄清目标。随后，轻量级优先级排序与对话状态机制选择一个高影响且未解决的目标进行澄清。在BIRD-Interact Lite和Full数据集上，使用两种LLM骨干网络的实验表明，任务成功率和澄清质量均有持续提升。相比原始交互策略，DisGuAD使归一化奖励提升4.40–8.97分，总体关键模糊性覆盖率从48.62%提升至82.75%，从45.76%提升至85.14%。其澄清信号还使两个独立设计的静态文本转SQL框架的性能最高提升13.97分奖励，同时保持其SQL生成器不变。结果表明，合理SQL查询间的分歧为选择对下游生成至关重要的澄清目标提供了可操作的证据。
👤 Su Dong, Jiayan Lin, Yujia Liu, Zijin Hong, Qinggang Zhang et al. 📅 2026-10-14 🔗 arXiv

[2611.23218]
What Does a Bias Head Measure? Non-Identifiability in Head-Level Bias Attribution
偏见头测量的是什么？注意力头层面偏见归因的不可识别性
Methods that localise social bias in large language models to individual attention heads and remove it by ablation are validated by their effect on a fairness metric, never by evidence that the statistic selecting the heads measures bias. We audit this on Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The head map correlates at $ρ= -0.950$ and $-0.768$ with a label-blind response-length map, apparently identifying a surface confound. An intervention that breaks the length-label coupling rejects this reading: with the prompt held fixed, response length explains 1% of the statistic's variance. What does explain it is prompt identity, at 64% of variance, in an ordering that is not monotonic in length. Within any fixed prompt the fairness label, response length and the presence of a protected-group token are collinear at $φ= 0.95$, partly by definition of the labelling rule, so no observational analysis can attribute the map to any of them, and no method in this family performs an experiment that could. The substantive claims fail independently: bias selectivity is not late-layer-concentrated on either model under either reference reading, and no head set we tested reduces the unfairness rate when ablated. Its complement, scored on the opposite label, reliably destroys refusal behaviour ($p = 2.7\times10^{-5}$), demonstrating that the pipeline detects effects at this scale.
中文翻译
将大型语言模型中的社会偏见定位到单个注意力头并通过对这些头进行消融来消除偏见的方法，其有效性仅通过公平性指标的变化来验证，从未通过证据证明用于选择头的统计量确实衡量了偏见。我们在Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上对此进行了审计。结果显示，头映射与无标签响应长度映射的相关系数分别为ρ = -0.950和-0.768，似乎识别出了一个表面混淆因素。一项打破长度-标签耦合的干预实验表明：在提示固定的情况下，响应长度仅解释了该统计量方差的1%。真正解释其方差的是提示身份，占比达64%，且该关系并非随长度单调变化。在任意固定提示下，公平性标签、响应长度和受保护群体词元的存在性高度共线（φ = 0.95），部分源于标注规则的定义，因此任何观测分析都无法将该映射归因于其中任一因素，而此类方法中也无任何一种执行可检验的实验。实质主张独立失败：无论采用何种参考解读，偏见选择性均未集中在晚期层；我们测试的所有头集合在消融后均未降低不公平率。其补集在相反标签上的评分则可靠地破坏了拒绝行为（p = 2.7×10⁻⁵），表明该流水线能够检测到这一尺度的影响。
👤 S.M Seefat Alam 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.23185]
Polarity Composition Hypothesis: Causal Evidence from Negation in NLI
极性组合假说：自然语言推理中否定的因果证据
We investigate how language models process negation in natural language inference (NLI). We propose the \textit{Polarity Composition Hypothesis}, a high-level causal model of this computation. Behavioral evaluations show that language-model predictions largely conform to the proposed hypothesis. Using Distributed Alignment Search, we identify causally effective subspaces aligned with the variables in the polarity-composition hypothesis. These representations transfer across negation forms and partially to a subset of MNLI. We release the dataset and code.
中文翻译
我们研究语言模型如何处理自然语言推理（NLI）中的否定。我们提出了一个高阶因果模型——极性组合假说，用于描述这一计算过程。行为评估显示，语言模型的预测结果在很大程度上符合该假说。利用分布式对齐搜索，我们识别出与极性组合假说中变量对齐的因果有效子空间。这些表示在不同否定形式间具有迁移能力，并部分迁移到MNLI的一个子集。我们已发布数据集和代码。

[2611.23158]
MoE--LoRA Methods for Multilingual Adaptation: A Systematic Empirical Study
用于多语言适配的MoE-LoRA方法：一项系统性实证研究
Routed low-rank adapters offer a parameter-efficient means to improve the performance of large language models (LLMs) across tasks. Hence, it is assumed that they can also be used to improve the support of low-resource languages in LLMs, which currently serve English far better than the thousands of other languages spoken worldwide. However, this assumption has never been verified empirically under a shared multilingual evaluation protocol. We address exactly this gap by comparing eight low-rank adaptation (LoRA) methods on a frozen base model using 200 unique languages in more than 9{,}000 evaluation settings. Our results suggest that a reduced-scale search can identify viable configurations of LoRA methods but does not reliably predict their relative performance, as the top-ranking configuration is never best at scale. LoRA reliably improves language-model fit on translation data, consistently degrades reading comprehension, and benefits low-resource languages most. These findings suggest that LoRA methods should be regarded as targeted tools rather than general-purpose means for improving the multilingual capabilities of LLMs.
中文翻译
路由低秩适配器为提升大型语言模型（LLMs）在各类任务上的性能提供了一种参数高效的手段。因此，人们普遍认为它们也可用于增强LLMs对低资源语言的支持，而目前这些模型对英语的支持远优于全球数千种其他语言。然而，这一假设从未在统一的多语言评估协议下得到实证验证。我们通过在冻结的基础模型上比较八种低秩适配（LoRA）方法，在超过9,000个评估设置中使用200种不同语言，填补了这一空白。我们的结果表明，缩小规模的搜索可以识别出可行的LoRA配置，但无法可靠预测其相对性能，因为排名最高的配置在大规模下并不总是最优。LoRA在翻译数据上能可靠提升语言模型拟合度，却始终会降低阅读理解表现，并对低资源语言带来最大益处。这些发现表明，LoRA方法应被视为针对性工具，而非通用手段来提升LLMs的多语言能力。
👤 Nikit Srivastava, Joel Dag, René Speck, Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.23093]
What's in a Tree ? Hierarchical $n$-gram models as controlled approximators of language
树形结构中蕴含什么？层级n-gram模型作为语言的受控近似器
Hierarchical models have been proposed as synthetic settings for studying language. However, their relevance to realistic language corpora remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce tree-grams, autoregressive generative $n$-gram models that build the conditional probability tensor $p(x_n \mid x_{1:n-1})$ through a hierarchical composition of order-2 and order-3 tensors. $n$ controls the maximal context window, whereas a rank hyperparameter $d$ controls model capacity, yielding an interpretable and systematically scalable family of approximators of language. We show how tree-grams outperform existing $n$-gram baselines, while remaining competitive with deep transformer-based language models. We demonstrate the utility of such controlled approximations by using tree-grams as generative models. In particular, we show that hyperparameter transfer under $μ$P can be made more robust when narrower models are tuned on simpler data, generated by tree-grams with moderate $n$ and $d$. In addition, we show that entropy and effective range of correlations control performance gap after fine-tuning. This is because tree-grams allow us to explicitly control horizon and capacity. When allowed to route across candidate topologies, we observe emergent sparsity in tree-grams. Such concentrated routing permits pruning upto 95\% of topologies without significant change in performance.
中文翻译
层级模型已被提议作为研究语言的合成环境，但其与真实语料的相关性仍待深入探索。本文引入树形n-gram（tree-grams），一种自回归生成式n-gram模型，通过二阶和三阶张量的层级组合构建条件概率张量p(x_n | x_{1:n-1})。n控制最大上下文窗口，而秩超参数d控制模型容量，从而形成一个可解释且系统可扩展的语言近似器家族。我们展示了树形n-gram在性能上优于现有n-gram基线，同时与基于Transformer的深度语言模型保持竞争力。通过将树形n-gram用作生成模型，展示了此类受控近似的实用性。特别地，当在较简单数据上使用窄模型进行调优时，μP下的超参数迁移更具鲁棒性，这些数据由中等n和d的树形n-gram生成。此外，我们发现熵和相关性的有效范围控制微调后的性能差距，因为树形n-gram允许我们显式控制视野和容量。当允许在候选拓扑间路由时，我们观察到树形n-gram中出现涌现稀疏性，这种集中路由使得在不显著影响性能的情况下可剪枝高达95%的拓扑结构。
👤 Aritra Das, Francesco Cagnetta, Darshil Doshi, Victor V. Albert, Maissam Barkeshli et al. 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22975]
Large Language Models Have Not Yet Transformed Science
大语言模型尚未真正改变科学
The emergence of large language models (LLMs) in late 2022 has raised questions about the future of science, but assessing their impact requires understanding how scientific practices were already evolving. We analyze 2.5 million full-text papers across five disciplines from 2015 to 2025, tracing trends over a broad spectrum of lexical, structural, epistemological, and social dimensions. Aside from frequency shifts in some words, most pre-2023 trends continued with limited detectable breaks. Among the notable exceptions, growth in computer science coauthor teams accelerated significantly in Asia, while apparent declines in biomedical topic diversity largely reflected standardization in article formats. Our study establishes an empirical foundation for monitoring the health of global science as LLM adoption deepens.
中文翻译
2022年底大语言模型（LLMs）的出现引发了关于科学未来走向的讨论，但评估其影响需要理解科学实践本身早已存在的演变趋势。我们分析了2015年至2025年间五个学科领域的250万篇全文论文，追踪了词汇、结构、认识论和社交等多个维度的广泛趋势。除了部分词语频率的变化外，大多数2023年前的趋势持续发展，未见明显断裂。值得注意的例外包括：亚洲计算机科学合作团队的增长显著加速；生物医学主题多样性的所谓下降，实际上主要反映文章格式的标准化。本研究为随着LLM采用的深化，监测全球科学健康状况提供了实证基础。
👤 Ron Bekkerman, Nichole Li, Bahareh Kargar, Mark Somers 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22827]
Compact and Decodable Electrocardiogram Text for Large Language Models
紧凑且可解码的心电图文本以供大语言模型使用
When a twelve-lead electrocardiogram is written as text for a large language model, the number of tokens has to be balanced against the information lost through compression. This paper presents a deterministic encoding that writes eight independent leads as amplitude letters and exact time gaps and recovers the remaining four limb leads algebraically. The key points are selected with a cardiac-cycle-dependent tolerance, the amplitudes are parameterized by a 26-letter logarithmic codebook learned from a training set, and a 10-step tolerance schedule keeps all records within a fixed token budget. The encoding was applied to 21,388 records from the public PTB-XL database and evaluated using an exact decode-and-re-encode round trip, waveform reconstruction error, and a paired comparison of convolutional classifiers trained with 5 different seeds on signals from the original and decoded databases. All records were contained in 4,080 tokens (average: 2,866 tokens, 64.3% fewer than a per-sample transcription), and all stored lead strings were exactly reproduced. The average correlation between the decoded and reference signals was 0.982. The macro-averaged area under the receiver operating characteristic curve was 0.9167 for the reference signals and 0.9157 for the decoded signals (paired difference -0.0010; 95% patient-level bootstrap interval, -0.0028 to 0.0007), within the pre-specified non-inferiority margin of -0.010; this is not a confirmatory result, because the test fold had been used during encoder development. The diagnostic content of a compact text can therefore be preserved, while the language model's ability to read the text is yet another matter.
中文翻译
当十二导联心电图被转化为大语言模型可读的文本时，必须在标记数量与压缩带来的信息损失之间取得平衡。本文提出一种确定性编码方法，将八个独立导联表示为幅度字母和精确时间间隔，并通过代数方式恢复剩余四个肢体导联。关键点的选择基于心脏周期依赖的容差，幅度采用由训练集学习得到的26字母对数编码表参数化，10步容差调度确保所有记录保持在固定标记预算内。该编码方法应用于公开的PTB-XL数据库中的21,388条记录，并通过精确解码与重编码往返测试、波形重建误差，以及使用原始数据库和解码数据库训练的卷积分类器（5个不同种子）的配对比较进行评估。所有记录均包含在4,080个标记内（平均2,866个标记，比单样本转录减少64.3%），所有存储的导联字符串均被精确复现。解码信号与参考信号之间的平均相关系数为0.982。参考信号的宏平均ROC曲线下面积为0.9167，解码信号为0.9157（配对差异-0.0010；95%患者级Bootstrap区间：-0.0028至0.0007），在预先设定的非劣效边际-0.010范围内；此结果并非确认性，因测试折已被用于编码器开发阶段。因此，紧凑文本可以保留诊断内容，但语言模型是否能有效读取该文本则是另一问题。
👤 Elif Dorukbaşı, İlker Türker 📅 2026-11-17 🔗 arXiv
[2611.22708]
Cross-cultural Adaptation of Recipes for Low-Resource Languages: A Benchmark and Multilingual Culinary Lexicon for Hausa, Yorùbá, and Igbo
低资源语言的食谱跨文化适配：豪萨语、约鲁巴语和伊博语的基准与多语言烹饪词典
Recent multilingual large language models (LLMs) have improved machine translation, yet culturally adaptive translation for low-resource languages (LRLs) remains underexplored. This is particularly challenging for recipes, where ingredients, cooking tools, measurements, and procedures may require adaptation to the target cultural context. In this work, we introduce ReciLoReL, a benchmark of 600 expert-translated and culturally adapted recipes across Yorùbá, Hausa, and Igbo (three of the most prominent languages in Nigeria), and ReciLex, a multilingual culinary lexicon containing approximately 15,000 translated entities across ten recipe entity categories, including ingredients, tools, chef actions, state of tools, and durations. We evaluate state-of-the-art multilingual LLMs for English-to-LRL recipe translation under zero-shot and few-shot settings and investigate whether augmenting LLMs with ReciLex improves culturally adaptive recipe translation without requiring large target-language recipe corpora for training or fine-tuning. Beyond conventional reference-based translation evaluation using BLEU, chrF, and TER, we introduce five criteria for evaluating cultural adaptation, adapted from established cross-cultural adaptation methods. Across the selected LLMs and languages, ReciLex improves average BLEU from 13.57 to 14.51 and chrF from 37.89 to 38.74, while reducing TER from 77.14 to 75.12. Human evaluation further shows improvements ranging from 0.11 to 0.36 points across the five cultural adaptation evaluation criteria on a five-point scale. Overall, our work provides new resources, evaluation criteria, and empirical evaluation for studying culturally adaptive recipe translation in low-resource settings.
中文翻译
近年来，多语言大语言模型（LLMs）在机器翻译方面取得了进展，但针对低资源语言（LRLs）的文化适应性翻译仍研究不足。这在食谱翻译中尤为突出，因为食材、烹饪工具、度量单位和操作步骤可能需要根据目标文化背景进行调整。本文提出了ReciLoReL，一个涵盖约鲁巴语、豪萨语和伊博语（尼日利亚三大主要语言）的600个专家翻译并经过文化适配的食谱基准，以及ReciLex，一个包含约15,000个跨十类食谱实体（如食材、工具、厨师动作、工具状态、时长等）的多语言烹饪词典。我们在零样本和少样本设置下评估了最先进的多语言LLMs在英译LRL食谱任务中的表现，并探究将ReciLex引入LLMs是否能在无需大规模目标语言食谱语料库进行训练或微调的情况下提升文化适应性食谱翻译效果。除了传统的基于参考文本的BLEU、chrF和TER评估外，我们还引入了五项基于成熟跨文化适配方法的文化适应性评估标准。在所选LLMs和语言上，ReciLex使平均BLEU从13.57提升至14.51，chrF从37.89提升至38.74，TER从77.14降至75.12。人工评估进一步显示，在五点量表上，各项文化适应性评估标准得分提升0.11至0.36分。总体而言，本工作为低资源环境下研究文化适应性食谱翻译提供了新资源、评估标准和实证评估框架。

[2611.22705]
Route Optimization under Port Congestion and Carbon Reduction: An NSGA-II Approach
港口拥堵与碳减排下的航线优化：基于NSGA-II的方法
Maritime shipping simultaneously faces the dual challenges of escalating port congestion and increasingly stringent decarbonization mandates. Large-scale vessel anchorage at hub ports inflates unit transport costs while generating significant additional \(CO_2\) emissions from idling auxiliary engines. This study develops a multi-objective routing optimization framework that jointly incorporates time, transport, and carbon emission costs under congestion-dependent generalized cost functions. A variational inequality (VI) model is formulated to characterize network equilibrium in vessel route allocation, while the Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II (NSGA-II) is deployed to solve the resulting three-objective optimization problem. The model is empirically validated using China's coastal container network (Shanghai as the primary congested port, Ningbo-Zhoushan as the backup alternative), with sensitivity analyses conducted across congestion levels, carbon tax rates, and fuel price differentials. Key findings indicate that: (1) alternative ports effectively hedge congestion when diversion costs fall below waiting costs, with a diversion threshold at approximately 60 vessel calls; (2) a carbon tax of 80/t \(CO_2\) pushes clean fuel adoption above 50%; and (3) fuel prices primarily influence energy-type selection rather than port diversion decisions, confirming that the two decision dimensions operate independently. These findings offer actionable decision support for port authorities, regulators, and shipping operators seeking to balance economic efficiency with environmental sustainability.

[2611.22685]
Visibility Graph Representations of Word Embeddings for Text Classification
用于文本分类的词嵌入的可视图表示
Text classifiers depend on representations that expose the distinctions between classes in a form the learning model can use, and a restricted vocabulary changes which representation is most effective. This paper studies natural visibility graphs as an intermediate representation for news topic classification under controlled vocabulary budgets. Word embeddings are averaged into a fixed-length document vector, the vector is read as an ordered numerical series, and its natural visibility graph is passed to a classifier either as an ordered node-degree vector or as an adjacency matrix. On the four-class AG News benchmark, chi-square selection retains 100 to 1,500 words, and the graph inputs are compared with direct embedding-based representations from continuous bag-of-words, skip-gram, GloVe, FastText and BERT. With a fully connected classifier, the degree representation is the most accurate configuration from 700 selected words onward; it reaches its highest accuracy of 91.24% at 1,000 words and exceeds the BERT-based baseline by 0.29 percentage points at 1,500 words. In a separate convolutional comparison, the adjacency input reaches 86.16% at 1,000 words, against 83.12% for the strongest static embedding. The results indicate that visibility-derived features can be competitive once the selected vocabulary provides adequate coverage. We discuss the roles of graph compression, coordinate ordering and classifier choice, and the controls needed to establish the reliability of the small differences observed.

[2611.22665]
Contract-Explicit Evaluation: From Auditing Learned Readers to Scoped Certificates
合同显式评估：从审计学习阅读器到范围化证书
Evaluation readings of document-level relation extraction (DocRE) systems depend jointly on the model and the evaluation contract: gold version, scoring function, threshold. Rankings reverse across contracts, yet no existing method determines which comparisons survive the reversal. We address this gap with contract-explicit evaluation: a preregistered five-step protocol (Declare, Measure, Scope, Certify, Hedge), operationalizing the classical concordance decomposition of Kendall's rank correlation, that scopes certifiable regions and delivers distribution-free census certificates across two ecosystems. In the DocRE audit (44 training runs of one in-house architecture family), the scoring dimension scopes to a census certificate (three flagged instances, Clopper--Pearson upper bound 0.146); the confirmatory record includes registered negatives, and the audit prices its own simplification finding---the calibration dial's entire margin is one withdrawn commit. In an LLM-judge census (17 judges, 49,938 battles), 12.25% of leaderboard comparisons require hedging at the pre-declared split criterion (bound 0.124; panel-robust 0.143), with an exploratory two-tier reading certifying 69% at a bound \le 0.072 and a validity decomposition against released human labels (65% of contested battles are judge error, not ambiguity). The cross-model certificate is withdrawn on real confidence scores; the failure spectrum maps the boundary. The protocol, map, ledger, and per-instance artifacts are released.

[2611.22619]
Understanding Cross-Modal Inconsistency through Representation Subspaces in Omni Models
通过Omni模型中表示子空间理解跨模态不一致
Omni Models process audio, vision, and text within a unified language backbone, yet often produce different answers for semantically equivalent inputs across modalities. Existing representation-level analyses do not distinguish between information that predicts whether disagreement will occur and the representation whose perturbation affects the model's answer. In this paper, we estimate a low-dimensional Answer Subspace from answer-position representations and use it to separate answer-related components from the remaining representation. Across three Omni Models and two multimodal reasoning tasks, information predictive of cross-modal inconsistency lies mainly outside the Answer Subspace, concentrating in a reproducible two-dimensional representation that is not explained by a fixed modality offset. Matched interventions reveal distinct behavioral roles: perturbing the Answer Subspace substantially changes the model's predictions, whereas perturbing the disagreement representation, like a random subspace of the same dimensionality, leaves them almost unchanged. Our key finding is that cross-modal disagreement can be induced by misaligning modality-specific answer representations: perturbing the Answer Subspace for only one modality induces disagreement, whereas applying the same perturbation to both largely preserves agreement. Thus, the representation that predicts disagreement is distinct from the answer-level representation through which disagreement can be induced, showing that linear decodability does not imply behavioral efficacy.
中文翻译
Omni模型在统一的语言主干中处理音频、视觉和文本，但对语义等价的输入在不同模态下常产生不同的答案。现有的表示层面分析无法区分预测分歧是否会发生的信息与影响模型答案的表示。本文从答案位置表示中估计一个低维答案子空间，并用其将与答案相关的成分与其他表示分离。在三个Omni模型和两个多模态推理任务上，预测跨模态不一致的信息主要存在于答案子空间之外，集中在可重复的二维表示中，且不能由固定的模态偏移解释。匹配干预实验揭示了不同的行为角色：扰动答案子空间会显著改变模型预测，而扰动分歧表示（如同维度的随机子空间）则几乎不改变预测。关键发现是，跨模态分歧可通过错位特定模态的答案表示来诱导：仅对某一模态扰动答案子空间会引发分歧，而对两者施加相同扰动则基本保持一致性。因此，预测分歧的表示与可引发分歧的答案级表示是不同的，表明线性可解码性并不意味着行为有效性。

[2611.22499]
From syllable representation via gesture generation to articulation: A spiking neural model

Speech production requires a transformation from phonological representations into coordinated vocal-tract movements. This paper presents a spiking neural sensorimotor model within the Neural Engineering Framework and Semantic Pointer Architecture. Each syllable occurrence has a neural oscillator whose evolving phase coordinates its gestures and initiates the next syllable. Phase-dependent commands recruit separate neural populations for vocalic, oral-constriction, velopharyngeal, and glottal actions (gestures). Neural ramp integrators shape the rise and fall of gesture activation. Together with articulatory targets, these trajectories determine the extent and time course of articulatory movements. Opponent neural populations provide control signals to a geometric articulatory model. Thirty reference recordings cover an input corpus of eight lexical words, an alternative realization of one word, a syllable sequence, and isolated syllables. Two additional paired simulation subcorpora of sixty runs each, covering two words and one syllable sequence, assess activation reliability across neural realizations. Adjusting gesture command amplitudes increased successful realizations from 57/60 to 60/60 under the specified criteria, with ramp rate factors held constant. The model demonstrates how neural oscillator and ramp dynamics generate articulatory timing under specified phasing rules. It complements a preceding mental-lexicon model through an ordered syllable interface. Continuous online execution across all components and connection to the acoustic extension remain further integration steps. Accessible code and synchronized visualization support inspection of the neural activation trajectories and resulting vocal-tract movements.

Growing Clusters Lower the Bar: Profile Accretion in LLM-Based Incremental Entity Resolution

Incremental entity resolution pipelines ask a large language model (LLM) whether a new record belongs to an existing cluster and show it the records already linked. Analyses of matching oracles assume that the answer depends only on the records compared. For the LLM matchers we test, it depends on cluster size, and the model lowers its bar for joining a cluster as the cluster grows. On French procurement suppliers from FOPPA, with SIREN identifiers as ground truth, we hold the new record fixed and vary only the number of verified members shown. In Claude Sonnet~5 and Haiku~5.5 the rise is \clSonD{} and \clHaiD{} points. It does not arise on Czech and Bulgarian TED notices, where one record with its full address already identifies the company. [2611.22382]
RICoT: Recurrent Interleaved Chain-of-Thought for Page Stream Segmentation
RICoT：用于页面流分割的循环交错思维链
Large language models (LLMs) have renewed interest in Page Stream Segmentation (PSS), splitting a multi-page file into its constituent documents. Yet published LLM methods still frame the task as boundary classification over one or a few neighboring pages, and commercial document-splitting APIs do not disclose their methods. We reimagine PSS for LLMs as a sequential process over the whole stream and propose RICoT (Recurrent Interleaved Chain-of-Thought), a prompting framework that works through the pages as a reader would: within a sliding window it reasons about each page and commits its boundary decision before moving on, and across windows it carries forward a compact record of what it has read, keeping per-call cost bounded. We also introduce FinancePSS-205, a benchmark of 205 real-world, de-identified financial page streams, to be released publicly. On FinancePSS-205, RICoT matches or outperforms pairwise and windowed prompting with the same backbone for each of three open-weight LLMs; on a TABME test subset, RICoT with GLM-5 reaches document F1 0.847 and boundary F1 0.915, above published zero-shot results. A 2B-parameter student, trained on a larger teacher's decisions with and without the page-level reasoning, performs comparably in both modes and comes within 0.05 document F1 of the strongest prompted model on FinancePSS-205.

Comparing Implicit and Explicit Language Attitudes toward the Dutch multiethnolect Straattaal in Humans and Large Language Models
人类与大型语言模型对荷兰多族裔混合语Straattaal的隐性和显性语言态度比较
While language attitudes have been extensively studied in humans, the extent to which large language models (LLMs) encode similar associations between language varieties and social attributes remains unclear. Contemporary sociolinguistic research distinguishes between explicit attitudes, which stem from deliberative processing, and implicit attitudes, which arise from automatic processing. This study investigates whether LLMs exhibit human-like implicit and explicit language attitudes toward the Dutch multiethnolect Straattaal, particularly its association with migrant communities. We use closely matched designs to assess attitudes in humans and LLMs. For humans, we use an Implicit Association Test (IAT) and an explicit rating task. For LLMs, we use the LLM Word Association Test (LLM WAT) and the same explicit rating task. Human participants strongly associate Straattaal with individuals with a migration background at both implicit and explicit levels. In the LLM WAT, Straattaal is consistently associated with individuals with a migration background, with stronger associations observed in larger and newer models. When presented with the same explicit rating task as human participants, LLMs produce comparable response patterns. Overall, the findings suggest that many LLMs encode language attitudes resembling those observed in humans, however we also highlight important questions about how concepts and measures developed to study human attitudes should be interpreted in LLMs. This highlights the relevance of incorporating sociolinguistic perspectives and language variation into NLP research to better identify and mitigate socially and linguistically embedded biases.

[2611.22312]
Zero-Dimensional Attention Topology for Linguistic Acceptability
用于语言可接受性判断的零维注意力拓扑
Topological summaries of Transformer attention provide sentence representations for linguistic acceptability classification. We examine a zero-dimensional persistent-homology ($H_0$) representation that summarizes minimum-spanning-tree merge values from 144 symmetrized BERT attention graphs into 1,440 features. The retrospective evaluation uses 8,517 downstream training sentences and 521 in-domain and 497 out-of-domain CoLA validation sentences after data-integrity filtering. The recorded out-of-domain Matthews correlation coefficients are 0.438 for the topological representation and 0.508 for attention statistics, compared with 0.545 for BERT. Attention-feature results are retained aggregate measurements; BERT predictions are rescored and length controls are refitted from the accompanying records. Analytical results show that the representation retains token-count information and discards a class of directional attention differences. Source resampling of BERT predictions also demonstrates the sensitivity of uncertainty estimates to the sampling unit. The reported results do not demonstrate a predictive advantage for this fixed topological representation over simpler attention summaries. They identify representation properties and evaluation controls relevant to subsequent studies of attention topology.

Sleep2Word: Peripheral-to-EEG Translation via Duration-Aware Wordization
Sleep2Word：基于时长感知词化的外周信号到EEG的转换
Estimating full-night electroencephalography (EEG) spectrograms from peripheral measurements could extend EEG-informed sleep analysis to recordings acquired without EEG. This task requires compact representations of signals with different temporal structures. We introduce Sleep2Word, a framework for translating respiration, electrocardiography (ECG), or photoplethysmography (PPG) into EEG spectrograms through duration-aware physiological words. Building on context-dependent chunking, Sleep2Word groups physiological embeddings into variable-duration chunks and jointly quantizes their content and duration into modality-specific wordbooks. A block-autoregressive translator then predicts target EEG word identities and occurrence-specific duration refinements within fixed-duration blocks. This design accommodates independent source and target wordbooks, limits temporal drift, and shortens the autoregressive decoding path. Trained on over 20K PSG cases across 9 datasets, Sleep2Word consistently outperforms existing spectrogram generation baselines in EEG spectrogram reconstruction, sleep staging, arousal detection, and survival prediction. Compressing full night sequences by up to 69.7%, the learned words yield a favorable accuracy-compute trade-off. Sleep event analysis further shows that the learned words capture physiologically plausible associations across modalities, suggesting their potential for interpretable cross-modal analysis.

TextSphere: Efficient Text-to-Image Generation via Shared Hyperspherical Alignment and Uniformity
TextSphere：通过共享超球面对齐与均匀性实现高效文本到图像生成
Recent advances in text-to-image generation have substantially improved image quality, particularly through diffusion-based methods that require multiple inference steps. In contrast, Sphere Encoder offers an efficient generative framework capable of producing images with fewer forward steps. However, extending it to free-form text is nontrivial, because its class-conditioned design lacks cross-modal alignment. To address this challenge, we propose TextSphere, an efficient text-to-image generative framework that extends Sphere Encoder with language conditioning and cross-modal alignment. We incorporate pretrained CLIP text embeddings through adaptive layer normalization (AdaLN) to guide image generation while retaining the original spherical sampling mechanism. To bridge the cross-modal representation gap, we introduce a Spherical Semantic Alignment and Uniformity (SSAU) objective that jointly promotes semantic alignment and representation uniformity on the hypersphere. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, MS-COCO-2014, and Flickr30k demonstrate that TextSphere consistently reduces generation FID (gFID) over the text-conditioned baseline while achieving reconstruction FID (rFID) below 1.0. With only 4 and 6 sampling steps, TextSphere achieves lower gFID than the compared diffusion- and GAN-based models trained from scratch on CUB-200-2011 and MS-COCO-2014, respectively. We further observe that improved reconstruction fidelity does not necessarily yield better generation quality. In addition, we use Partial Least Squares Singular Value Decomposition (PLS-SVD) to analyze cross-modal alignment and modality gap, offering insight into the effectiveness of our approach.

[2611.22859]
When the tail is earned by its companions: measuring label dependence in long-tailed multi-label classification
当尾部标签由其同伴赢得时：测量长尾多标签分类中的标签依赖性
A rare label seldom occurs alone, and a classifier can recover it from the frequent labels it co-occurs with, its companions, without having learned the label itself. Existing diagnostics of this need a trained model: none says before training whether a corpus admits the failure, or what the standard aggregate hides. Macro-F1, like any measure built from per-label confusion counts, is unchanged by how a label's recall divides between occurrences with and without a companion. We introduce a within-label estimand of that division, a reading rule under which a fall in dependence counts only if companion-free recall rises, and a statistic of training labels that bounds, before training, how far reported recall can exceed companion-free recall. Across 23 corpora, 7 training objectives and five families of label-correlation mechanisms, companion-free recall falls short on all six corpora where the estimand is defined. Up to 18.4% of the tail true positives macro-F1 credits would vanish at the companion-free rate, within the training-label bound on every corpus. No long-tail objective reduces the dependence, but architecture moves it. Models whose tail score is computed from other labels' predictions are strongly dependent.
中文翻译
罕见标签很少单独出现，分类器可以从与其共现的常见标签（即同伴）中恢复该标签，而无需自己学习该标签。现有诊断方法需要训练好的模型：没有一种方法能在训练前判断语料库是否允许这种失败，也无法揭示标准聚合所掩盖的内容。宏F1，如同任何基于每标签混淆计数的度量一样，不受标签召回率在有无同伴情况下的分配影响。我们引入了一种标签内部的估计量，用于衡量该分配比例，并提出一种读取规则：只有当无同伴召回率上升时，依赖度下降才被计入；同时提出一种训练标签的统计量，可在训练前界定报告召回率超过无同伴召回率的上限。在23个语料库、7种训练目标和五类标签相关性机制下，估计量定义的六个语料库中，无同伴召回率均不足。在每个语料库中，最多可达18.4%的尾部真正例宏F1得分将在无同伴率下消失，且始终在训练标签界限内。没有任何长尾目标能减少依赖性，但架构可移动依赖性。那些从其他标签预测计算尾部得分的模型表现出强烈依赖性。

[2611.22409]
SignAligner: Dual-Granularity Span Generation for Sign Language Alignment
SignAligner：双粒度跨度生成用于手语对齐
Large sign-language corpora benefit from temporal labels at two scales: subtitle spans for subtitle alignment and word spans for sign spotting. Sign spottings already locate words in time at corpus scale, but training subtitle alignment jointly on subtitle and word spans remains underexplored. We present SignAligner, a prompt-conditioned visual-language span generator that maps a sign-language video and a subtitle-sentence or target-word query to a textual start/end frame interval. It is first trained jointly on subtitle spans and word spans from sign spottings, then refined with overlap-based rewards while retaining auxiliary word examples. On the subtitle test set of the British Broadcasting Corporation-Oxford British Sign Language (BOBSL) corpus, the refined model reaches 66.68 F1@0.50 (F1 with a temporal intersection-over-union threshold of one half), the best among compared systems without external sign-video resources. This overlap-reward refinement outperforms cross-entropy continuation from the same jointly trained model. Joint training with word spans improves subtitle alignment in a pair of our generators matched in code, labels and maximum update budget. The advantage remains after both models of the pair are refined with overlap rewards. For word queries, the refined model follows the queried word, not a fixed position in the video: when the word's sign lies away from the video centre, it is far more accurate than predictions that use position alone. Together, these findings support one shared span generator refined with overlap rewards, aligning subtitles and producing word spans for human review.

[2611.21925]
DNeo: Your Parallel Draft Model Needs Better Target Feature Encoding
DNeo：你的并行草稿模型需要更好的目标特征编码
Speculative-diffusion decoding uses a small parallel drafter model to generate b draft tokens in one forward pass, which then undergo parallel verification via a larger target model. Faster inference can be achieved when multiple draft tokens pass verification simultaneously. Recent parallel draft architectures (DFlash, DSpark) improve draft quality by conditioning on rich features computed by the target, through a mechanism known as feature encoding. However, standard feature encoding mechanisms have three limitations: (1) they are lossy, applying encoding projections that are destructive to the underlying target feature-space via a discovered effect known as residual mixing, (2) they are memory-less, with no record of previously processed proposals, and (3) they utilize features sampled uniformly from across the target layers, omitting important hidden states. To address these issues, we propose Mixture-of-Features Attention (MoFA), a lightweight feature-encoding approach equipped with a working memory that encodes previously rejected proposals. Leveraging MoFA, rejection memory, and a set of further enhancements, including 2-tap causal convolutions, prefix-only cross-attention, and non-uniform target layer selection, the final architecture, DNeo, achieves 1.3x faster generation speed than SoTA (DFlash-2) without impacting drafter footprint or generation quality. DNeo marks a new state of the art in high quality, fast LLM inference.
中文翻译
推测性扩散解码使用一个小的并行草稿模型在一次前向传播中生成b个草稿标记，然后通过更大的目标模型进行并行验证。当多个草稿标记同时通过验证时，可以实现更快的推理。最近的并行草稿架构（DFlash、DSpark）通过一种称为特征编码的机制，利用目标模型计算的丰富特征来改进草稿质量。然而，标准的特征编码机制存在三个局限性：(1) 它们是信息有损的，因为编码投影会破坏底层目标特征空间，这一现象被称为残差混合；(2) 它们是无记忆的，没有记录先前处理过的提案；(3) 它们从目标层中均匀采样特征，忽略了重要的隐藏状态。为解决这些问题，我们提出了混合特征注意力（MoFA），一种轻量级的特征编码方法，配备工作内存以编码先前被拒绝的提案。结合MoFA、拒绝记忆以及一系列其他增强技术（包括双击因果卷积、仅前缀交叉注意力和非均匀目标层选择），最终架构DNeo在不增加草稿模型规模或影响生成质量的前提下，实现了比当前最优（DFlash-2）快1.3倍的生成速度。DNeo标志着高质量、高速大语言模型推理的新里程碑。
👤 Jameson Sandler, Shane Bergsma, Mostafa Elhoushi, Bin Claire Zhang, Gavia Gray et al. 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21852]
The Hidden Cost of MoE Diffusion Decoding: Profiling and Fixing the Sampler
MoE扩散解码的隐性成本：采样器的剖析与修复
Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) decode by iteratively denoising a full canvas of tokens, so every forward pass runs a per-token sampling and stopping pipeline over the entire canvas-by-vocabulary logits tensor. We profile single-user decoding of a 26B-parameter diffusion Mixture-of-Experts model on a bandwidth-bound edge accelerator (NVIDIA GB10) and report a result that is easy to miss: after weight quantisation, this logits pipeline, not the expert GEMMs, is the single largest consumer of decode time. Unlike autoregressive decoding, where sampling touches one position's logits per committed token, diffusion decoding processes S times as many logit entries per output token, where S is the number of denoising steps. We show this pipeline is largely redundant or fusable and remove it in two lossless tiers: a bit-exact tier whose output is byte-identical to the reference decoder, and an accuracy-parity tier using a fused single-pass entropy kernel and Gumbel-max sampling. Combined with a W4A16 expert kernel, the full stack reaches 2.4-2.7 times end-to-end speedup over bf16 at unchanged benchmark accuracy on GSM8K, HumanEval, and MATH, on a single 273 GB/s device. We confirm the finding on a second, independently built diffusion MoE (LLaDA2.0-mini: pipeline 28.8% of decode, fused W4A16 3.3 times at accuracy parity) and show it disappears on a dense diffusion model (3.6%). The cost is specific to MoE diffusion, where quantisation makes the backbone cheap. Code and quantised weights are released.
中文翻译
掩码扩散语言模型（dLLMs）通过迭代去噪整个标记画布来进行解码，因此每次前向传播都会在完整的画布-词汇表logits张量上运行逐标记采样和停止管道。我们在带宽受限的边缘加速器（NVIDIA GB10）上对一个260亿参数的扩散混合专家模型进行了单用户解码性能分析，并报告了一个容易被忽视的结果：在权重量化后，这个logits管道而非专家GEMM，成为解码时间的最大单一消耗。与自回归解码不同，在自回归解码中每提交一个标记只触达一个位置的logits，扩散解码每输出一个标记需处理S倍于常规数量的logits条目，其中S为去噪步数。我们证明该管道在很大程度上是冗余或可融合的，并通过两种无损层级将其移除：一个比特精确层级，其输出与参考解码器字节完全一致；另一个精度对齐层级，采用融合的一次性熵核和Gumbel-max采样。结合W4A16专家核，整个系统在单个273 GB/s设备上实现了2.4至2.7倍的端到端加速，同时在GSM8K、HumanEval和MATH基准测试中保持不变的准确性。我们在第二个独立构建的扩散MoE（LLaDA2.0-mini：管道占解码时间的28.8%，融合W4A16实现3.3倍加速且精度对齐）上验证了该发现，并显示该现象在密集扩散模型中消失（仅占3.6%）。该成本特异于MoE扩散模型，因为量化使主干变得廉价。代码和量化权重已发布。
👤 Harshit Samrat, Ramakrishna Paramahamsa Kompella 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21702]
Watermark Evidence under Open-Weight LLM Modification: Exact Bounds for Low-Rank Output Watermarks
开放权重大模型修改下的水印证据：低秩输出水印的精确边界
Open-weight language models can continue generating after their released weights are modified, while a watermark verifier still scores outputs against the original base and marked models. We study how such modifications change the evidence available to an OpenStamp-style verifier. At each shared prefix, we represent the modification as a residual in the next-token logits. We derive an exact finite-vocabulary lower bound on the expected log-likelihood-ratio evidence under a residual-span constraint, along with a sharp span-only deviation bound. We also characterize the first-order evidence shift through a covariance term with an explicit second-order remainder, and determine the residual span needed to reach a target evidence level. For sequence-level detection, we derive an exact conditional exponential-moment bound and combine it with a supermartingale argument and a distribution-free order-statistic tolerance bound. This yields a high-confidence upper bound on miss probability after attack selection on calibration data and evaluation on independent certification continuations. In experiments with OpenStamp on Llama-2-7B and Mistral-7B-v0.3, INT8 quantization and 30% pruning retain test true-positive rates of 85.6-94.8%, while 500-step LoRA fine-tuning lowers them to 28.8% and 11.6%. The covariance prediction tracks the measured mean LoRA evidence drift on both models, with no remainder-bound violations across 160,000 test prefixes. Additionally, we observe zero false positives among 500 base-model continuations per model. Through these theoretical and empirical results, the current research provides a better understanding of watermark robustness to model-editing/tuning attacks.
中文翻译
开放权重的语言模型可以在其发布权重被修改后继续生成内容，而水印验证器仍会根据原始基础模型和标记模型对输出进行评分。我们研究此类修改如何改变OpenStamp风格验证器可用的证据。在每个共享前缀处，我们将修改表示为下一个标记logits中的残差。我们推导出在残差跨度约束下的期望对数似然比证据的精确有限词汇下界，以及一个尖锐的仅跨度偏差界。我们还通过一个具有显式二阶余项的协方差项刻画了一阶证据偏移，并确定达到目标证据水平所需的残差跨度。对于序列级检测，我们推导出一个精确的条件指数矩界，并结合超鞅论证和分布无关的顺序统计容忍度界。这给出了在攻击选择校准数据后、在独立认证延续上评估时的高置信度误检概率上界。在Llama-2-7B和Mistral-7B-v0.3上的实验表明，INT8量化和30%剪枝保留了85.6%-94.8%的测试真阳性率，而500步LoRA微调则将其降至28.8%和11.6%。协方差预测在两个模型上均准确跟踪了测量的LoRA证据漂移，且在16万测试前缀中未出现余项界违反。此外，每模型500个基础模型延续中观察到零个假阳性。通过这些理论与实证结果，本研究深化了对水印在模型编辑/微调攻击下鲁棒性的理解。
👤 Prashant Shekhar, Dumindu Samaraweera, Caroline Howard 📅 2026-11-16 🔗 arXiv
[2611.21639]
ClearTalk: Data-Driven Mandarin Articulation Analysis and Personalized Communication Support for Children with Dysarthria
ClearTalk：面向构音障碍儿童的中文发音分析与个性化沟通支持的数据驱动系统
Children with dysarthria may experience reduced intelligibility and difficulty expressing everyday needs. ClearTalk is a smartwatch-based system that converts Mandarin speech-recognition differences into interpretable articulatory profiles for personalized practice and staged communication. Target and recognized utterances are aligned at the syllable level, summarized across seven articulatory positions, and ranked by a count-sensitive asymmetric heuristic. For 13 dysarthric speakers from the Mandarin Dysarthria Speech Corpus, system syllable-level accuracy showed a Pearson correlation of 0.908 with expert intelligibility, with an R-squared value of 0.824 and a p-value below 0.001. The system-selected priority targets achieved an 86.7 percent overlap with the consensus of three blinded speech-language pathologists; across 210 target consonants, automated labels achieved 86.7 percent agreement, a Cohen's kappa of 0.76, and a macro-F1 score of 0.81. In a balanced single-session pilot with 15 children, mean pre/post gains were 14.4, 29.4, and 27.7 percentage points for mild, moderate, and severe groups, while communication-round completion rates were 81.3, 66.7, and 48.0 percent. These findings support the feasibility of using interpretable patterns of speech errors to guide wearable articulation practice and communication assistance.
中文翻译
构音障碍儿童可能面临清晰度下降及表达日常需求困难的问题。ClearTalk是一款基于智能手表的系统，将中文语音识别差异转化为可解释的发音特征图谱，用于个性化练习和分阶段沟通支持。目标语句与识别语句在音节层面进行对齐，总结为七个发音位置，并通过一种计数敏感的非对称启发式方法进行排序。针对来自中文构音障碍语音语料库的13名患者，系统在音节级别的准确率与专家清晰度评估之间呈现皮尔逊相关系数0.908，R平方值为0.824，p值低于0.001。系统选定的优先目标与三位盲法言语语言病理学家的共识重合率达86.7%；在210个目标辅音上，自动标注达成86.7%的一致性，Cohen's kappa为0.76，宏平均F1得分为0.81。在包含15名儿童的平衡单次试点中，轻度、中度和重度组的平均前后提升分别为14.4、29.4和27.7个百分点，而沟通回合完成率分别为81.3%、66.7%和48.0%。这些发现支持了利用可解释的语音错误模式来指导可穿戴发音练习与沟通辅助的可行性。
